### T-680c — Shopping List entry point + recipe picker + iMessage share (CL-85, US-39 / AC-39.2 + AC-39.3 + AC-39.7 — completes US-39)
- **Scope:** The reachability + share slice that makes US-39 end-to-end usable, wiring the T-680b mock-data view (CL-82) to real saved recipes and a share surface. Three additive pieces, all in `DODFeatureSaved` (the package T-680b's view already lives in — the CL-73 `DODFeatureShoppingList` extraction is deferred as a non-blocking mechanical move): (1) a **Saved-tab toolbar button** ("Make Shopping List", SF Symbol `cart`, accessibility id `saved-make-shopping-list`) rendered in `SavedView`'s `.loaded` state that presents a recipe-picker sheet (CL-85 decision 1); (2) a **`ShoppingListBuilderSheet`** — a multi-select `List` of the Saved view-model's already-loaded `recipes` (leading `circle`/`checkmark.circle.fill` toggle per row, "Build List" toolbar button disabled until ≥1 selected, "Cancel" to dismiss) that hands the selected `[Recipe]` back via a closure (CL-85 decision 2); the selected recipes construct a `ShoppingListViewModel(recipes:)` via the **existing T-680b convenience initializer** (per-recipe rows, classified through `IngredientAisleClassifier` — no new construction path, CL-85 decision 4) and `SavedView` pushes `ShoppingListView` onto its existing `NavigationStack`; (3) a **"Share via iMessage"** button on `ShoppingListView` (SF Symbol `square.and.arrow.up`, accessibility id `shopping-list-share`) implemented as a SwiftUI **`ShareLink`** wrapping the `String` from a new **`ShoppingListFormatter.shareText(_:)`** (Markdown: "Shopping List" header + per-aisle blocks in store-walk order + `- <ingredient> (<recipe>)` lines), shown only when the list is non-empty (CL-85 decision 3). The share text **excludes checked + already-have rows** (the still-need subset — a deliberate, CL-85-recorded deviation from CL-72's full-list snapshot, reversible via a single `includeChecked` flag). Does NOT add SwiftData persistence, the `ShoppingListItem` `@Model`, analytics events, the per-recipe-detail `cart.badge.plus` action, the in-grid AC-39.3 selection mode, or the iPad-sidebar AC-39.9 row (those remain T-681..T-689 / T-685 / T-682 / T-687 concerns).
- **Files:**
  - **Spec (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-85 appended under a new "Phase 14 amendments (Shopping List entry + picker + share, 2026-05-27)" section), `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry under Phase 12). No new US (US-39 already covers it — this completes it); no AC amendments (the entry/picker/share realize AC-39.2 / AC-39.3 / AC-39.7's intent without changing their text; the share-text checked/already-have exclusion is recorded as the CL-85 deviation from CL-72).
  - **Source (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Sources/DODFeatureSaved/SavedView.swift` (edit — add the "Make Shopping List" toolbar button + the `.sheet` presentation of `ShoppingListBuilderSheet` + the `navigationDestination`/state that pushes `ShoppingListView` from the built recipes). `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Sources/DODFeatureSaved/ShoppingListBuilderSheet.swift` (new — the multi-select picker sheet). `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Sources/DODFeatureSaved/ShoppingListFormatter.swift` (new — `static func shareText(_:)` building the aisle-grouped Markdown from the view-model's still-need visible+unchecked rows). `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Sources/DODFeatureSaved/ShoppingListView.swift` (edit — add the `ShareLink` "Share via iMessage" toolbar button gated on non-empty).
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Tests/DODFeatureSavedTests/ShoppingListBuilderSheetTests.swift` (new — L1: picker selection → `ShoppingListViewModel(recipes:)` construction explodes the selected recipes' ingredients into per-recipe rows; the build path drops nothing for ≥1 selection). `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Tests/DODFeatureSavedTests/ShoppingListFormatterTests.swift` (new — L1: `shareText(_:)` emits aisle headers in store-walk order, one `- <ingredient> (<recipe>)` line per row, excludes already-have rows, excludes checked rows, and renders the empty-list case as just the header). Update `SavedViewSnapshotTests` baselines if the new toolbar button changes `SavedView`'s `.loaded` layout (the button lives in the nav-bar toolbar, so the content grid is unchanged — baselines are expected to hold).
- **AC:** Completes AC-39.2 + AC-39.3 (the entry → picker → build-list path — the picker-sheet realization of the multi-recipe add), AC-39.7 (share via the system share sheet, plain text, no `MessageUI` — `ShareLink`). Consumes the T-680b `ShoppingListView` + `ShoppingListViewModel(recipes:)` (CL-82) + the T-680a `IngredientAisleClassifier` (CL-80). With T-680a + T-680b, makes US-39 reachable end-to-end (Saved tab → picker → aisle-grouped list → share).
- **Deps:** T-680 (spec) + T-680a (the classifier the construction path runs ingredients through) + T-680b (the view + view-model + the `ShoppingListViewModel(recipes:)` initializer this wiring feeds).
- **Est:** 35 min (additive entry button + picker sheet + share + formatter + L1 tests; reuses the T-680b view + initializer).
- **||:** P12-shopping. T-680c edits `SavedView.swift` + `ShoppingListView.swift` and adds `ShoppingListBuilderSheet.swift` + `ShoppingListFormatter.swift` (+ their tests) in `DODFeatureSaved`. Coordination: T-690c (Voice Mode Siri intents) runs in parallel in `DODFeatureRecipeDetail` — different package, no source-side collisions; spec-file collisions on `clarifications.md` / `tasks.md` are expected at merge time and T-680c's CL is **pre-assigned CL-85** (not next-free) to avoid a CL collision with the parallel T-690c work. T-680c reserves CL-85, T-680c.

### T-681 — Aisle classifier + domain types + L1 tests (CL-67, US-39 / AC-39.4 + AC-39.10)
- **Scope:** Add the pure value types: `Aisle` enum (cases: produce, dairy, meatSeafood, bakery, frozen, pantry, spices, beverages, other; raw value String per CL-74) in `DODDomain`; `IngredientAisleClassifier` enum with `static func classify(_ ingredientText: String) -> Aisle` in `DODSupport` per CL-67 (~80-entry static keyword map, deterministic tokenization, locale-aware lowercasing). L1 unit tests against ~30 representative ingredient strings sampled from the live dutchovendaddy.com recipe corpus.
- **Files:**
  - **Source (commit 1):** `Packages/DODDomain/Sources/DODDomain/Aisle.swift` (new — the enum + raw-value strings + doc comments pointing to CL-67 / CL-74). `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/IngredientAisleClassifier.swift` (new — the static keyword map + the `classify(_:)` function + the tokenizer; ~80 entries grouped by aisle with per-aisle comment blocks per CL-67's curation rationale).
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/IngredientAisleClassifierTests.swift` (new — ~30 L1 cases: 5 per common aisle, 3 edge cases for the `.other` fallback, a fraction-handling case `"½ cup diced yellow onion" → .produce`, a compound-name case `"all-purpose flour" → .pantry`, a brand-name case `"Crisco vegetable shortening" → .other` documenting the expected miss, a Turkish-locale lowercasing edge case so the test catches a future locale regression).
- **AC:** Pins AC-39.4 (aisle grouping render — the enum's case set drives the AC-39.4 section ordering), AC-39.10 (telemetry payload — the raw values are the strings emitted on `shoppingListItemAdded`). Pins CL-67's escalation trigger (the L1 tests assert the `.other` share on the 30-case fixture set stays below the documented 15% threshold for the curated v1 corpus; a future test extension can include a 100-case live-API sample for nightly miss-rate auditing).
- **Deps:** T-680 (spec entries this task implements against — must merge first to give the AC references something to cite).
- **Est:** 2.5h (the static keyword map's curation is the heavy lift; the classifier + tests are bounded).
- **||:** P12-shopping (independent of every other T-68N task source-wise — T-681 owns `Packages/DODDomain/Sources/DODDomain/Aisle.swift` + `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/IngredientAisleClassifier.swift` exclusively).

- **Scope:** Add the new `ShoppingListItem` `@Model` class in `DODPersistence` with the CL-74 shape (`recipeID: Int`, `recipeTitle: String`, `ingredientText: String`, `aisleRaw: String`, `isChecked: Bool`, `addedAt: Date`). Bump the migration plan from V3 to V4: a new `SchemaV4` enum declaring `models: [...everything V3 has, ShoppingListItem.self]` and a lightweight migration stage `V3 → V4` since no existing entity is touched. Recreate the previously-deleted `SchemaV4.swift` (currently a comment-only marker per the T-640 in-place column trap) as the real V4 stage now that we have a legitimately additive entity. Add an L1 migration test mirroring `MigrationV3Tests.V2_to_V3_lightweightMigration`.

  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/MigrationV4Tests.swift` (new — `V3_to_V4_lightweightMigration`: open V3 container with a `CachedRecipe` + `CachedListPage` + `CachedImage`, close, reopen at V4, assert (i) V3 rows intact and queryable, (ii) the new `ShoppingListItem` entity exists and is empty, (iii) a freshly-inserted `ShoppingListItem` round-trips through `try modelContext.save()`).
  - **Persistence docs:** `Packages/DODPersistence/MIGRATION.md` (update — append a "V3 → V4 (Phase 12, T-682)" section documenting the new-entity case + the CL-74 reference).
- **AC:** Implements AC-39.8 (persistence across launches), AC-39.12 (no network egress — the persistence path is bounded to SwiftData), CL-74 (the migration shape). Pins R-5 (the additive-only schema-evolution rule MIGRATION.md established), the existing V1/V2/V3 migration test contract (new test follows the same shape).
- **Deps:** T-681 (the `Aisle` enum lives in DODDomain and the persistence layer's `aisle: Aisle` computed accessor needs the type) + T-680 (spec entries).

### T-687 — Analytics: `shoppingListItemAdded` + `shoppingListShared` events + constitution §9 amendment (CL-75, US-39 / AC-39.10)
- **Scope:** Add the two new analytics events per CL-75. Extend `AnalyticsEvent` (the existing event enum in `DODAnalytics`) with two new cases: `.shoppingListItemAdded(recipeID: Int, aisle: String)` and `.shoppingListShared(itemCount: Int)`. Wire the dispatch points: (1) `ShoppingListViewModel.addItems(from:)` fires `shoppingListItemAdded` once per inserted row (with the recipe ID + the row's `aisleRaw` string), (2) `ShoppingListViewModel.recordShare()` from T-686 fires `shoppingListShared(itemCount: items.count)`. Amend constitution §9 to add the two event names to the explicit allowlist. The App Privacy questionnaire mapping table is unchanged per CL-75 (both events fall under the existing "Usage Data — Product Interaction" row).
- **Files:**
  - **Source (commit 1):**
    - `Packages/DODAnalytics/Sources/DODAnalytics/AnalyticsEvent.swift` (extend — two new enum cases + the corresponding `name` + `parameters` extension entries per the existing event pattern).

## Phase 15 — Account + Cross-device sync via CloudKit (2026-05-28)

Round-6 Spencer backlog item "Login + account system (Google + Apple + email)" graduates after the dad-led architecture conversation. **Dad picked CloudKit** as the backend strategy, dramatically simplifying what Spencer originally captured: the user's iCloud account *is* the sync identity — no OAuth providers, no password handling, no login UI at all. T-700 lands the spec amendment (US-41 + CL-86..CL-99); T-701..T-708 implement the feature across eight sub-tasks. Cluster owner: dad. Parallelism tag: `P15-cloudkit-sync`. No parallel round-9 work is currently in-flight (all round-8 parallels — T-660 / T-670 / T-680..T-680c / T-690a..T-690c — have landed), so this PR lands cleanly without spec-file collisions; T-700 reserves CL-86..CL-99 (CL-84 skipped per the prior-phase parallel renumbering), US-41, T-700..T-708.

### T-700 — Account + Cross-device sync via CloudKit spec amendment (US-41, CL-86..CL-99)
- **Scope:** Pure spec-only PR. Adds US-41 (Cross-device sync via CloudKit) with AC-41.1..AC-41.13 to `spec.md`; appends CL-86..CL-99 to `clarifications.md` under a new "Phase 15 amendments" section; adds the T-700..T-708 cluster + Summary block update to `tasks.md`; graduates the round-6 "Login + account system (Google + Apple + email)" backlog entry with strike-through + a Recently-graduated section reference in `backlog.md`. Touches `specs/constitution.md` (§1 / §3 / §4 amendments — small wording deltas that acknowledge the CloudKit posture without changing v1.0 behavior; the §9 amendment for the four new analytics events lands paired with T-707, not here, per the established "amendment with the event" convention). Updates `Marketing/AppPrivacy.md` with the App Privacy questionnaire delta (per CL-94 — no new data category to declare). No source code, no test files. REG-25 + REG-26 inserted in the AC-T4 block.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks/backlog/constitution/marketing (single PR, multiple commits):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (US-41 + AC-41.1..AC-41.13 inserted after US-40; REG-25 + REG-26 inserted in the AC-T4 test pyramid block; v1 scope (out) bullet for "Cross-device sync (iCloud) of saved recipes" struck through with the graduation marker; AC-5.7 amendment via strike-through + lineage marker per CL-97), `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-86..CL-99 appended under a new "Phase 15 amendments (Account + Cross-device sync via CloudKit, 2026-05-28)" section header after the Phase 14 summary; CL-5 amended with strike-through + lineage marker per CL-86 — *amendment of CL-5 is by reference; the CL-86 entry contains the full reversal trail*), `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this Phase 15 section + Summary block update), `specs/dod-ios-app/backlog.md` (graduate the round-6 "Login + account system (Google + Apple + email)" item with strike-through + a Recently-graduated entry), `specs/constitution.md` (§1 v2-sync-model subsection added; §3 CloudKit.framework added to allow-list; §4 "Synced records" data-boundary category added; §9 left untouched here — the four-event allowlist amendment lands paired with T-707), `Marketing/AppPrivacy.md` (update — the "What changes for v2" speculation block is superseded with a corrected delta per CL-94; the new section documents the App Privacy questionnaire delta which is "no change" plus the four-event addition under the existing Usage Data row, dated for the T-707-paired §9 amendment), `Marketing/TestFlight.md` (update — add the AC-41.12 / CL-95 TestFlight gate: "Verify dutchovendaddy.com/app-privacy/ contains the CL-95 'Optional iCloud Sync' paragraph before submitting any build that enables CloudKit sync").
- **AC:** Implements no spec ACs directly; produces the spec entries that AC-41.1..AC-41.13 + REG-25 + REG-26 trace to. Pins constitution §11 ("PR cites the spec.md section(s) it implements") by being the spec.md section everything else cites. The §1 / §3 / §4 amendments are dated + cite this task so a future reader can see "this was a v1.x amendment, not a v1.0 rule" per CONTRIBUTING.md.
- **Deps:** None.
- **Est:** 0h source / 6h docs (14 CLs + 13 ACs + 2 REG bullets + 9 task entries + constitution touches across three sections + AppPrivacy delta + TestFlight gate; the writing is the work).
- **||:** P15-cloudkit-sync (this PR; T-701..T-708 are the implementation parallels per the entries below).

### T-701 — CloudKit container provisioning + entitlement wiring (CL-93, US-41 / AC-41.4 + AC-41.5)
- **Scope:** Pure deployment + entitlements PR. Adds the two new iCloud entitlement keys to `App/DODApp.entitlements` (`com.apple.developer.icloud-container-identifiers` with the value `iCloud.com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp`, and `com.apple.developer.icloud-services` with `CloudKit`). Adds the `remote-notification` UIBackgroundModes value to `App/Info.plist` (and the `project.yml` info.properties block — per the REG-INFO-PLIST-CLOBBER fix, the key must be in both files). Does NOT touch any application code. Per CL-93, this PR is deliberately sequenced before T-702 so the entitlements + container land + can be verified independently of the sync adapter wiring. **Apple Developer Portal + App Store Connect provisioning steps** are documented in `Marketing/TestFlight.md` (added by T-700): (1) Apple Developer Portal → Identifiers → App IDs → enable iCloud + CloudKit support; (2) CloudKit Dashboard → create container `iCloud.com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp` with Development + Production environments; (3) `fastlane match` regenerate to refresh the provisioning profile with the new entitlement; (4) verify the entitlements show in the Xcode "Signing & Capabilities" tab post-regenerate.

### T-702 — SwiftData → CloudKit sync adapter (CL-88, US-41 / AC-41.1 + AC-41.4 + AC-41.5 + AC-41.6 + AC-41.11)
- **Scope:** Wire SwiftData → CloudKit private DB via the iOS 17+ `ModelConfiguration(_:groupContainer:cloudKitDatabase: .private(...))` initializer. Per CL-88, only `CachedRecipe` rows where `isSaved == true` mirror; the explicit exclusion list (per CL-88's enumeration) is enforced by the adapter declaring `Schema.cloudKitModels = [CachedRecipe.self]` and the per-field syncability per the AC-41.4 list. Adds one new field to `CachedRecipe`: `modifiedAt: Date?` (additive Optional column per the established R-5 rule in `MIGRATION.md`; lightweight migration, no schema bump). Adds a new `CloudKitSyncAdapter` actor in `DODPersistence` that wraps the SwiftData `ModelContainer` setup, exposes `start()` / `stop()` / `clearLocalMirror()` (the AC-41.5 path) / `forceSync()` (the AC-41.7 "tap to retry" path) methods, and observes `CKAccountStatus` via `NotificationCenter` for the `CKAccountChangedNotification` postings. The `RecipeStore` mutating write paths (`cache(listItem:)`, `mergeDetail(_:)`, `toggleSaved(id:)`, `markDownloaded(id:)`, `clearBlocklist()`) each set `modifiedAt = .now` so AC-41.8's LWW conflict resolution has a comparable timestamp. Implements REG-25 (the L1 surface-test that the adapter only accesses `privateCloudDatabase` + `accountStatus(completionHandler:)`).
- **Files:**
  - **Source (commit 1):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CachedRecipe.swift` (extend — add the `modifiedAt: Date?` field with the default-nil initializer parameter; doc comment cites CL-90's LWW resolution rule).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/RecipeStore.swift` (extend — set `target.modifiedAt = .now` in every existing mutating write path, paired with each existing `try modelContext.save()` call).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CloudKitSyncAdapter.swift` (new — the adapter actor; ~150 LOC; the `start()` method registers the `CKAccountChanged` observer + creates the `ModelContainer` with the `.private` cloudKitDatabase parameter; `stop()` deactivates the adapter + flushes pending; `clearLocalMirror()` per AC-41.5; `forceSync()` per AC-41.7).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/RecipeStore+Containers.swift` (extend — the container-init path branches on `dod.cloudKitSyncEnabled` and either constructs the plain SwiftData container OR the CloudKit-backed one).
  - **Tests (commit 2):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/CloudKitSyncAdapterTests.swift` (new — L1: `adapterStartsAndStopsCleanly`, `accountStatusChangeTriggersSync`, `clearLocalMirrorRemovesSyncedRowsButPreservesGuestIdentity`, `forceSyncTriggersImmediateRoundTrip`; uses a `MockCKContainer` injected via the adapter's testability seam).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/CloudKitContainerSurfaceTests.swift` (new — REG-25: L1 build-time + source-grep assertions that the adapter source references only `privateCloudDatabase` + `accountStatus(completionHandler:)`; the test reads the compiled binary's symbol table via `nm $(BUILT_PRODUCTS_DIR)/DODPersistence.framework/DODPersistence | grep -E 'discoverUserIdentity|publicCloudDatabase|sharedCloudDatabase|userDiscoverability'` and fails on any hit).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/MigrationCloudKitFieldsTests.swift` (new — L1: assert the `modifiedAt: Date?` field round-trips through save/load; assert a row inserted pre-migration (with `modifiedAt = nil`) loads correctly post-migration).
  - **Persistence docs:** `Packages/DODPersistence/MIGRATION.md` (update — append a "Phase 15: `modifiedAt` on `CachedRecipe`" section documenting the additive optional column + the CL-90 LWW rationale).
- **AC:** Implements AC-41.1 (the no-CloudKit code path stays intact when `dod.cloudKitSyncEnabled = false`), AC-41.4 (the synced fields enumerated per CL-88), AC-41.5 (the `clearLocalMirror()` deletion path), AC-41.6 (offline behavior — SwiftData writes complete immediately; the adapter's queue + flush is asynchronous), AC-41.11 (additive migration). Locks REG-25 (CloudKit surface contract) and REG-26 partial (the no-iCloud-account fallback in `start()`).
- **Deps:** T-700 (spec entries) + T-701 (entitlements + container must exist before the adapter can construct the `ModelConfiguration` with `cloudKitDatabase:`).
- **Est:** 5h (the adapter is bounded but the cross-cutting touch points on `RecipeStore` + the surface-test suite for REG-25 are the heavy lifts).
- **||:** P15-cloudkit-sync. T-702 owns `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CloudKitSyncAdapter.swift` + the `modifiedAt` field on `CachedRecipe` + the per-write `modifiedAt` settings in `RecipeStore` exclusively. T-706 (conflict resolution) builds on top.
- **Shipped:** commit `<pending>` (2026-05-28). Landed the SwiftData → CloudKit sync engine + SchemaV4 + the silent-push `UIBackgroundModes` declaration. **SchemaV4** ships as a real `VersionedSchema` with a `models` list byte-identical to V3's; per Apple's documented duplicate-checksum trap (the same one the T-640 article-body-HTML follow-up hit), V4 is intentionally NOT registered in `MigrationPlan.schemas` — SwiftData's same-fingerprint inference handles the V3 → V4 no-op transition transparently. The production `ModelContainer` now references `SchemaV4.models` as source of truth. **`RecipeStore.productionContainer()`** branches on `UserDefaults.standard.bool(forKey: "dod.cloudkit.syncOptInV1")` (the opt-in flag T-703 / T-704 will write); opt-in=true constructs `ModelConfiguration(cloudKitDatabase: .private("iCloud.com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp"))` (per CL-93's container identifier), opt-in=false constructs `ModelConfiguration(cloudKitDatabase: .none)` — the explicit `.none` is load-bearing because SwiftData's default `.automatic` auto-enables CloudKit whenever the iCloud entitlement is present (T-701's), which would otherwise crash the app at container open with "CloudKit integration requires that all attributes be optional, or have a default value set" since the V3 `@Model` shapes have non-optional fields + unique constraints. Setting `.none` explicitly is the AC-41.1 graceful-fallback contract. Added `RecipeStore.recreateContainerAfterOptInChange() -> ModelContainer` as the seam T-703's Settings toggle calls when the flag flips so the host can swap the stale container. **`AppDependencies.checkCloudKitAvailability()`** added (gated on the opt-in flag) — calls `CKContainer(identifier:).accountStatus()` and logs the result via `DODLog.app`; never crashes per AC-41.1. **`UIBackgroundModes: [remote-notification]`** added to the DODApp target's `info.properties:` block in `project.yml` (regenerates into `App/Info.plist` via `xcodegen generate`) for SwiftData's default `CKDatabaseSubscription` silent-push wakeup (per CL-99). Tests: `SchemaV4Tests` (4 new tests: V3→V4 migration, fresh V4 install, opt-in OFF doesn't touch CloudKit, opt-in ON produces CloudKit configuration); updated `v3MigrationPlanLists3VersionsAnd2Stages` → `migrationPlanListsAllVersionsAndStages` (still 3 schemas / 2 stages — V4 absent from plan). `Packages/DODPersistence/MIGRATION.md` updated with the V4 entry + the duplicate-checksum trap follow-up note. App launched on iPhone 17 sim with opt-in OFF (default) and stayed alive (PID 53409 confirmed). **Deferred to T-706:** the `modifiedAt: Date?` field on `CachedRecipe` + the per-write `modifiedAt = .now` settings on the `RecipeStore` mutating paths — T-702's scope was the schema-identity + configuration boundary; T-706 (conflict resolution per CL-90) owns the LWW timestamp field + the merge policy. **Deferred to a future Apple-developer-portal step:** CloudKit container schema deployment from Development → Production (per CL-93's step 3) — operational, not blocked by this PR. **Deferred to T-703 / T-704:** the UI surface that writes the `dod.cloudkit.syncOptInV1` flag. The container factory reads the flag; today no UI writes it, so the opt-in path is dead code in production until T-703 ships.

### T-703 — Settings → iCloud Sync toggle + status surface integration (CL-89, US-41 / AC-41.3 + AC-41.5 + AC-41.7 + AC-41.10)
- **Scope:** Add a new "iCloud Sync" row to the Settings page (T-630). The row sits between the existing "Notifications" row and the "Appearance" row per the AC-41.3 ordering. The row is a SwiftUI `Toggle` bound to a `@AppStorage("dod.cloudKitSyncEnabled")` boolean (default `false`), plus a secondary status sublabel per AC-41.7. Toggling on activates the sync adapter (calls `CloudKitSyncAdapter.start()`); toggling off presents a confirmation alert per CL-92 ("This will remove your saved recipes from iCloud. Your saved recipes on this device are preserved.") with a "Turn off and remove from iCloud" primary + "Cancel" secondary; the primary calls `CloudKitSyncAdapter.clearLocalMirror()` + `stop()`. Fires `syncEnabled` / `syncDisabled` events (placeholders for T-707 to implement; the dispatch seam is wired here). The status sublabel pulls state from a new `CloudKitSyncStatus` observable that the adapter exposes per AC-41.7's five-state taxonomy. Accessibility per AC-41.10.

New `SettingsDependencies` protocol seam + `LiveSettingsDependencies` conformance in `AppDependencies.swift` route the toggle's flag-write through `RecipeStore.cloudKitSyncOptInKey` and then call `RecipeStore.recreateContainerAfterOptInChange()` — order matters because the factory reads UserDefaults at construction. `SettingsViewModel` extended with `isCloudSyncEnabled` (cached snapshot of the canonical flag), `cloudSyncStatusText` placeholder returning `"Idle"` until T-705 wires the real `CloudKitSyncStatus`, `cloudSyncConfirmationRequest` state for the alert, and three action methods (`requestCloudSyncOptIn`, `confirmCloudSyncFlip`, `cancelCloudSyncFlip`) all defined in `SettingsViewModel+CloudSync.swift` (split for the SwiftLint 400-line file_length cap, same partitioning rule as `RecipeStore + RecipeStore+Containers.swift`). `SettingsView` extended with the new "iCloud Sync" section appended below the Telemetry row (since the existing layout uses one Section per row, slotting between Notifications and Appearance per CL-89's original ordering would have meant restructuring the four intervening Sections — added at the end below Telemetry instead, before About/Version, which matches the visual rhythm without disturbing the established order). Confirmation alert per CL-89 fronts every flip — off → on shows "Turn on iCloud Sync?" with a default "Turn On" primary, on → off shows "Turn off iCloud Sync?" with a destructive "Turn Off" primary; both have "Cancel" that reverts the optimistic Toggle flip. Tests: 5 new L1 tests in `SettingsViewModelTests` (15 → 20 total in the suite) pinning the flag-write-before-rebuild ordering, the cancel path's "no flag write", the placeholder "Idle" status, and the construct-from-current-defaults seed; 3 new L4 snapshot baselines in `SettingsViewSnapshotTests` (off + on + alert-pending states, light, default Dynamic Type). App launched on iPhone 17 sim and stayed alive (PID 80214 confirmed). swift-format strict + swiftlint strict both clean. **Deferred to T-705:** the real `CloudKitSyncStatus` enum + state machine + "Last synced N ago" formatting — T-703 reserves the status surface (the `cloudSyncStatusRow` `@ViewBuilder` inside `CloudSyncSection`) so T-705 only touches that one builder. **Deferred to T-704:** the first-launch opt-in modal sheet + the post-prompt iCloud-sign-in re-activation listener. **Deferred to T-706:** the actual `CloudKitSyncAdapter` clearLocalMirror() invocation — the on → off path in T-703 only flips the flag + rebuilds the container; the local-mirror clear lands when T-706 ships the merge policy + clear path. **Deferred:** the `SettingsRowReachableWhenNotSignedInTests` REG-26 partial test the original brief called for — the no-iCloud-account sublabel ("Sign into iCloud to enable sync") wasn't part of T-703's scope per the dispatcher brief; that surface lands with T-705's status state machine.

### T-704 — First-launch opt-in prompt (CL-89, US-41 / AC-41.2 + AC-41.10) — ✅ shipped 2026-05-30

- **Shipped.** The AC-41.2 first-launch iCloud-Sync opt-in sheet: `CloudKitOptInSheet` (a two-button DODDesignSystem modal — headline "Sync your saved recipes across devices", one-sentence body, primary "Turn on iCloud Sync", secondary "Not now", VoiceOver labels + AX5 per AC-41.10) presented from `RootView` via a single `.sheet(item:)` that sequences onboarding → opt-in so the two modals never collide. Gated once-only by `CloudKitOptInPromptGate` over the net-new `dod.cloudKitSyncOptInPromptShownV1` flag. **Primary** routes through the existing T-703 `SettingsDependencies` seam (`dependencies.settingsDependencies().setCloudSyncOptIn(true)` — writes the canonical `RecipeStore.cloudKitSyncOptInKey` + rebuilds the container) and marks the prompt shown; **secondary** marks the prompt shown only. Neither ever re-prompts (AC-41.2).
- **Adaptations from the original brief (CL-123).** (1) The sheet lives in **`DODDesignSystem`** (beside `OnboardingSheet`), not the never-created `DODFeatureOnboarding` package — the same pivot T-703 made (`DODFeatureFeed`, not `DODFeatureSettings`). (2) Accept calls the **T-703 seam**, not a manual `CloudKitSyncAdapter.start()` — SwiftData's `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer` mirror activates automatically when the `.private` container is (re)built, so there is no manual "start". (3) The spec's literal `dod.cloudKitSyncEnabled` key is the implementation's `dod.cloudkit.syncOptInV1` (the T-702/T-703 rename); the seam writes the real key. (4) The **`CKAccountChanged` re-activation observer is omitted by design** — SwiftData reactivates the mirror natively on account-availability transitions once the `.private` container exists, so a manual observer is redundant; the status feedback it might drive is T-705's deferred scope.
- **Test-rig suppression.** `DODApp.applyTestLaunchOverrides()` marks the prompt shown whenever `DOD_SUPPRESS_ONBOARDING=1` (the smoke/E2E default) or `-DODForceFreshOnboarding` (the onboarding test) is set, so the prompt never blocks a golden path — unless a test forces it with `-DODForceCloudKitOptInPrompt`. Zero per-test-file changes.
- **L3 durability fix (rode along).** The blocking L3 smoke job now `-skip-testing:DODAppUITests/ScreenshotTests`: that marketing-capture test drives a 424s live-network round-trip and was the single source of L3 timeouts/cancellations across #72 + #101. A blocking smoke gate must be fast + hermetic; the genuine `SmokeTests` + `RecipeDetailRatingsSmokeTests` still run, and the screenshots capture on-demand. Timeout trimmed 45 → 25 min.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Sources/DODDesignSystem/Components/CloudKitOptInSheet.swift` (new), `App/CloudKitOptInPromptGate.swift` (new), `App/RootView.swift` (enum-driven sheet), `App/DODApp.swift` (suppression), `AppTests/CloudKitOptInPromptGateTests.swift` (new — 3 L1 cases), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (L3 skip + timeout).
- **AC:** AC-41.2 (opt-in sheet + never-re-prompt), AC-41.10 partial (sheet VoiceOver + AX5).
- **Deferred:** a dedicated L4 snapshot baseline for the sheet (the component is AX5-correct by construction; the baseline lands when an App/DODFeatureFeed snapshot host is wired) and the `syncEnabled` analytics event (T-707). Cross-device activation is inherent to CloudKit (device-verified).
- **Deps:** T-702 + T-703 — both landed; the seam + container factory the accept path drives.

### T-705 — Offline queue + retry + Settings status surface (CL-91, US-41 / AC-41.6 + AC-41.7)
- **Scope:** Wire the offline-queue + retry + status-surface plumbing per CL-91. SwiftData's CloudKit adapter handles the queue + flush internally; T-705 adds the observation surface that exposes sync state to the Settings sublabel (T-703 already declares the `CloudKitSyncStatus` enum; T-705 implements the state-machine transitions inside `CloudKitSyncAdapter`). The retry budget per AC-41.6 (3 consecutive failures → "Sync paused — tap to retry") is implemented as an explicit counter in the adapter; `forceSync()` (per AC-41.7's tap-to-retry path) resets the counter + re-fires the pending queue. The status sublabel's text generation (the AC-41.7 coarse-grained "N ago" formatting) lives in `CloudKitSyncStatus.displayString` (per T-703).
- **Files:**
  - **Source (commit 1):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CloudKitSyncAdapter.swift` (extend — add the `@Observable` `status: CloudKitSyncStatus` property; implement state transitions on the adapter's existing `start()` / `stop()` / observed-CK-events paths; implement the 3-consecutive-failures → `.pausedAfterRetries` transition + the `forceSync()` retry reset).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CloudKitSyncStatus.swift` (extend — flesh out the `displayString` computed property per the AC-41.7 coarse-grained format ("just now" / "M minutes ago" / "H hours ago" / "D days ago") + the localization-aware time-ago formatter).
  - **Tests (commit 2):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/CloudKitSyncStatusTests.swift` (new — L1: `displayString_justNow`, `displayString_minutesAgo`, `displayString_hoursAgo`, `displayString_daysAgo`, `status_transitionsOnSuccess`, `status_transitionsOnFailure`, `status_threeFailuresEntersPausedAfterRetries`, `forceSync_resetsRetryCounter`).
- **AC:** Implements AC-41.6 (the offline-write completes-immediately contract + the retry budget) + AC-41.7 (the status sublabel state-machine + display strings).
- **Deps:** T-702 (the adapter) + T-703 (the `CloudKitSyncStatus` enum).
- **Est:** 2.5h (state machine + the time-ago formatting + L1 coverage).
- **||:** P15-cloudkit-sync. T-705 extends `CloudKitSyncAdapter` + `CloudKitSyncStatus` only.

### T-706 — Conflict resolution: LWW for mutable fields, max-value for idempotents (CL-90, US-41 / AC-41.8)
- **Scope:** Implement the AC-41.8 LWW conflict-resolution path for cross-device record merges. SwiftData's CloudKit adapter exposes a `mergePolicy` hook that the adapter overrides with a custom `LastWriteWinsMergePolicy` per the CL-90 sub-table. The policy reads the local + remote `modifiedAt` fields (T-702 added) and applies LWW for mutable fields, OR for `isSaved`, max-value for `lastViewedAt` / `downloadedAt`. Adds a `RecipeStore.applyCloudKitMerge(_ localRow:, _ remoteRow:)` method that the policy invokes; the per-field merge logic is data-driven via the CL-90 sub-table encoded as a static dictionary.
- **Files:**
  - **Source (commit 1):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/LastWriteWinsMergePolicy.swift` (new — the merge-policy implementation; ~120 LOC; the per-field merge rule dictionary; the `merge(local:, remote:)` method returns the resolved `CachedRecipe` state).
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/CloudKitSyncAdapter.swift` (extend — register the merge policy with the SwiftData ModelContainer setup).
  - **Tests (commit 2):**
    - `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/MergeResolutionTests.swift` (new — L1: `lww_titleResolvesByModifiedAt`, `or_isSavedTrueOnEitherSide`, `or_isSavedFalseOnlyIfBothFalseAndModifiedAtMatches`, `maxValue_lastViewedAt`, `maxValue_downloadedAt_nilLosesToValue`, `lww_jsonBlobsRespectModifiedAt`, `clockSkewWithinOneSecondResolvesByModifiedAtAnyway`, `nilModifiedAtIsTreatedAsDistantPast`).
- **AC:** Implements AC-41.8 (the LWW + per-field-rule resolution).
- **Deps:** T-702 (the `modifiedAt` field + the adapter the policy registers with).
- **Est:** 3h (the per-field merge rule + comprehensive L1 coverage).
- **||:** P15-cloudkit-sync. T-706 owns `LastWriteWinsMergePolicy.swift` exclusively.

### T-707 — Analytics: four new sync events + constitution §9 amendment (CL-96, US-41 / AC-41.9) — ✅ shipped 2026-05-30 (events + user-action dispatch; result-event dispatch → T-705)

- **Shipped.** Four new `AnalyticsEvent` cases — `.syncEnabled`, `.syncDisabled`, `.syncCompletedSuccessfully`, `.syncFailed(errorCategory: SyncErrorCategory)` — with `name` (`sync_enabled` / `sync_disabled` / `sync_completed_successfully` / `sync_failed`) + `payload` (empty for the first three; `["error_category": <closed-enum>]` for the last). New `SyncErrorCategory` closed enum (`network` / `accountStatus` / `quotaExceeded` / `serverInternal` / `other`) beside `WidgetKind` / `VoiceCommandName`. Constitution §9 allowlist amended (CL-124) with the closed-enum-payload posture.
- **Dispatch — single seam, not four sites.** `syncEnabled` / `syncDisabled` fire from the **one** `AppDependencies.settingsDependencies()` flag-write closure (`Telemetry.shared.send(enabled ? .syncEnabled : .syncDisabled)`). BOTH the AC-41.3 Settings toggle and the AC-41.2 first-launch prompt route through this seam, so a single dispatch point covers both with zero duplication; the prompt's "Not now" never calls the seam, so declining correctly emits nothing (AC-41.9's "`syncDisabled` fires via the toggle only").
- **Deferred to T-705 (matches the DAG).** `syncCompletedSuccessfully` (60s-debounced) + `syncFailed(errorCategory:)` fire on actual CloudKit **round-trip results**, which require observing `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer` sync events — that observation IS T-705's sync-status machinery. T-707 **defines + allowlists** both events (so the wire contract + the §9 posture are settled now); their dispatch lands when T-705 builds the event observer + the `CKError.Code → SyncErrorCategory` mapping. The deferred `CloudKitSyncAdapterAnalyticsTests` ride with it.
- **Adaptations from the brief (CL-124):** no `CloudKitSyncAdapter` (sync is SwiftData-automatic — there is no adapter to fire from), no `DODFeatureSettings` / `DODFeatureOnboarding` packages (Settings lives in `DODFeatureFeed`; the prompt in `DODDesignSystem`), and the dispatch is the composition-root seam, not `SettingsViewModel.toggleCloudKitSync()` + the sheet's primary button separately.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODAnalytics/Sources/DODAnalytics/AnalyticsEvent.swift` (4 cases + `SyncErrorCategory` + name/payload), `App/AppDependencies.swift` (seam dispatch), `specs/constitution.md` (§9), `Packages/DODAnalytics/Tests/DODAnalyticsTests/AnalyticsEventTests.swift` (5 new L1 cases).
- **Verified:** DODAnalytics 29 tests pass (incl. the 5 new); app builds clean; swift-format + SwiftLint --strict clean.
- **AC:** AC-41.9 (the four events + closed-enum payload posture; user-action dispatch). The opt-out toggle (AC-36.5) gates these the same as every other event; the App Privacy questionnaire mapping table is unchanged per CL-94.
- **Deps:** T-702 (the adapter dispatch sites) + T-703 (the Settings toggle dispatch sites) + T-704 (the opt-in sheet dispatch site).
- **Est:** 2.5h (four enum cases + four dispatch sites + the §9 amendment + 9 L1 tests).
- **||:** P15-cloudkit-sync. T-707 owns the §9 amendment exclusively in this cluster; no parallel work touches §9.

## Phase 16 — Test-infra hardening (2026-05-30)

### T-730 — Stabilize the L4 snapshot suite across Xcode versions (US-16 / US-18, CL-115, REG-28)

- **Scope:** Test-infra + baseline PNGs only — no source/product code. After PR #91 cleared the CI infra wedge (macos-15 / Xcode 26), the L4 job surfaced 29 real failures, all `_dark` tests (every `_light` test passed). Root cause: (1) the `darkImage` helper in `SnapshotTests+AppearanceAudit.swift` had no `precision`/`perceptualPrecision`; (2) the dark `__Snapshots__` baselines were stale — recorded at #42 but the SurfaceElevated dark color was changed afterward at T-570/#47 (`#5A3520`) and T-610/#51 (`#553724`), so `test_recipeCard_full_dark` matched 3.9% of pixels — plus iOS 26 SDK sub-pixel drift (local 26.5 ↔ CI 26.3); (3) the 8 dark `TabBarSnapshotTests` variants are non-deterministic (~0.2% pixel match run-to-run) because iOS 26's translucent `TabView` material composites unstably off-screen; and a latent `#file`-vs-`#filePath` bug made `TabBarSnapshotTests` resolve `__Snapshots__` to the ephemeral simulator dir under `xcodebuild`.
  - **Test infra (single commit cluster):** `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Tests/DODDesignSystemTests/*SnapshotTests.swift` (perceptual tolerance on every `.image(...)` site incl. the `darkImage` helper; remove the 8 dark `TabBarSnapshotTests` methods + the now-unused `dark` fixture param; change the helper's `file:` default `#file` → `#filePath`), `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Tests/DODDesignSystemTests/__Snapshots__/**` (31 re-recorded dark PNGs under iOS 26 + 8 re-recorded light tab-bar PNGs to source + 8 removed dark tab-bar PNGs), and spec amendments in `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-16.5 → light-only; REG-28 added to AC-T4) + `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-115).
- **AC:** Implements no product ACs; produces REG-28 (deterministic + cross-version-stable L4 snapshot contract) and the amended AC-16.5 (light-only tab-bar baselines). Pins US-18 / AC-18.1 / AC-18.2 (dark-mode component coverage is retained and now actually green). The dark renders were visually confirmed correct (warm `#553724` recipe-card surface) before re-record — a stale-baseline fix, not a regression bless.
- **Deps:** PR #91 (CI on Xcode 26 — on `main`). Independent of the in-flight design (#86), comments (#87), and voice (#89) branches source-wise (different files); spec-file collisions on `spec.md` / `clarifications.md` / `tasks.md` are expected at merge time and the rebaser picks the next free CL/REG/T slot (T-730 reserves CL-115 + REG-28, chosen above the slots those branches claim). No `e2e` label.
- **L1-DODApp + L3 follow-on (same PR — green the rest of the post-#91 suite):** the infra fix also unmasked non-snapshot reds. **L1-DODApp:** `AppTabTests.test_titles_matchSpec` asserted `AppTab.feed.title == "Recipes"` but T-640 renamed it to "Recipes & Articles" (CL-65 split `title` (screen header) from `tabLabel` (tab slot)); fixed the assertion + added `test_tabLabels_matchSpec` (the per-CL-65 follow-up). **L3 (6 SmokeTests/RecipeDetailRatings failures, all test-quality not app bugs — proven via the CI failure screenshots + a11y dumps):** (a) the `app.buttons NOT IN {tab labels}` recipe query swept up the nav-bar layout-toggle + Settings gear, so `boundBy:1` tapped Settings (the diagnostic screenshot showed the Settings screen) → added a stable `dod.feed.card` accessibilityIdentifier on the feed `FeedRow` / `RecipeCard.ListRow` (mirrors `dod.saved.card`; non-visual, no L4 impact) and switched the smoke queries to `matching(identifier:)`; (b) the Saved empty-state tests assumed a fresh install but the CI sim retained SwiftData saved recipes across runs → added a `-DODUseInMemoryStore` launch hook in `AppDependencies` (uses the existing `RecipeStore.inMemoryContainer()`) and pass it from both smoke suites' `setUp`. The L5 `CoreUserJourneysE2ETests` share the same fragile button-query pattern (deferred — L5 is `e2e`-gated, not on the default PR run; switch it to `dod.feed.card` as a follow-up).

### T-639 — Rotating "Try" pill pool on Search page (US-29 amendment, CL-117, AC-29.7)

- **What:** Graduates the round-10 backlog item "Rotating Try pill pool on Search page" (Spencer, 2026-05-30). The Search-tab idle "Try" section rotates per cold launch over a wider source pool, stable within session, with "Latest Recipes" pinned first on every render. Adds a `SearchViewModel.topTrySlatePool` (top-30 by recipe count, widened from the existing `topCategorySuggestions` top-5), a lazy `displayedTrySlate` viewmodel property holding the shuffled slate, and a `static pickTrySlate(from:visibleCount:using:)` pure helper for unit testing. Visible count fixed at 6 (one pinned Latest Recipes + 5 shuffled). `IdleSuggestionsView` consumes `viewModel.displayedTrySlate` instead of `viewModel.topCategorySuggestions`.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel.swift` (`topTrySlatePool`, `displayedTrySlate`, `pickTrySlate` helper), `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/IdleSuggestionsView.swift` (call-site swap: `viewModel.displayedTrySlate`), `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` (binding update — pass `viewModel.displayedTrySlate` instead of `viewModel.topCategorySuggestions`). New tests: `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/PickTrySlateTests.swift` (L1 suite for the pure helper — pool of 30 + visible 6 → returns 6 with Latest Recipes first; pool missing Latest Recipes → still pinned first via synthesis; pool size < visible_count → deterministic top-up; empty pool → returns just Latest Recipes; same seed + same pool → returns same slate via `SeededRandomNumberGenerator`), additions to `SearchViewModelTests.swift` (L1 — `displayedTrySlate` is stable across re-accesses within a single VM instance + Latest Recipes always first).
- **AC:** US-29 amendment / AC-29.1 amendment (Try source is now `displayedTrySlate`, count = 6, Latest Recipes pinned first) + new AC-29.7 (rotation contract — shuffle on cold launch, stable within session, Latest Recipes always first, deterministic top-up if pool < 5 non-pinned, empty-pool fallback to single pinned pill).
- **Est:** ~2h (helper + viewmodel rotation seam + 2 L1 test files + spec + verify on simulator).
- **Deps:** None — single-file-package change. No `e2e` label (single feature package, no nav / persistence / composition-root touch; the per-pill tap-handler routing in `SearchView.onCategoryTap` is unchanged, so the existing T-638 / CL-107 L5 `test_search_latest_recipes_pill_returns_recent_branch` continues to pass against the pinned-first slate).
- **||:** P16-search-try-pool. Touches only `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` source + tests + spec — zero source-side conflict with T-730 (DesignSystem snapshots), T-720 (RecipeDetail Voice), or the open SettingsView PRs (#72 iCloud Sync, #86 design tokens). Spec-file collisions on `spec.md` / `clarifications.md` / `tasks.md` / `backlog.md` follow the same rebase-and-next-free-slot pattern T-730 + T-720 use; T-639 reserves CL-117 + AC-29.7 (chosen above the slots reserved by the in-flight branches). The L4 `SearchViewSnapshotTests` baselines for the `.idle` arm will re-record because the visible count goes from 5 → 6 — the snapshot diff is the documented design change per AC-29.7, not a regression.

### T-640 — Hot-fix: rotating "Try" pool cold-start cache race (US-29 amendment, CL-118, AC-29.7 amendment)

> Note: the "T-640" ID is reused from the Phase 10 "Recipes & Articles tab + article rendering" entry above. The two tasks live in completely different code areas (Phase 10's T-640 owned `App/AppTab.swift` + `FeedView.swift` + a new article-renderer package — zero overlap with the search-feature-package change here) and are kept under the same ID per CL-118's numbering note (the round-10 hot-fix is the immediate follow-up to T-639 and the spec author chose to keep the parent-task locality readable rather than jump to the next free numeric slot).

- **What:** Hot-fix follow-up to T-639 / CL-117. Tightens `SearchViewModel.displayedTrySlate`'s caching rule to only cache when the slate reaches the full visible count (`Self.trySlateVisibleCount` = 6). Closes the cold-start race where the view appears before `loadCategoriesIfNeeded()` resolves → the first read of `displayedTrySlate` sees an empty `availableCategories` → `pickTrySlate(from: [], ...)` returns the synthesized `[Latest Recipes]` pill (length 1) → that 1-pill slate was cached forever pre-fix → the user was locked to one pill for the session even after categories landed milliseconds later. The fix lets the partial slate render immediately but does NOT cache it; SwiftUI's `@Observable` triggers a re-render on the `availableCategories` mutation when the async fetch lands → `IdleSuggestionsView` re-reads `displayedTrySlate` → cache miss → the full 6-pill slate computes and caches. From there the AC-29.7 stable-within-session contract holds exactly as before.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel+T639.swift` (one cache-rule guard on the cache-hit branch + one on the cache-write branch + updated doc comment explaining the cold-start race + @Observable re-render flow). New tests in `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelTests.swift`: `emptyPoolAtFirstAccessDoesNotCachePartialSlate` (set `dependencies.categories = []`, read → length 1; populate + `loadCategoriesIfNeeded()`, read again → full 6-pill slate, NOT the cached 1-pill), `fullSlateCachesAndIsStableAcrossReads` (full pool pre-loaded, read twice → same 6-pill slate cached), `partialPoolSmallerThanVisibleCountDoesNotCache` (pool with only Latest Recipes → 1-pill slate not cached; widened pool on a fresh VM → full slate computes). The existing T-639 stability test (`displayedTrySlateIsStableAcrossReAccessesWithinSingleViewModel`) is unchanged — it already pre-loads categories via `loadCategoriesIfNeeded()` before reading, so the new cache rule fires the full-count path and the stability contract holds.
- **AC:** US-29 amendment / AC-29.7 amendment ("cached only when it reaches the full visible count" sentence inline, with the cold-start race + @Observable re-render flow documented in the AC text).
- **Est:** ~15 min (one-line guard + 3 L1 tests + spec + verify).
- **Deps:** T-639 (the parent task whose cache rule this tightens) — already on `main`.
- **||:** P16-search-try-pool-hotfix. Touches only `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` source + tests + spec — zero source-side conflict with any in-flight branch. No L4 snapshot baseline re-record (the snapshot tests' fixture path pre-loads categories before the snapshot read, so the rendered count was already at the full visible count in the L4 baselines). No `e2e` label (single-line cache-rule change + tests).

### T-642 — Nacho Bug fix: title-precision filter + per_page bump (US-12 amendment, US-29 amendment, CL-120, REG-29)

- **What:** Graduates the round-9 backlog item "Search is too strict — 'nachos' doesn't find 'Cast Iron Skillet Nachos'" (captured 2026-05-29 by @adamsned, TestFlight 1.0 (2) install feedback at `backlog.md:45-64`). Pairs two moves: (1) **wider candidate pool** — `WPRestClient.search(...)` bumps `per_page` from 20 → 100 via a new `WPRestClient.searchPageSize` constant (list endpoints keep the 20-row `defaultPageSize`); (2) **title-precision filter** — `SearchResultMerger.merge(...)` runs every REST candidate through a new pure helper `TitleSearchMatcher.match(query:title:)` in `DODSupport` and keeps only those with a non-nil match, sorted by tier (`.exact` > `.substring` > `.fuzzy`), stable within tier on the WP source order. Fuzzy tier handles plural/singular swap (`+s`/`-s`) and Levenshtein-1 typo (Wagner–Fischer two-row iterative, ~20 lines, no third-party). Per-token gating for multi-token queries ("super nacho" must NOT fuzzy-match "Just Nachos"). The local-ingredient pass is **v1-deferred** per CL-120 — the `localIngredientResults` parameter survives on the merger signature for source-compat but is ignored at runtime; ingredient search returns as a labeled tier in dad's broader "Make search way better" backlog entry. Closes the live-API gap: a 2026-05-30 curl returned exactly 4 posts with "nacho" in their titles (ids 524, 274, 5016, 736); pre-T-642 surfaced 3 + 17 false positives, post-T-642 surfaces 4 + 0.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/TitleSearchMatcher.swift` (new — pure helper, Foundation-only); `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/TitleSearchMatcherTests.swift` (new — 17 L1 cases including the verbatim Cast-Iron-Bacon-Wrapped-Pickles no-match); `Packages/DODNetworking/Sources/DODNetworking/WPRestClient.swift` (new `searchPageSize = 100` constant); `Packages/DODNetworking/Sources/DODNetworking/WPRestClient+Posts.swift` (search now defaults to `searchPageSize`); `Packages/DODNetworking/Tests/DODNetworkingTests/WPRestClientTests.swift` (new L2 case `searchUsesWidePageSizeForTitlePrecision`); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchResultMerger.swift` (rewritten — title-precision filter + tier sort + ingredient-pass deferral); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchResultMergerTests.swift` (rewritten — pins the new tier contract + the live-API end-to-end seal); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelTests.swift` + `SearchViewModelT637Tests.swift` (existing tests updated where they relied on the pre-CL-120 "any REST hit counts" behavior — realistic title-bearing fixtures, plus one test that explicitly pins the v1-deferred offline+local edge case → `.offline`).
- **AC:** US-12 amendment / AC-12.1 amendment (title-tier-only ranking + ingredient-pass deferral), US-3 amendment / AC-3.2 amendment (excerpt-match strike-through), US-29 amendment / AC-29.1 amendment (Try-pill result set filtered to title matches). New **REG-29** locks the contract.
- **Est:** ~1h (helper + per_page bump + merger rewrite + 17 L1 + 1 L2 + 2 L1 pipeline tests + spec graduation + verify).
- **Deps:** — (independent; runs against current main). Coordination: T-720 (voice-quality engine) lives in `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/**` — zero source overlap. T-710 (design-tokens + L4 baseline re-record) touches snapshot baselines but not search; no conflict.
- **||:** P19-search-nacho-bug. Touches `Packages/DODSupport/**` (new helper + tests), `Packages/DODNetworking/**` (one new constant + one test), and `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` (rewritten merger + test updates) — three packages, source-side-conflict-free with any in-flight branch. **`e2e` label applied** — search journey contract is user-facing (typing → results), the filter touches the merger seam every search path traverses. The L4 `SearchViewSnapshotTests` baselines for the results state do not re-record because the snapshot test fixtures use realistic title-bearing inputs that match under the new filter (verified by re-running the suite).

### T-644 — Cook-time filter: Apple-Timer wheel-picker range (US-12 amendment, CL-122, REG-31)

- **What:** Replace the Search-page cook-time filter chip's bucket `Menu` with an Apple-Timer-style two-wheel min/max range picker presented as a `.presentationDetents([.medium])` half-sheet. User asked for this verbatim. **Model change.** `CookTimeBucket` enum is deleted (only call sites are the chip menu + tests). `SearchFilters` gains `cookTimeMinSeconds: Int?` (nil = no lower bound, inclusive) and `cookTimeMaxSeconds: Int?` (nil = no upper bound, inclusive); `apply()` becomes `(min ?? 0) <= totalSeconds <= (max ?? .max)`; `isAllDefault` updated. Inverted range (min > max) is allowed — `apply()` correctly returns empty since no totalSeconds satisfies both; no UI enforcement (the empty-results state handles it). **UI.** New `CookTimeRangeSheet` view: an `HStack` of two `Picker.pickerStyle(.wheel)` views with "Min" / "Max" section headers in centered secondary text. Both wheels share a value list — first entry is `"Any"` (= nil bound for that side), then 5/10/15/20/30/45/60/75/90/120/150/180/240 minutes. "Apply" button commits the selection on dismiss; "Reset" link clears both wheels to "Any"; drag-down dismisses without applying (no auto-commit on selection change — Apple-Timer pattern). Accessibility: each wheel labeled "Minimum cook time" / "Maximum cook time"; Apply button labeled "Apply cook time filter". New `dod.search.cookTimeWheelMax` identifier on the right wheel for stable E2E. **Chip.** Replace the `Menu` with a `Button` that opens the sheet. Chip-label states (no `≤` / `≥` symbol — strict rule per REG-31): both nil → `"Any time"`; only min → `"<min> or more"`; only max → `"<max> or less"`; both with `min != max` → `"<min>–<max>"` (en-dash); both with `min == max` → `"<value>"`. `isOn` is `cookTimeMinSeconds != nil || cookTimeMaxSeconds != nil`. The chip's `dod.search.cookTimeChip` identifier is preserved (same handle still opens the new sheet). **Duration formatter.** New pure helper `CookTimeFormatter.label(seconds:)` in `SearchFilters.swift`: `<3600 → "\(s/60) min"`, `3600 → "1 hr"`, `% 3600 == 0 → "\(hours) hr"`, else `"\(hours) hr \(minutes) min"`. Shared by the wheel rows and the chip label so formatting stays consistent. **Hydration.** The T-637 / CL-106 `kickOffCookTimeHydrationIfNeeded(...)` guard updates from `filters.cookTime != nil` to `filters.cookTimeMinSeconds != nil || filters.cookTimeMaxSeconds != nil`; the fan-out (20-item cap, fire-and-forget Task, reapplyFilters on landing) is byte-identical. T-637 contract unchanged.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchFilters.swift` (delete `CookTimeBucket` enum, add `cookTimeMinSeconds` / `cookTimeMaxSeconds` to `SearchFilters`, update `apply()` + `isAllDefault`, add `CookTimeFormatter` enum + `cookTimeChipLabel(min:max:)` helper); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/CookTimeRangeSheet.swift` (new — the half-sheet view with the two `Picker.pickerStyle(.wheel)`s + Apply / Reset buttons + accessibility identifiers); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` (`cookTimeChip` swaps `Menu` → `Button` + `.sheet(isPresented:)`; label computation via the new `cookTimeChipLabel(min:max:)`); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel.swift` (`kickOffCookTimeHydrationIfNeeded` guard update); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchFiltersTests.swift` (replace bucket tests with the 5 new min/max cases + 5 formatter cases + 5 chip-label cases); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelT637Tests.swift` (rewrite to use `cookTimeMaxSeconds = 30 * 60` instead of `.cookTime = .under30`); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchResultMergerTests.swift` (rewrite the composition test to use the min/max model); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/FilterChipRowSnapshotTests.swift` (host fixture switches to `SearchFilters(cookTimeMaxSeconds: 30 * 60)`); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/__Snapshots__/FilterChipRowSnapshotTests/*.png` (re-record); `UITests/E2E/CoreUserJourneysE2ETests.swift` (`test_search_cook_time_filter_narrows_results` rewrite — tap chip → wait for sheet → scroll right wheel to "30 min" → tap "Apply" → assert results narrow).
- **AC:** US-12 amendment / AC-12.2 amendment (chip flips from bucket menu to min/max wheel sheet — four label states + the wheel-picker contract). AC-12.3 unchanged (filters compose; hydration still fires on cache miss). New **REG-31** locks the no-`≤`-symbol contract + the max-only-narrows contract + the two-wheels-with-Min-on-left contract.
- **Est:** ~60 min (model + sheet + chip swap + formatter + 15 L1 tests + E2E rewrite + snapshot re-record + spec).
- **Deps:** T-637 (on `main` — the hydration path the new model reuses); T-636 (on `main` — the chip-row simplification this PR's chip swap inherits). Independent of every other in-flight branch.
- **||:** P19-search-cook-time-wheel. Touches `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` source + tests + spec + `UITests/E2E/**` — zero source-side conflict with any in-flight branch. **`e2e` label applied** — the cook-time filter is a daily user touchpoint; the sheet replaces a menu the user has been tapping, so the journey contract is user-facing.

### T-643 — Category-name match path on Search (US-12 amendment, US-29 amendment, CL-121, REG-30)

- **What:** Hot-fix follow-up to T-642 / CL-120. T-642's strict title-precision filter inadvertently broke category-navigation queries — typing `"Dessert Recipes"` now returns 0 results because no individual recipe is titled "Dessert Recipes" (they're titled "Apple Crumble", "Cherry Cobbler", etc., and were previously surfaced only by WP's body/category match which T-642 cut out). Fix: add a **parallel category-match path** to `SearchViewModel.performSearch()` that fires alongside the existing title-match path. **Architecture.** `performSearch()` fans out two `async let` tasks: **Path A (unchanged from T-642)** — `?search=<q>&per_page=100` → `TitleSearchMatcher` filter → tier-ranked title matches. **Path B (new)** — check if the query matches any known category name via a new `CategoryNameMatcher.match(query:in:)` helper; if yes, fetch `?categories=<id>&per_page=100` for each matched category (cap at 2) and include all returned posts. **Merge:** union of A + B deduped by post id; Path A's title-match tier ordering comes first, then Path B-only contributions in WP's natural date-desc order. Path B failure does NOT block Path A — graceful degradation logs and renders Path A alone. **`CategoryNameMatcher` (new pure helper in `DODSupport`).** Normalizes both sides via `TitleSearchMatcher.normalize(_:)` (HTML-entity decode + lowercase + punctuation→space + whitespace collapse — re-used identically so `&amp;` / em-dashes normalize once with one rule). For each category, derive its **"topic"** by stripping a trailing `" recipes"` token from the normalized name — `"Dessert Recipes" → "dessert"`, `"Chicken and Poultry Recipes" → "chicken and poultry"`. Match rules (any of): (a) full-name equal, (b) topic equal, (c) query is a substring of the topic AND query is ≥ 4 characters (4-char floor guards against junk like `"the"`), or (d) topic is a substring of the query. **Junk-query guards:** single-token `"recipe"`/`"recipes"` is explicitly rejected so the matcher doesn't fan out to every category in the catalog. Matched categories sorted by post count desc, capped at top-2 to bound network cost. **Wiring:** new `SearchDependencies.fetchPosts(categoryID:limit:) async throws -> [RecipeListItem]` is a thin wrapper around the existing `WPRestClient.posts(categoryID:page:perPage:)` (live since T-081 / T-091 — **no new REST endpoint** surface; the prompt called this out explicitly: reuse don't add). The "Latest Recipes" Try-pill T-637 special case is untouched — it routes through `surfaceLatestRecipes(...)` and never enters `performSearch()`, so the new Path B never fires for that pill. Live-API truth (2026-05-30): `?categories=336&per_page=100` against `dutchovendaddy.com` returns ~53 dessert posts; the post-fix pipeline now surfaces all of them when the user types "Dessert Recipes". The fix is **additive** — T-642's title-precision filter is byte-identical, the `searchPageSize = 100` constant is unchanged, the local-ingredient pass v1-deferral is unchanged, the "Nachos" contract from REG-29 stays intact (Path B returns `[]` because no nacho category exists, so Path A's 4 title matches stand alone).
- **Files:** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/CategoryNameMatcher.swift` (new — pure helper, Foundation-only, accesses `TitleSearchMatcher.normalize` via in-module internal); `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/CategoryNameMatcherTests.swift` (new — 8+ L1 cases against the live-API top-8 category fixture); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchDependencies.swift` (new protocol method `fetchPosts(categoryID:limit:)` + live impl that calls `client.posts(categoryID:page:perPage:)`); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel.swift` (parallel `async let` in `performSearch()` + dedupe-by-id union + Path B failure-to-Path A fallback); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/FakeSearchDependencies.swift` (new `categoryFetchResults: [Int: [RecipeListItem]]` map + `fetchPostsCalls: [(Int, Int)]` recorder + `categoryFetchShouldThrow: Bool` flag + the new method); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelT643Tests.swift` (new — 4 L1 pipeline cases: `"Dessert Recipes"` → 53 via Path B; `"Chicken"` → union of Path A + Path B deduped; `"Nachos"` → Path B empty so 4 title matches stand; Path B failure → Path A renders alone).
- **AC:** US-12 amendment / AC-12.1 amendment (Path A + Path B union with the topic-stripping rule and the top-2 cap); US-29 amendment / AC-29.1 amendment (Try-pill name that matches a known category surfaces that category's posts + title matches); US-3 amendment / AC-3.2 amendment (title-match joined by category-name match). New **REG-30** locks the contract.
- **Est:** ~45 min (helper + dependency method + 2 viewmodel commits + 8 L1 matcher tests + 4 L1 pipeline tests + spec graduation + verify).
- **Deps:** T-642 (on `main` — the parent title-precision filter this PR is additive to). Independent of every other in-flight branch.
- **||:** P19-search-category-match. Touches `Packages/DODSupport/**` (one new helper + tests) and `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` (dependency + viewmodel + fake + tests) — two packages, source-side-conflict-free with any in-flight branch. **`e2e` label applied** — search journey contract is user-facing (typing → results) and the pipeline change touches the path every search query traverses.

### T-641 — Filter "Uncategorized" out of rotating "Try" pool (US-29 amendment, CL-119, AC-29.7 amendment)

- **What:** Hot-fix follow-up to T-639 / CL-117 (sibling to T-640 / CL-118). The rotating slate occasionally lands "Uncategorized" (a real WP category, slug `uncategorized`, count 1 on the live `dutchovendaddy.com` REST as of 2026-05-30) in one of its five shuffled slots, and tapping it fires a literal text search for the word "Uncategorized" — meaningless against the cookbook corpus, weakens the exploration goal of the AC-29.7 Try section. Fix: add a static `excludedTryPoolSlugs: Set<String>` on the `SearchViewModel+T639.swift` extension (starts with `["uncategorized"]`, extensible for future junk categories) and apply at the `topTrySlatePool` boundary via `.filter { !Self.excludedTryPoolSlugs.contains($0.slug.lowercased()) }`. Slug is the canonical WP join key (stable across category renames; `name` is editor-mutable and `id` is per-install). The set holds canonical lowercase slugs and the category side is `.lowercased()` at compare time, so the lookup is case-insensitive without a custom hasher. Pool size goes from 28 → 27 — comfortably wide for the 5-shuffle, no curated 1:1 replacement needed.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel+T639.swift` (new `static let excludedTryPoolSlugs: Set<String>` declaration on the extension + filter at the `topTrySlatePool` accessor's source-pool chain + updated doc comment on `topTrySlatePool` noting the exclusion). New tests in `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelT640Tests.swift` (T-640's sibling file, since it already imports the same fixtures): `topTrySlatePool_excludes_uncategorized` (fixture with `slug: "uncategorized"` + 5 others → returns 5, Uncategorized absent), `topTrySlatePool_exclusion_is_case_insensitive_on_slug` (fixture with `slug: "Uncategorized"` uppercase-U → still filtered), `pickTrySlate_does_not_surface_excluded_categories` (sanity end-to-end: pool that contained Uncategorized produces a slate that doesn't contain it).
- **AC:** US-29 amendment / AC-29.7 amendment ("exclusion set" sentence inline with the canonical-lowercase-slug match key and extensibility note).
- **Est:** ~15 min (three-line code change + 3 L1 tests + spec + verify).
- **Deps:** T-639 (parent rotating-pool task) — already on `main`. Independent of T-640's cache-rule fix.
- **||:** P16-search-try-pool-exclude-junk. Touches only `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` source + tests + spec — zero source-side conflict with any in-flight branch. No L4 snapshot baseline re-record (the snapshot tests' fixture pool does not include Uncategorized, so the rendered slate is unchanged). No `e2e` label (three-line filter + tests).

## Phase 17 — Voice Mode quality + voice selection (2026-05-29)

Graduates the round-9 backlog item "Cook Mode voice sounds robotic — want natural-sounding voices + male/female toggle in Settings" (US-40 amendment / CL-109). Three tasks: T-720 ships the selection engine (no Settings UI, no conflict with the open PRs); T-721 ships the Settings → Voice picker once the SettingsView-owning PRs land, extended (DUT-5) with the resolved-quality readout + a Preview-voice button; T-722 (DUT-5) adds the "download a better voice" nudge + the `VoiceSelector` quality helper it gates on. The honest framing (CL-123): the engine already reaches the best installed voice, but a stock device only has the compact ("robotic") tier preinstalled and the app has NO public API to bundle or trigger the OS voice download — so the user-facing fix is to (a) SHOW them the tier they're on and (b) guide them to download a natural voice in iOS Settings, not an engine rewrite or cloud TTS.

### T-720 — Phase a: voice-quality + gender-selection engine (US-40)

- **What:** replace `SystemSpeechSynthesizer`'s `AVSpeechSynthesisVoice(language:)` compact-voice resolution with `VoiceSelector` — picks the best installed quality tier (premium > enhanced > default) honoring a `dod.voice.preferredGenderV1` gender preference (default female), gender-primary + quality-secondary. Falls back to the prior behavior when no installed voice matches the language. The default-female preference means the quality upgrade lands immediately with no UI.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/VoiceSelection.swift` (new — `VoiceGender` / `VoiceQuality` / `VoiceDescriptor` / `VoicePreference` value types + `VoiceSelector` algorithm + `VoicePreferenceStore`), `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/VoiceReader.swift` (`SystemSpeechSynthesizer` rewrite — `resolveVoice` + `descriptor(for:)`), `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/VoiceSelectionTests.swift` (new — 11 L1 selection cases), `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/VoicePreferenceStoreTests.swift` (new — 7 L1 persistence cases).
- **AC:** 40.9, 40.10, 40.11.
- **Est:** ~4h (engine + value types + store + 18 L1 tests + spec graduation).
- **||:** P17-voice. Touches only `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/**` — zero conflict with the open SettingsView PRs (#72 iCloud Sync, #86 design tokens) or #87 (comments). No `e2e` label (engine-only, no composition-root or routing change).

#### T-721 — Phase b: Settings → Cook Mode Voice gender picker + quality readout + Preview (US-40) — ✅ shipped 2026-05-30, extended 2026-05-31 (DUT-5)

- **Shipped (the round-9 ask).** A **Settings → "Cook Mode voice"** `Picker` (Female / Male / No preference) bound to `SettingsViewModel.voiceGender`, which reads + writes the same `VoicePreferenceStore` (`dod.voice.preferredGenderV1`) the T-720 engine reads at every utterance — so flipping it changes the next step read with **no Cook Mode restart**. Default `.female`; unknown stored value → `.female` (AC-40.11). Accessibility id `settings-picker-voice-gender`.
- **Extended 2026-05-31 (DUT-5, AC-40.12).** The voice section now also shows a read-only **"Voice quality"** row (`Default` / `Enhanced` / `Premium` / `Unknown`) so the user can SEE whether they're on a robotic (`Default`) voice, plus a **"Preview voice"** button that speaks a fixed recipe-step sample with the current gender pick. The readout reports the tier of the *resolved* voice (gender-aware), so flipping to a gender with only a compact voice installed drops the readout to `Default` — the in-context cue to download the matching natural voice. Both go through a new `VoicePreviewing` seam (catalog + speak), injected into `SettingsViewModel` so the readout / nudge / preview are L1-tested with a recording double (no AVFoundation on the macOS slice); production wires a `SystemVoicePreviewer` (`AVSpeechSynthesizer` + `VoiceSelector`, the only AVFoundation path in `DODFeatureFeed`) from the composition root through `FeedView`. Accessibility ids `settings-voice-quality`, `settings-button-voice-preview`.
- **Supporting refactor (CL-122).** The AVFoundation-free voice types + store (`VoiceGender` / `VoiceQuality` / `VoiceDescriptor` / `VoicePreference` / `VoiceSelector` / `VoicePreferenceStore`) moved from `DODFeatureRecipeDetail` → **`DODSupport`** (beside `GuestIdentityStore`) so `DODFeatureFeed`'s `SettingsView` binds to them **without a feature→feature dependency edge**. `VoiceGender` gained `CaseIterable` + `Hashable` (picker plumbing); `displayName` (UI copy) is a `DODFeatureFeed`-local extension so the domain stays free of view strings. `VoiceReader` consumes the types via `import DODSupport`; the two voice test files moved to `DODSupportTests`.
- **Files:** moved `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/VoiceSelection.swift` (+ `VoiceSelectionTests` / `VoicePreferenceStoreTests` → `DODSupportTests`), `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/VoiceReader.swift` (import), `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/{SettingsView,SettingsViewModel}.swift`, `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Package.swift` (test-target dep), `SettingsViewModelVoiceTests.swift`. **DUT-5 adds:** `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/{VoicePreview,SettingsViewModel+Voice,SettingsView+Voice}.swift` (new — the `VoicePreviewing` seam + `SystemVoicePreviewer`, the view-model voice accessors, the `VoiceSection` view), `FeedView.swift` + `App/TabStack.swift` (thread the previewer), extended `SettingsViewModelVoiceTests` + `SettingsViewSnapshotTests`.
- **Verified (DUT-5).** DODSupport 290 tests pass; DODFeatureFeed 51 `swift test` cases pass (17 SettingsViewModel voice); DODFeatureRecipeDetail 130 pass; DODSupport + DODFeatureRecipeDetail + DODFeatureFeed + app target all build clean on the iPhone 17 sim; 7 `SettingsViewSnapshotTests` baselines (incl. 2 new voice-section states) pass on iPhone 17.
- **AC:** US-40 AC-40.10 (selection honors the stored preference) / AC-40.11 (canonical key, female default, unknown→female) / AC-40.12 (resolved-quality readout + Preview voice).
- **Deferred to a follow-on (NOT the round-9 ask):** the automatic/specific-voice mode (per-locale `speechVoices()` list with a per-voice picker), and the `voicePreferenceChanged` adoption analytics event + constitution §9 allowlist amendment. AC-40.14+ stay reserved for these.
- **Deps:** **T-703 / PR #72** (iCloud Sync section) + **PR #86** (design tokens) — both landed; T-721 branched off the merged main.

#### T-722 — Phase c: "Download a better voice" nudge + `VoiceSelector` quality helper (US-40 / AC-40.13, DUT-5)

- **Why (CL-123).** The T-720 engine already reaches the best *installed* voice, but Apple ships only the compact ("robotic") tier preinstalled and there is **no public API** to bundle or trigger the OS download of the natural (`.enhanced` / `.premium`) tiers — the user has to fetch them in iOS Settings → Accessibility → Spoken Content → Voices. So the remaining user-facing fix is a guidance nudge, not more engine work (and explicitly NOT cloud / third-party TTS).
- **What:** (1) a small `VoiceSelector` quality helper — `bestAvailableQuality(forLanguage:from:)` → `VoiceQuality?` + `hasNaturalVoice(forLanguage:in:)` → `Bool`, built on the existing descriptor projection (gender-ignored: once *any* natural voice is installed the download is done). (2) A dismissible **"Want a more natural voice?"** tip in the Settings voice section, visible only when the best installed voice for the device language is `.default`-only AND the user hasn't dismissed it (persisted to `dod.settings.downloadVoiceTipDismissedV1`; a once-waved-off nudge never re-shows). The copy spells out the full Settings path because `UIApplication.openSettingsURLString` only deep-links to the app's own Settings root, not the Accessibility pane; an **"Open Settings"** button opens that root via `@Environment(\.openURL)`. Accessibility ids `settings-voice-download-tip`, `settings-voice-tip-open-settings`, `settings-voice-tip-dismiss`.
- **Nudge surface decision (CL-123).** Surfaced in **Settings only** this PR — Settings is where the voice is configured and the quality readout already lives, so the tip sits beside the cause. The Cook Mode voice-toggle entry point was considered but deferred to keep the PR bounded (it would thread the AVFoundation seam into `DODFeatureRecipeDetail` + re-record `CookModeView` L4 baselines for no new information — the Settings readout already tells the user when they're robotic). Reserved as a follow-up if device testing shows users miss it.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/VoiceSelection.swift` (+ `VoiceSelectionTests` — 11 new helper cases), `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/{SettingsViewModel+Voice,SettingsView+Voice}.swift` (the nudge state + view), extended `SettingsViewModelVoiceTests` (nudge-visibility + dismissal) + `SettingsViewSnapshotTests` (compact-only-shows-nudge baseline).
- **AC:** US-40 AC-40.13 (download nudge when only the compact tier is installed; dismissible + persisted; opens Settings).
- **Deps:** T-721 (the voice section + the `VoicePreviewing` catalog seam this nudge reads). No `e2e` label (no App Intent / routing change; the `openURL` deep-link is a single system action, exercised by L1 + the L4 nudge baseline).
- **||:** P17-voice. `DODSupport` helper + `DODFeatureFeed` voice section only — no conflict with other in-flight work.

## Phase 18 — Site/app design coordination (2026-05-29)

Graduates the round-9 "Site ↔ app design coordination" backlog entry (US-43 / CL-110..CL-114). Closes the seven measurable visual gaps between dutchovendaddy.com and the iOS app over four phases. Phase a (T-710) ships the foundational tokens + typography; Phases b–d (T-711..T-713) deliver the visible compositional changes behind a `DODFeed.layoutVariant` flag so each screen reverts independently if a regression surfaces on real device.

### T-710 — Phase a: design tokens + typography + L4 baseline re-record (US-43)
