### T-648 — Shared `DODSearchField` component: unify Search-tab and Categories-tab search bars (US-3 amendment, US-29 amendment, US-19 amendment, CL-126, REG-32)

- **What:** Extract a new `public struct DODSearchField: View` in `DODDesignSystem` so the Search-tab and Categories-tab search bars share both **the brand brown surface** (`DODColor.surfaceElevated`) AND **the pill shape** (`Capsule(style: .continuous)`) — the user-stated end state is Search's COLOR + Categories' SHAPE on both bars. **The shape today.** Search renders an inline `HStack(magnifyingglass, TextField, optional Clear)` with `.padding(DODSpacing.sm)` + `.background(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: DODSpacing.sm).fill(DODColor.surfaceElevated))` — correct brand brown color, moderate corner radius (12pt — not pill enough). Categories uses `.searchable(text:placement:.navigationBarDrawer(displayMode:.automatic))` — dark mode renders the system `secondarySystemBackground` (near-black) inside the system tall capsule — correct pill shape, wrong color. **The fix — extract once, adopt twice.** New `DODSearchField` body: `HStack(spacing: DODSpacing.xs) { magnifyingglass.foregroundStyle(.labelSecondary), TextField(placeholder, text: $text).autocorrectionDisabled().dodFont(.body).foregroundStyle(.label), optional clear button when !text.isEmpty }` wrapped with `.padding(.vertical, .sm).padding(.horizontal, .md)` and `.background(Capsule(style: .continuous).fill(DODColor.surfaceElevated))` — gives the wider-than-tall pill proportion with the brand surface. Public init: `init(text: Binding<String>, placeholder: String, onClear: (() -> Void)? = nil)`. `onClear` defaults to a plain text-clear when no closure supplied — so the simplest call site (`DODSearchField(text: $searchText, placeholder: "Search")`) works without ceremony. **Search-tab adoption** (`SearchView.swift`): delete the inline `searchField` private var; the body's first child becomes `DODSearchField(text: $viewModel.query, placeholder: "Search recipes", onClear: { viewModel.clear() })` — the `onClear` closure preserves the VM-side state/items/lastQuery/debounce-cancel cleanup, not just the query-string clear. **Categories-tab adoption** (`CategoryListView.swift`): remove `.searchable(text:placement:.navigationBarDrawer(...))` from the loaded-list modifier chain; wrap the existing List in `VStack(spacing: 0) { DODSearchField(text: $searchText, placeholder: "Search").padding(.horizontal, .md).padding(.bottom, .sm); List { ... } }` so the field sits as a sticky element above the List. List keeps its existing `.scrollContentBackground(.hidden)` + `.background(DODColor.surface)` + `.listStyle(.insetGrouped)` modifiers byte-identical; `.navigationTitle("Categories")` stays on the outer view. **The deliberate trade:** Categories' search bar no longer slides into a nav-bar drawer — it sits as a sticky element. Alternative (re-skin the system `.searchable` bar via `UISearchBar.appearance()` proxy) was rejected because the proxy can't configure radius and would taint every future search-bar surface globally.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Sources/DODDesignSystem/Components/DODSearchField.swift` (new — public struct, doc-comment citing T-648 / CL-126 and naming the two call sites); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` (delete inline `searchField` private var, replace with `DODSearchField` call); `Packages/DODFeatureCategories/Sources/DODFeatureCategories/CategoryListView.swift` (remove `.searchable`, wrap List in `VStack` with `DODSearchField` above); `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Tests/DODDesignSystemTests/ComponentsTests.swift` (three new L1 smoke cases — constructs with text binding; clear button visibility flips with text non-empty; `onClear` closure fires when supplied); the existing L4 baselines (`SearchViewSnapshotTests` / `CategoryListViewSnapshotTests`) re-record on next sim run (record: .missing) for the new pill shape + the Categories bar position move.
- **AC:** US-3 amendment / AC-3.5 amendment (the search field is the shared `DODSearchField` component — brand brown surface, Capsule shape, magnifyingglass leading, conditional `xmark.circle.fill` clear button with VM-cleanup `onClear`); US-29 amendment (visual surface for the Search tab matches the rotating Try-pill capsules below it); US-19 amendment / AC-19.3 amendment (`.searchable` modifier struck through in favor of the shared `DODSearchField` slotted above the List). New **REG-32** locks the cross-tab visual contract.
- **Est:** ~45 min (one new component file + two call-site swaps + 3 L1 component tests + spec graduation + verify).
- **Deps:** main is at CL-125 / T-647 (list-rows brand surface) — independent of every in-flight branch.
- **||:** P21-shared-search-field. Touches `Packages/DODDesignSystem/**` (one new component file + 3 L1 cases), `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` (one call-site swap), `Packages/DODFeatureCategories/**` (one call-site swap + remove `.searchable`). Three packages, source-side-conflict-free with any in-flight branch. **`e2e` label applied** — the change touches two tab surfaces, the user's first-touch interaction on each, and the L4 snapshot baselines that lock the home-screen-of-each-tab visual.

### T-649 — "Did you mean?" search-suggestion banner when results sparse (US-12 amendment, US-29 amendment, CL-127)

- **What:** Graduates dad's "Make search way better" backlog bullet "**'Did you mean...' suggestions when results are sparse (< 3). Use a stem-based suggestion table sourced from the cached recipe titles**". Post-T-642 / CL-120 the title-precision filter is strict by design — `"naxxos"` returns 0 results because Levenshtein-1 fuzzy doesn't admit two-edit-distance neighbors. T-649 adds a soft escape hatch: when the merged result set settles with **fewer than 3 items** AND the user did issue a search, the viewmodel computes a closest-cached-title-token suggestion via Levenshtein-`(1, 4]` and surfaces it as a tappable banner above the result list. **Three pieces.** **(1) New pure helper** `SearchSuggestionEngine` in `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/SearchSuggestionEngine.swift` — `public static func suggest(query: String, cachedTitles: [String], minDistance: Int = 1, maxDistance: Int = 4) -> String?`. Tokenizes `cachedTitles` (lowercase, punctuation-stripped, length ≥ 3 to skip "a"/"of"/"and" noise), finds the closest cache token to any query token with distance in `(minDistance, maxDistance]`, substitutes only the changed token back into the query string (preserves multi-word phrases verbatim — `"cast iron naxhos"` → `"cast iron nachos"`), tie-breaks across equal-distance candidates by cache token frequency. Self-suggestion (normalize-equivalent to the user's input) → nil. Reuses `TitleSearchMatcher.levenshteinDistance(_:_:)` for the math (same-module package-internal helper — no copy-paste). **(2) `SearchViewModel` wiring.** Add `public private(set) var didYouMean: String?` (observable). After `performSearch()` finishes (extension hook in `SearchViewModel+T643.swift`'s `applyFiltersAndFinalize(...)`), compute `didYouMean` when `items.count < 3 && !lastQuery.isEmpty`; clear otherwise. New `SearchDependencies.cachedRecipeTitles() async throws -> [String]` wraps a new `RecipeStore.cachedRecipeTitles() async throws -> [String]` (`try modelContext.fetch(FetchDescriptor<CachedRecipe>()).map(\.title)`) — reuses the existing cache, no new schema. New public method `applyDidYouMean()` rewrites `query` (which triggers the existing debounce path) and clears the suggestion. **(3) SearchView UI.** Above the result list (between `FilterChipRow` and the result rows), a tappable banner: `HStack { Text("Did you mean:") + Text(suggestion).foregroundStyle(DODColor.accent).underline() }` with `.onTapGesture { viewModel.applyDidYouMean() }`. Gated on `state == .results || state == .noResults` so the banner never flashes during the `.searching` transition. Brand-accent color (orange) + underline so it pops as a one-tap rescue. `accessibilityLabel("Did you mean \(suggestion)? Tap to search.")` per AX5.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/SearchSuggestionEngine.swift` (new — pure helper); `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/TitleSearchMatcher.swift` (promote `levenshteinDistance(_:_:)` from package-internal to `internal` — no behavior change, just access); `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/SearchSuggestionEngineTests.swift` (new — 9 L1 cases per CL-127's contract); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel.swift` (add `didYouMean` storage + `applyDidYouMean()` public method); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchViewModel+T643.swift` (compute suggestion at end of `applyFiltersAndFinalize(...)` finalize hop — keeps the main viewmodel file under the 400-line cap); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchDependencies.swift` (add `cachedRecipeTitles() async throws -> [String]` to the protocol + Live impl); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` (banner above result list — gated on settled state + non-nil suggestion); `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/RecipeStore.swift` (new `cachedRecipeTitles()` actor method); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/FakeSearchDependencies.swift` (add `cachedTitlesArray: [String]` slot + protocol stub); `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/SearchViewModelT649Tests.swift` (new — 3 L1 cases for the integration).
- **AC:** US-12 amendment / AC-12.1 amendment (the merged result set gains a downstream `didYouMean` hook when settled with fewer than 3 items; suggestion engine reads from cached titles already populated via AC-12.1's hydration path); US-29 amendment / AC-29.3 amendment (the banner co-exists with the no-results empty-state glyph; tap → re-run search).
- **Est:** ~50 min (one new pure helper + 1 L1 file + viewmodel wiring extension + view-side banner + 3 L1 integration tests + spec graduation + verify).
- **Deps:** main is at CL-126 / T-648 (shared `DODSearchField`) — independent of every in-flight branch. Builds on T-642's `TitleSearchMatcher` (already on main) for the shared Levenshtein helper.
- **||:** P22-did-you-mean. Touches `Packages/DODSupport/**` (one new helper + 1 L1 file + a one-line access modifier promotion), `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**` (viewmodel wiring + view-side banner + 3 L1 cases + fake-dep slot), `Packages/DODPersistence/**` (one new actor method). Three packages, source-side-conflict-free with any in-flight branch. **`e2e` label applied** — the change touches the Search tab's first-touch interaction when the user's query is sparse (the dominant friction surface the banner exists to soften).

### T-731 — Unify typography to System font: revert CL-113's SF Rounded family swap on `heading` / `caption` / `brand` (US-43 amendment, CL-128)

**Note on numbering:** originally landed as T-650 in PR #118 (`feat/T-650-uniform-system-font`), but T-650 was already taken by the Gallery ↔ List view toggle (US-38 / CL-64) shipped earlier in PR #58. Renumbered to T-731 (next free above @adamsned's T-730 CI fix) in a fast-forward docs PR after the merge. The original branch name + squash-merge commit subject preserve the T-650 marker for git-history traceability; every spec / clarification / task / source-comment reference now reads T-731.

- **What:** Reverts CL-113's SF Rounded family swap on `DODType.heading`, `DODType.caption`, and `DODType.brand`. The entire app returns to one font (the default System font, SF Pro). Joint @adamsned (dad) + @spencer0706 (Spencer) offline decision today: after seeing CL-113's rounded-on-card-titles-and-chips ship on real device for ~48 hours, both agreed that uniform System font reads better than the mixed-family register. Brand identity comes through weight, color, and spacing instead of via SF Rounded specifically. **Three token swaps in `Typography.swift`:** `heading` from `.system(.headline, design: .rounded)` → `.system(.headline)`; `caption` from `.system(.caption, design: .rounded, weight: .medium)` → `.system(.caption, weight: .medium)`; `brand` from `.system(size: 22, weight: .bold, design: .rounded)` → `.system(size: 22, weight: .bold)`. **What stays from CL-113:** the `.bold` weight on `displayLarge` + `displayMedium` (move 1) is preserved; the `.medium` weight on `caption` is preserved; the `DODType.brand` token's existence (size 22, `.bold`, reserved for wordmark moments) is preserved. **L4 baseline strategy:** per the CL-115 / T-730 pattern, **delete the affected baselines and let CI re-record** via the `record: .missing` workflow. Conservative over-deletion is the safer trade — under-deletion leaves stale baselines that block CI.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-43.7 amended via strike-through + replacement framing — uniform System font for `heading` + `caption`, brand register via weight/color/spacing; AC-43.8 amended via strike-through on the font literal — `design: .rounded` portion removed, size 22 + `.bold` weight stand); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-113 annotated in-place with strike-through + "(SF Rounded family swap reverted by CL-128 / T-731, 2026-05-31)" lineage marker on moves (2) + (3); CL-128 appended after CL-124 with the full reversal trail); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source (commit 2):** `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Sources/DODDesignSystem/Typography.swift` (three token swaps + top-doc + per-token doc-comment updates with the CL-128 lineage markers).
  - **Tests (commit 2 — deletions only):** snapshot baseline deletions across `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Tests/DODDesignSystemTests/__Snapshots__/`, `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Tests/DODFeatureFeedTests/__Snapshots__/`, `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/Tests/DODFeatureSavedTests/__Snapshots__/`, `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Tests/DODFeatureSearchTests/__Snapshots__/`, `Packages/DODFeatureCategories/Tests/DODFeatureCategoriesTests/__Snapshots__/`, `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/__Snapshots__/`. No `.swift` test files touched — only the `.png` baselines under `__Snapshots__/`.
  - **Out of bounds:** `body` + `bodyEmphasized` (already default design — untouched); `displayLarge` + `displayMedium` (CL-113's `.bold` weight swap stands); all view-side call sites that reference `.heading` / `.caption` / `.brand` (byte-identical — `Font` type signatures unchanged); `DODColor` / `DODSpacing` tokens (untouched); any L1 test that asserts `Font` instance equality (robust to the swap — compares `Font` value, not design family); the `DODType.brand` token's call sites in `Phase c (T-712)` (still reserved; only the token's font literal changes).
- **AC:** US-43 amendment / AC-43.7 amended (SF Rounded family swap on `heading` + `caption` reverted; uniform System font; `.medium` weight on `caption` preserved); US-43 amendment / AC-43.8 amended (`DODType.brand` font literal drops `design: .rounded`; size 22 + `.bold` weight stand). Pins AC-43.6 (display weights `.bold` — unchanged), AC-43.9 (L4 baseline re-record contract — applied here via delete-and-let-CI-re-record per CL-115 / T-730 pattern), AC-43.10 (L1/L2/L3 no regression).
- **Est:** ~25 min (three token swaps + doc-comment updates + snapshot baseline delete + spec amendments + verify).
- **Deps:** main is at CL-127 / T-649 — independent of every in-flight branch. Source-side touches only `Typography.swift` + snapshot baseline deletions; spec-side touches `spec.md` / `clarifications.md` / `tasks.md`.
- **||:** P22-uniform-system-font. Source-side-conflict-free with any in-flight branch (touches one Swift file + L4 baseline deletions). **`e2e` label applied** — the change is a user-facing visual change across many surfaces (every screen that renders card titles, chips, or time stamps picks up the family revert on next launch).

### T-732 — Recipe detail blurb expansion + drop servings chip (US-4 amendment, CL-129, REG-33)

- **What:** Three coordinated `RecipeDetailView`-only changes that ship together because they share the same surface (the area between the meta pills and the quick-jump row) and the same review unit (the recipe detail's blurb + meta region). **(1)** Drop the `"N servings"` mini-chip entry from `RecipeDetailView.metaPillItems` (the chip is duplicative — `RecipeServingsScaler`'s "Serves \(userServings)" row directly below already shows the canonical Serves indicator per AC-31.1). The `RecipeDetailMetaPills` component itself is unchanged. **(2)** Replace the existing `excerptText` private var's plain `Text(viewModel.listItem.excerpt)` (which surfaced the WordPress `the_excerpt()` server-side `[…]` / `[...]` truncation tail verbatim) with a collapse-by-default + tappable "More" → expanded-rich-blurb + "Less" → collapse-again expand/collapse contract. The collapsed-state displayed excerpt has its trailing `[…]` / `[...]` / bare-ellipsis truncation suffix stripped via a pure helper so the "More" button visually replaces the stranded "[...]". **(3)** Add a new `extractRecipeBlurb(html:) -> String` static helper to `DODSupport.ArticleBodyExtractor` that returns the recipe page's `entry-content` HTML cropped at the first WPRM `<div class="wprm-recipe-container">` open tag — the canonical recipe-card boundary across every WPRM theme. Falls back to the full `entry-content` slice when no WPRM card is present (rare). Parsed via the existing `ArticleHTMLParser.parse(html:)` into `[ArticleBlock]` and stored on `RecipeDetailViewModel.blurbBlocks: [ArticleBlock]` (additive `internal(set)` property, defaults to `[]`, populated alongside the existing `fetchAndParse()` + cached-recipe paths). The expanded blurb renders through a new shared `ArticleBlocksView` helper view extracted from `ArticleDetailView.blockView(_:)` (private → public extraction) — the single source of truth for native block rendering across both surfaces. **HTML-source path: option (b) chosen over option (a)** — option (a) would have reused `extractArticleBody(html:)` (which returns the entire `entry-content` div verbatim including the WPRM card's structured content); option (b) crops at the WPRM card boundary so the ingredient + instructions content stays in its canonical AC-4.2 / AC-4.3 sections and is not duplicated in the blurb. **Articles preserved.** `ArticleDetailView`'s `articleBody` non-empty branch swaps its inline `VStack { ForEach { blockView(...) } }` for `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: blocks)` — same render tree, same fonts, same colors. L4 `ArticleDetailViewSnapshotTests` baselines should NOT need to re-record; if they do, that's a regression to fix before commit.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-4.1 amended via strike-through on `servings` + replacement framing referencing AC-31.1 as the surviving Serves indicator; new AC-4.12 documents the expand/collapse contract on the description), `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-129 appended after CL-124 / before CL-128 with the full three-change rationale + the option (a)/(b) decision trail + REG-33 + the rejected alternatives), `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — shared block renderer (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/ArticleBlocksView.swift` (new — `public struct ArticleBlocksView: View` wrapping the per-block rendering, the `articleImage(_:caption:)` helper, the `placeholder`, and the `imageMaxHeight` static — extracted verbatim from `ArticleDetailView.blockView(_:)` so styling is byte-identical); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/ArticleDetailView.swift` (the non-empty-blocks branch of `articleBody` calls `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: blocks)` instead of the inline `VStack { ForEach { blockView(...) } }`; `blockView(_:)` + `articleImage(_:caption:)` + `placeholder` + `imageMaxHeight` private members are removed).
  - **Source — servings chip removal (commit 3):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailView.swift` (one branch deleted from the `metaPillItems` builder).
  - **Source — blurb expansion (commit 4):** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/ArticleBodyExtractor.swift` (new `extractRecipeBlurb(html:)` static helper); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailView.swift` (new `@State private var isBlurbExpanded: Bool = false`; `excerptText` private var rewritten to collapse-by-default with stripped `[…]` + inline "More" button; expanded branch renders `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: viewModel.blurbBlocks)` + bottom "Less" button; new private static `strippingExcerptTruncationTail(from:)` pure helper); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel.swift` (new additive `public internal(set) var blurbBlocks: [ArticleBlock] = []` property + import for `ArticleBlock`); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch.swift` (populates `blurbBlocks` from `dependencies.fetchHTML(...)` + `ArticleBodyExtractor.extractRecipeBlurb(html:)` + `ArticleHTMLParser.parse(html:)` on the recipe-path `.ready` branch; cached-recipe path in `onAppear()` also populates from the cached `Recipe.articleBodyHTML` when present).

### T-733 — Recipe blurb polish: paragraph cap + "..." tail + visibility gate (US-4 amendment, CL-130)

- **What:** Three refinements to the T-732 / CL-129 recipe blurb expansion that just shipped on `main`. The feature works but the user surfaced three specific complaints during build review on 2026-05-31. **(1) Cap the expanded blurb at 1-2 additional paragraphs.** Add a `paragraphLimit: Int = 2` parameter to `ArticleBodyExtractor.extractRecipeBlurb(html:)`. Walk the HTML string and truncate at the Nth `</p>` close tag — headings / images / lists that sit BEFORE the Nth `<p>` boundary are preserved (they're context for the surrounding paragraphs); content after is dropped. If the source has fewer than `paragraphLimit` paragraphs, return what it has (no padding). `paragraphLimit: 0` returns empty (degenerate-but-safe). Default value is 2 — the user's "1-2 paragraphs" ask. The article-body path (`extractArticleBody` / `extractContentHTML`) is NOT affected — articles render the full body without a cap. Live-API sampling on 2026-05-31 (recipes 21238 and 23034) confirmed 40 and 30 non-empty `<p>` blocks before the WPRM card on long-form blog-style recipes — the pre-T-733 behavior dumped all of them into the expansion. **(2) Restore the visible "..." tail on the collapsed-state excerpt.** In `RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` (the T-732 extension that renders `excerptText`), the collapsed branch now renders `Text(strippedExcerpt + "...")` instead of `Text(strippedExcerpt)` — a literal three-ASCII-dot "..." concatenated directly to the prose (no space), reading as a natural mid-sentence trail before the "More" button. The "..." is view-only — NEVER appended to the model's excerpt, NEVER stored, NEVER rendered in the expanded state (the expanded surface drops the "..." entirely; the rich blocks continue the prose). The existing `strippingExcerptTruncationTail(from:)` pure helper is UNCHANGED — still strips the six WP-source truncation-marker variants — the "..." is an app-controlled visible cue layered on top of the helper's WP-source strip. **(3) Broaden the More/Less visibility gate to "any `.paragraph` block in `blurbBlocks`".** Replace the existing `!viewModel.blurbBlocks.isEmpty` gate with a new `hasExpandableBlurb` computed property on the view model that pattern-matches `blurbBlocks` for at least one `.paragraph` case. A recipe whose pre-WPRM content is only a heading / image / list (no narrative paragraph) gets no More button — there'd be nothing meaningful to expand into. The gate interacts cleanly with the cap from refinement (1): a recipe with zero pre-card `<p>` blocks gets no More button; a recipe with at least one gets the button + the cap-bounded expansion.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-4.12 amended via strike-through + replacement framing on the "full" → "1-2 paragraphs" cap, the "..." tail in the collapsed state, and the broadened-but-narrower `.paragraph`-only visibility gate; AC-4.1 unchanged); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-130 appended after CL-129 with the three-refinement rationale, the live-API sampling result, the rejected alternatives, and the "..."-is-view-only invariant); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — extractor cap (commit 2):** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/ArticleBodyExtractor.swift` (`extractRecipeBlurb(html:)` → `extractRecipeBlurb(html:paragraphLimit:)` with default 2; new walk that tracks `<p>` open/close count and truncates at the Nth `</p>` close tag); `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/ArticleBodyExtractorTests.swift` (new L1 tests for the cap behavior — 5-paragraph fixture + limit 2, 1-paragraph fixture + limit 2, limit 0, mixed `[paragraph, heading, paragraph, image, paragraph]` + limit 2; existing tests pass unchanged since their fixtures have ≤2 paragraphs).
  - **Source — view-side "..." tail + visibility gate (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` (the collapsed-state `Text(strippedExcerpt)` becomes `Text(strippedExcerpt + "...")` — the "..." is only attached in the collapsed branch; the expanded branch is unchanged); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel.swift` (new `public var hasExpandableBlurb: Bool` computed property that pattern-matches `blurbBlocks` for a `.paragraph` case); same `RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` updates the visibility gates from `!viewModel.blurbBlocks.isEmpty` to `viewModel.hasExpandableBlurb` (both the outer conditional that decides whether to render the VStack at all and the inner conditional that gates the More/Less buttons).
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailViewModelBlurbTests.swift` (new L1 view-model tests for `hasExpandableBlurb` — returns `true` when `blurbBlocks` contains at least one `.paragraph`; `false` when `blurbBlocks` is empty; `false` when `blurbBlocks` contains only non-paragraph blocks). The existing `blurbBlocksPopulatedFromFreshFetchWithWPRMCard` test continues to pass — the 2-paragraph fixture is within the new default cap.
  - **Out of bounds:** `RecipeDetailMetaPills` (CL-129 fix 1 — unchanged); `strippingExcerptTruncationTail(from:)` pure helper (unchanged — still strips the six WP-source truncation markers; "..." is layered on top in the view); `ArticleBlocksView` (unchanged); `ArticleDetailView` (unchanged — articles render the full body, no paragraph cap); `RecipeServingsScaler`, `RecipeDetailHero`, `RecipeDetailQuickJump`, `RecipeDetailVideoSection`, `ingredientsSection`, `instructionsSection`, `RelatedRecipesStrip`, `RecipeDetailRatingsSection`, `cookNowSection`, the toolbar items (Save / Download / Share per AC-4.7 / AC-35.1 / AC-4.8), the snackbar surface (all byte-identical); `RecipeDetailViewModel`'s public surface other than the new `hasExpandableBlurb` computed property (unchanged — `blurbBlocks` type signature is `[ArticleBlock]`, cap is applied inside `fetchAndParse()` before assignment); `RecipeDetailDependencies` protocol (unchanged); `RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch.swift`'s call site updates the `extractRecipeBlurb` call to pass the default `paragraphLimit` parameter (one-call-site edit); article-classification + JSON-LD parse paths (unchanged).
- **AC:** US-4 amendment / AC-4.12 amended (strike-through + replacement framing on the "full" → "1-2 paragraphs" cap, the "..." tail in the collapsed state, the broadened-but-narrower `.paragraph`-only visibility gate). Pins AC-4.1 (unchanged), AC-4.2 + AC-4.3 (ingredients + instructions sections unchanged — the blurb still crops before the WPRM card before the cap is applied), AC-4.7 / AC-4.8 / AC-4.9 / AC-4.10 (Save / Share / offline / VoiceOver unchanged), US-37 / AC-37.3 (`ArticleDetailView` byte-identical — articles render the full body, the cap is recipe-blurb only), REG-33 (unchanged — the "..." is the app's visible cue, NOT the WP server-side `[…]` which is still stripped).

Spec-side touches `spec.md` / `clarifications.md` / `tasks.md`.

### T-737 — Comment POST: no-silent-fail + author-identity guard + on-device diagnostics (DUT-7, US-14 / AC-14.4, CL-134)

- **What:** Code-side hardening + diagnosis for the Urgent DUT-7 "comments are broken on the installed TestFlight build" report. **This is observability + defensive medicine layered on top of PR #87 (REG-27), NOT a confirmed root-cause fix** — the true cause is unconfirmed and several hypotheses are server-side (WordPress moderation/Akismet, Wordfence UA blocking) and cannot be fixed in app code. The owner must still verify on-device + in WP admin (see DUT-7 PR body). PR #87 already shipped two of the five hypotheses' mitigations (H3 User-Agent stamping; H1 category-specific snackbar that surfaces the bare HTTP status). T-737 closes the remaining code-side gaps. **(1) No silent failure — surface WordPress's own error reason (extends H1).** Pre-T-737 the snackbar's `.httpStatus(code)` branch showed only the numeric code; the `WPCommentsClient.postComment` non-2xx path threw away the response body. T-737 parses WP's REST error body (`{"code","message","data":{"status"}}`) on every non-2xx and threads it through a new additive `WPClientError.httpStatusWithBody(Int, message:)` case → the snackbar now reads "Couldn't post your comment (server said 403): <WP's reason>". A Wordfence-style HTML challenge body is tag-stripped + clamped to 140 chars so a security-plugin block still surfaces something recognizable. Bodyless / message-less errors fall back to the existing `.httpStatus(code)` (every other client — `WPRestClient`, `ImageLoader`, `RecipePageFetcher`, `WPRMRatingsClient` — stays byte-identical; only the comment path opts into body surfacing). **(2) Author-identity guard (hypothesis #4).** A fresh-install (or partially-saved) Keychain row can hand back an identity whose name or email is empty/whitespace — `GuestIdentityStore.load()` only nils a *missing* field, not an empty string. Posting that yields `author_email=""` → WP 400 → the "tap, nothing happens" report. T-737 adds two guards: the view model re-gates behind the US-15 guest-identity sheet + shows "Add your name and email to post a comment." (and trims whitespace off a valid identity before POSTing), and `WPCommentsClient.postComment` rejects an empty name/email with a typed error *before* the request fires (network-layer backstop, captured-request list proves nothing was sent). **(3) Structured on-device diagnostics.** `WPCommentsClient.postComment` now logs the request URL + post ID on send, the status + WP message on failure, and the status + comment id + WP moderation status on success — all under subsystem `com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp` / category `network`, privacy-safe (`.public` on URL/status/id only; NEVER the comment body or author PII). The owner foregrounds the TestFlight build with Console.app attached, posts a comment, and captures which branch fired to pin the actual hypothesis. **(4) ATS (hypothesis #5) — verified, no change.** `App/Info.plist` declares no `NSAppTransportSecurity` key, so the app uses Apple's default ATS (HTTPS allowed, cleartext HTTP forbidden). The endpoint is `https://www.dutchovendaddy.com/wp-json/...` → ATS does NOT block the POST (consistent with REG-27's H5 ruling). Documented caveat: if WP ever 301/302-redirects the POST to a non-HTTPS host the redirect would be blocked, but the canonical host is HTTPS-only today.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (new DUT-7 entry in the AC-T4 regression list under US-14 — the no-silent-fail body surfacing + the author-identity guard + the diagnostic log, explicitly flagged observability-not-root-cause); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-134 with the #87-already-shipped-vs-T-737-adds split, the server-side-vs-code-side hypothesis breakdown, and the owner-verification checklist); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — network layer (commit 2):** `Packages/DODNetworking/Sources/DODNetworking/WPClientError.swift` (new additive `httpStatusWithBody(Int, message:)` case — every existing `.httpStatus(_:)` site unchanged); `Packages/DODNetworking/Sources/DODNetworking/WPCommentsClient.swift` (parse WP error body on non-2xx via new `extractWPErrorMessage(from:)` + `stripHTML(_:)` helpers; empty-name/email guard before POST; `DODLog.network` send/success/failure breadcrumbs).
  - **Source — view model (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel.swift` (`comments` / `commentDraft` / `isSubmittingComment` setters relaxed `private(set)` → `internal(set)` for the extracted submit path); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel+CommentSubmit.swift` (NEW — `submitComment()` extracted from the parent file with the blank-identity guard + whitespace-trim before POST, keeping the parent under the 400-line cap); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel+CommentSnackbar.swift` (map the new `.httpStatusWithBody` case to "server said <code>: <message>").
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODNetworking/Tests/DODNetworkingTests/WPCommentsClientPostFailureTests.swift` (NEW — WP JSON error body surfaced as `httpStatusWithBody`; HTML challenge stripped; 401/403/422 bodyless fall back to `httpStatus`; empty email/name rejected before POST with captured-request proof; `extractWPErrorMessage` JSON/HTML/empty/blank/clamp unit coverage); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/CommentErrorSnackbarTests.swift` (new `httpStatusWithBody` snackbar test); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailCommentSubmitTests.swift` (NEW — blank-email re-gate-no-POST; whitespace-trim-then-post; server-error-message surfaced end-to-end) + `FakeRecipeDetailDependencies` gains an injectable `postCommentError`. All comment-write coverage stays at L1/L2 against `FakeHTTPClient` / `FakeRecipeDetailDependencies` — no write reaches the live blog (constitution §6).
  - **Out of bounds:** PR #87's `URLSessionHTTPClient` User-Agent stamping (REG-27 — unchanged, not duplicated); the four existing `WPClientError.httpStatus(_:)` call sites in `WPRestClient` / `ImageLoader` / `RecipePageFetcher` / `WPRMRatingsClient` (unchanged — they keep throwing the status-only case); `WPCommentsClient.postComment` signature + request body shape (REG-27 constraint — unchanged); `GuestIdentityStore` (unchanged — its partial-state-as-nil contract is correct; the empty-string case is guarded above it); `WPCommentsClient.comments` GET path (unchanged); `CommentComposer` view (unchanged — the composer already blocks empty submits client-side); `RecipeDetailViewModel` public read-only surface (unchanged — `comments` / `commentDraft` / `isSubmittingComment` stay publicly read-only, only the module-internal setter widened); the T-736 (CL-133) cache-hit blurb-refresh path (unchanged — orthogonal; T-737 touches only the comment-submit lines + property visibility); ATS / `App/Info.plist` (verified, intentionally unchanged).
- **AC:** US-14 / AC-14.4 (every comment-POST failure path — non-2xx with/without a WP body, network error, validation failure — produces a distinct, specific, user-visible message; success and failure are visibly distinct). Pins US-15 / AC-15.1 (blank identity re-gates behind the guest-identity sheet). New DUT-7 entry in the AC-T4 regression list. Explicitly observability + defensive hardening, not a confirmed root-cause fix — the H1..H5 hypothesis list stays open until the owner's on-device Console capture + WP-admin check pin the cause (which #87 may already have resolved).
- **Est:** ~75 min (spec/CL/tasks + network-layer commit + view-model commit + L1/L2 test additions + lint/format + simulator build + PR).

DUT-5 also has an open PR editing `DODFeatureRecipeDetail` — T-737 keeps its edits localized to the comment-submit path to minimize conflicts; a trivial rebase is fine.
- **||:** P24-comments-broken (DUT-7). Source-side near-conflict-free with in-flight branches — the only shared package with DUT-5 is `DODFeatureRecipeDetail`, and T-737 touches the comment-submit path + property visibility + two extension files, not the blurb/detail surfaces DUT-5 edits, and is orthogonal to T-736's cache-hit blurb-refresh path. **No `e2e` label** — comment-write coverage is deliberately held at L1/L2 (constitution §6: E2E must never write to the live blog).

### T-736 — Recipe blurb populates on cache-hit re-open via background refresh (US-4 amendment, CL-133)

- **What:** Close the gap where `viewModel.blurbBlocks` is view-local state that only ever populates via the fresh-fetch `fetchAndParse()` path, while `RecipeDetailViewModel.onAppear()` short-circuits to the cache-hit fast path on every re-open of a previously-cached recipe (`cached.hasDetail == true`). The cache-hit branch sets `loadState = .ready` and returns without touching `blurbBlocks`, so the view's empty-`blurbBlocks` fallback (`Text(strippedExcerpt)` with `DODColor.label` + `DODType.body` per T-734 / CL-131 lines 124-134) is what the user sees on second-and-onwards opens — visibly missing the "..." tail, the More button, and the inline bold/italic/link attribute runs that the rich `ArticleBlocksView` path provides. The user perceived this as "lots of recipes" missing the rich blurb (framed initially as a dark-mode bug, then verified by audit to be cache-path-specific and orthogonal to mode). T-736 audit (live API, 2026-05-31): the extractor + parser succeed on 100% of 50 newest, 100% of 50 oldest, 99% of 100 mid-catalog recipes — coverage is at-100% on the extractor side, so the gap is exclusively in the view-model cache-hit branch. The fix adds a new `refreshBlurbBlocks(forCanonicalURL:)` private helper on `RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch` that the cache-hit branch fires-and-forgets after `loadState = .ready`. The helper: (1) gates on `await dependencies.isOnline()` — offline cache-hits skip the network call (fallback path is acceptable when there's no fresh data); (2) `try?`s `dependencies.fetchHTML(for: canonicalURL)` — fail-silent on transient errors (no snackbar disturbs the already-rendered cached view); (3) runs the same `ArticleBodyExtractor.extractRecipeBlurb` + `ArticleHTMLParser.parse` pipeline `fetchAndParse` uses; (4) assigns `blurbBlocks` only if the result is non-empty (guard against a transient parse-failure downgrading a previously-successful population). Pattern matches `loadRatingsAndComments` cached-first-then-refresh shape. The view's `@Observable` binding picks up the assignment and re-renders the blurb surface from the fallback `Text` path to the rich `ArticleBlocksView` path with the full styling. The fallback path itself is unchanged — pre-T-736 already uses `DODColor.label` (T-734 / CL-131) so the dark-mode visual register was correct before T-736 too. The article cache-hit path (`cached.kind == .article`) is unchanged — articles already persist `articleBodyHTML` in `Recipe` and render rich on re-open without needing the refresh.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-4.12 amended via strike-through + replacement framing — the cache-hit `.recipe` branch now ALSO triggers a background blurb-blocks refresh via `refreshBlurbBlocks(forCanonicalURL:)`, gated on `isOnline()`, fail-silent on fetch error, non-empty-guarded on parse-result assignment); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-133 appended after CL-132 with the audit findings, the cache-vs-persistence decision rationale, the offline-skip + fail-silent + non-empty-guard sub-decisions, the rejected alternatives); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — view-model cache-hit refresh (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel.swift` (`onAppear()` cache-hit `.recipe` branch line 128-131 — after `loadState = .ready` + `loadRelated`, `await refreshBlurbBlocks(forCanonicalURL: canonicalURL)`); `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch.swift` (new `func refreshBlurbBlocks(forCanonicalURL:) async` private helper with the four-step contract above).
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailViewModelBlurbRefreshTests.swift` (NEW file) — five tests: (a) cache-hit + online + fetch succeeds → `blurbBlocks` populated post-`onAppear`; (b) cache-hit + offline → `blurbBlocks` stays empty (skip refresh); (c) cache-hit + online + fetch throws → `blurbBlocks` stays empty AND `snackbarMessage` unchanged (no user-visible error); (d) cache-hit + online + fetch returns HTML with zero `<p>` blocks → previously-set `blurbBlocks` is NOT overwritten (non-empty guard); (e) cache-miss → existing `fetchAndParse` path still populates `blurbBlocks` (sanity test).
  - **Out of bounds:** `extractRecipeBlurb` / `ArticleHTMLParser.parse` / `ArticleBlocksView` (unchanged — extractor coverage is at 100% per T-736 audit); `appendingEllipsisTail(to:)` / `collapsedBlurbBlocks(from:)` / `textOnlyBlurbBlocks(from:)` (T-734 / T-735 — unchanged); `RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` (unchanged — the empty-`blurbBlocks` fallback path stays exactly as T-734 / CL-131 / T-735 / CL-132 left it; the fix populates the array from outside the view); `RecipeDetailViewModel` public surface (unchanged — no new public properties; the new helper is internal); `RecipeDetailDependencies` protocol (unchanged — re-uses existing `fetchHTML` / `isOnline` methods); `Recipe` data model (unchanged — no new fields, no persistence change); `ArticleDetailView` (US-37 — unchanged); the fresh-fetch `fetchAndParse()` path (unchanged — no cache hit means no behavior change); the article cache-hit path `cached.kind == .article` line 132-138 (unchanged — articles already persist `articleBodyHTML`).
- **AC:** US-4 amendment / AC-4.12 amended (the cache-hit branch of `onAppear()` for `cached.kind == .recipe` now ALSO triggers a background blurb-blocks refresh; the view re-renders from the fallback `Text` path to the rich `ArticleBlocksView` path on re-open). Pins AC-4.1 (unchanged), AC-4.2 + AC-4.3 (unchanged), AC-4.7 / AC-4.8 / AC-4.9 / AC-4.10 (Save / Share / offline / VoiceOver unchanged — offline cache hit still renders cached recipe content), US-37 / AC-37.3 (`ArticleDetailView` byte-identical), REG-33 (unchanged — the cache-hit refresh is additive to the existing contract).

### T-735 — Recipe blurb: filter images + broaden extractor coverage (US-4 amendment, CL-132)

- **What:** Two coordinated fixes to the T-732 / T-733 / T-734 recipe-blurb-expansion surface that just shipped. **(1) Filter `.image` cases out of the blurb-rendering path** so the collapsed and expanded surfaces render TEXT ONLY — no Pinterest-preview / lazy-load placeholder photos that the production HTML interleaves with intro paragraphs. Live-API audit on 2026-05-31 of 11 production recipes (`?p=17615, 21238, 23034, 274, 5016, 736, 524, 23406, 19319, 21869, 18342`) found 3 recipes (`?p=274, 5016, 524`) where the parser returns image-first block sequences — `[image, image, paragraph, paragraph]`, `[image, paragraph, paragraph]`, and `[image, image, paragraph, image, list, paragraph]` respectively — so the pre-T-735 `collapsedBlurbBlocks(from:)` helper (which does `blocks.first`) was rendering an opaque Pinterest social image where the user expected the first intro paragraph + "..." + "More". The user's stated brief: "blurb is a narrative intro peek, not a media gallery." The fix is a view-level filter (new `textOnlyBlurbBlocks` computed property on `RecipeDetailView` + a `static func textOnlyBlurbBlocks(from:)` testable helper) that the collapsed render, the expanded render, and the More-button visibility gate ALL consume — a blurb whose only blocks are images correctly hides the More button (nothing text-y to expand into). The extractor and view-model stay structurally faithful (still return the full block sequence including images for any future consumer); the filter is view-side. `ArticleDetailView` is NOT touched — articles still render `.image` blocks per AC-37.3. **(2) Broaden the extractor's WPRM-boundary detection** so recipes using Tasty Recipes / Meal Vista / `wp-block-tasty-recipes-recipe-card` plugins instead of WPRM also get a clean pre-card crop. New helper `sliceBeforeRecipeCard(in:)` on `ArticleBodyExtractor` scans `<div ...>` tags for any of four boundary class tokens — `wprm-recipe-container` first (highest priority — the dominant case on `dutchovendaddy.com`), then `tasty-recipes`, `mv-create`, `wp-block-tasty-recipes-recipe-card` — and crops at the FIRST match. The existing `sliceBeforeWPRMCard` becomes a thin alias that delegates to the broadened helper (one inline call site, unchanged signature). Live-API audit found ZERO recipes in the sample use Tasty / Meal Vista today; the broadening is defensive, anticipating future plugin migrations or guest-post recipes.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-4.12 amended via strike-through + replacement framing — the blurb-rendering path filters `.image` blocks; the extractor's boundary detection broadens to Tasty / MV / wp-block-tasty as additional crop boundaries beyond WPRM); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-132 appended after CL-131 with the audit findings, the view-level vs extractor-level filter decision, the rejected alternatives, and the additive REG-33-stays-unchanged note); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — view-side image filter (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` (new `static func textOnlyBlurbBlocks(from: [ArticleBlock]) -> [ArticleBlock]` that admits `.paragraph` / `.heading` / `.list` and drops `.image`; new `textOnlyBlurbBlocks: [ArticleBlock]` computed property on the same extension that calls the static helper with `viewModel.blurbBlocks`; the `excerptText` body's three call sites that previously read `viewModel.blurbBlocks` and `viewModel.hasExpandableBlurb` swap to the filtered set and a local `hasExpandableTextBlurb` computed property). `ArticleDetailView` is NOT touched.
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailBlurbStylingTests.swift` (extend with three new tests for `textOnlyBlurbBlocks(from:)` — mixed `[paragraph, image, paragraph]` → `[paragraph, paragraph]`; image-only input → `[]`; empty input → `[]`).
  - **Source — extractor coverage broadening (commit 3):** `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/ArticleBodyExtractor.swift` (new `static func sliceBeforeRecipeCard(in:)` helper that scans for the four boundary class tokens in priority order; existing `sliceBeforeWPRMCard` delegates to the new helper for source-compatibility).
  - **Tests (commit 3):** `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/ArticleBodyExtractorTests.swift` (new `extractRecipeBlurbCropsAtTastyRecipesBoundary` test fixture — a recipe page with `<div class="tasty-recipes ...">` as the card boundary returns the pre-card slice; existing WPRM-boundary tests pass unchanged because WPRM remains the highest-priority boundary token).
  - **Out of bounds:** `extractRecipeBlurb(html:paragraphLimit:)` signature (T-733 / CL-130 — unchanged); `cappingAtParagraphLimit` (T-733 / CL-130 — unchanged); `extract` / `extractContentHTML` (US-37 — unchanged; articles still render images); `appendingEllipsisTail(to:)` / `collapsedBlurbBlocks(from:)` (T-734 / CL-131 — unchanged); `hasExpandableBlurb` computed property on `RecipeDetailViewModel` (T-733 / CL-130 — unchanged; the view-side now uses a local `hasExpandableTextBlurb` on the filtered set, the view-model property stays for any other consumer); `strippingExcerptTruncationTail(from:)` pure helper (CL-129 — unchanged); `ArticleBlocksView` (T-732 / CL-129 — unchanged); `ArticleDetailView` (unchanged — articles still render images); `RecipeDetailMetaPills` (CL-129 fix 1 — unchanged); `RecipeServingsScaler`, `RecipeDetailHero`, `RecipeDetailQuickJump`, `RecipeDetailVideoSection`, `ingredientsSection`, `instructionsSection`, `RelatedRecipesStrip`, `RecipeDetailRatingsSection`, `cookNowSection`, the toolbar items (Save / Download / Share per AC-4.7 / AC-35.1 / AC-4.8), the snackbar surface (all byte-identical); `RecipeDetailViewModel` public surface (unchanged); `RecipeDetailDependencies` protocol (unchanged); `RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch.swift` (unchanged).
- **AC:** US-4 amendment / AC-4.12 amended (the blurb-rendering path filters `.image` blocks; the More-button visibility gate operates on the filtered set; the extractor's boundary detection broadens to Tasty / MV / wp-block-tasty as additional crop boundaries beyond WPRM). Pins AC-4.1 (unchanged), AC-4.2 + AC-4.3 (ingredients + instructions sections unchanged), AC-4.7 / AC-4.8 / AC-4.9 / AC-4.10 (Save / Share / offline / VoiceOver unchanged), US-37 / AC-37.3 (`ArticleDetailView` byte-identical — still renders images), REG-33 (unchanged — the visual changes are additive to the existing contract).

### T-734 — Unify recipe blurb styling across collapsed + expanded states (US-4 amendment, CL-131)

- **What:** Close the visual-consistency gap between the T-732 / CL-129 + T-733 / CL-130 collapsed and expanded blurb states on `RecipeDetailView`. Pre-T-734 the collapsed surface rendered `Text(strippedExcerpt + "...")` (plain `String` from the WP excerpt) with `.foregroundStyle(DODColor.labelSecondary)` (lighter gray, no bold/italic/link styling) while the expanded surface rendered `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: viewModel.blurbBlocks)` with `.foregroundStyle(DODColor.label)` (full label color, with bold/italic/`AttributedString`-link styling per the parser). Tapping "More" shifted both the color AND the inline-attribute treatment — visually inconsistent across the same content surface. **The fix — single source-file change in `RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift`.** When `viewModel.hasExpandableBlurb == true` (dominant case — `blurbBlocks` carries at least one `.paragraph`), the collapsed branch renders the first paragraph block from `viewModel.blurbBlocks` via `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: [firstBlockWithEllipsisTail])` — same view, same per-block styling, same `DODColor.label` foreground, same inline bold / italic / link treatment as the expanded surface. The `"..."` tail (T-733's view-only cue) attaches inline to the first paragraph's `AttributedString` via a new pure helper — `appendingEllipsisTail(to: ArticleBlock) -> ArticleBlock` — that pattern-matches `.paragraph(AttributedString)`, mutates a copy via `append(AttributedString("..."))` (preserves inline-attribute runs for the prose before the appended text; the appended `"..."` carries no attributes), and returns `.paragraph(updated)`. The expanded branch is unchanged — `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: viewModel.blurbBlocks)` already renders all 1-2 paragraphs via the T-733-capped extractor output, no "..." tail. **Empty-`blurbBlocks` fallback (the rare case — recipes whose pre-WPRM region has no `<p>` blocks, e.g. recipe 17615 per the CL-130 live-API note).** When `blurbBlocks` is empty, `hasExpandableBlurb` is `false`, no More button is rendered, and the WP excerpt is the only blurb surface. T-734 renders that fallback as `Text(strippedExcerpt)` with `.dodFont(DODType.body)` and `.foregroundStyle(DODColor.label)` (full label color matching `ArticleBlocksView`'s `.paragraph` treatment) — the lighter `.labelSecondary` is removed from the blurb-rendering path entirely. No `"..."` tail (nothing to expand into). **Deliberate consequence:** the collapsed surface now shows the full first paragraph from `blurbBlocks[0]` (typically longer than the WP `the_excerpt()` snip). The user accepted this trade in the brief — visual consistency wins over collapsed-state brevity.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-4.12 amended via strike-through + replacement framing — the collapsed surface's content source becomes the first `blurbBlocks` paragraph via `ArticleBlocksView` when `hasExpandableBlurb == true`; fallback path renders WP excerpt with `.label` not `.labelSecondary`); `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (CL-131 appended after CL-130 with the styling-unification rationale, the inline-vs-separate "..." placement decision, the first-paragraph-vs-first-N-sentences rationale, the rejected alternatives, and the deliberate longer-collapsed-text consequence); `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source — view-side styling unification (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailView+Blurb.swift` (the collapsed branch's `Text(strippedExcerpt + "...")` → `ArticleBlocksView(blocks: collapsedBlurbBlocks)` when `hasExpandableBlurb == true`; new static helper `appendingEllipsisTail(to: ArticleBlock) -> ArticleBlock` that mutates a `.paragraph`'s `AttributedString` via `append(AttributedString("..."))`; new static helper `collapsedBlurbBlocks(from: [ArticleBlock]) -> [ArticleBlock]` that returns the first block of the input with the ellipsis appended, or `[]` if input is empty; the fallback `Text(strippedExcerpt)` path swaps `.foregroundStyle(DODColor.labelSecondary)` → `.foregroundStyle(DODColor.label)`).
  - **Tests (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailBlurbStylingTests.swift` (new — L1 helper coverage: `appendingEllipsisTail(to:)` with a `.paragraph` containing no inline attributes returns a `.paragraph` whose `AttributedString` ends in `"..."`; with a `.paragraph` containing a bold run mid-string, the bold run is preserved and the `"..."` is plain; `collapsedBlurbBlocks(from:)` with empty input returns `[]`; with a single-paragraph input returns one block whose text ends in `"..."`; with a multi-paragraph input returns one block (the first) whose text ends in `"..."`). The existing `RecipeDetailViewModelBlurbTests`, `RecipeDetailExcerptStripTests`, `RecipeDetailViewSnapshotTests` continue to pass; the snapshot baselines are still empty per the existing `__Snapshots__/.gitkeep` so the L4 surface re-records on next run via the existing `record: .missing` workflow.
  - **Out of bounds:** `extractRecipeBlurb(html:paragraphLimit:)` (T-733 / CL-130 — unchanged); `hasExpandableBlurb` computed property (T-733 / CL-130 — unchanged); `strippingExcerptTruncationTail(from:)` pure helper (CL-129 — unchanged); `ArticleBlocksView` (T-732 / CL-129 — unchanged); `ArticleDetailView` (unchanged — articles already use the rich-block path); `RecipeDetailMetaPills` (CL-129 fix 1 — unchanged); `RecipeServingsScaler`, `RecipeDetailHero`, `RecipeDetailQuickJump`, `RecipeDetailVideoSection`, `ingredientsSection`, `instructionsSection`, `RelatedRecipesStrip`, `RecipeDetailRatingsSection`, `cookNowSection`, the toolbar items (Save / Download / Share per AC-4.7 / AC-35.1 / AC-4.8), the snackbar surface (all byte-identical); `RecipeDetailViewModel` public surface (unchanged — no new properties, the rendering decision is view-layer-only); `RecipeDetailDependencies` protocol (unchanged); `RecipeDetailViewModel+Fetch.swift` (unchanged — the existing `blurbBlocks` population is reused).
- **AC:** US-4 amendment / AC-4.12 amended (strike-through + replacement framing on the collapsed surface's content source — now the first `blurbBlocks` paragraph via `ArticleBlocksView` when `hasExpandableBlurb == true`; fallback path's color swap from `.labelSecondary` to `.label`). Pins AC-4.1 (unchanged), AC-4.2 + AC-4.3 (ingredients + instructions sections unchanged), AC-4.7 / AC-4.8 / AC-4.9 / AC-4.10 (Save / Share / offline / VoiceOver unchanged), US-37 / AC-37.3 (`ArticleDetailView` byte-identical), REG-33 (unchanged — the visual unification is additive to the existing More/Less + WP-truncation-strip contract).
- **Est:** ~30 min (spec amendment + single source-file commit + L1 helper test additions + verify).

## Phase 19 — CloudKit recipe sync fix (DUT-6, 2026-05-31)

Linear issue **DUT-6 "CloudKit recipe sync does not work between iPhone and iPad"** (Urgent). Triage found four root causes; only two are code-fixable, and the dominant one is owner-side. This phase lands T-736 (the code fix + diagnosability + documentation). Branch `fix/dut-6-cloudkit-sync`; cluster owner: dad. The four causes, and where each is addressed:
1. **[OWNER-SIDE, top suspect — not code-fixable]** The CloudKit schema was likely never deployed to the **Production** environment. `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer` auto-creates record types only in the **Development** environment; TestFlight / App Store builds run against Production, which never auto-creates them, so the mirror's import/export fail with "Did not find any record types". Fixable only by the owner in the CloudKit Console. The code must degrade cleanly (no crash); the deploy steps are documented in the PR + below.
2. **[CODE — primary fix, T-736]** The iCloud Sync toggle did not re-wire the live store. `AppDependencies.settingsDependencies()` rebuilt the `ModelContainer` and reassigned `modelContainer`, but the live `RecipeStore` (a `@ModelActor`, container fixed at init) was never rebuilt, no `ModelContainer` is injected into the SwiftUI environment, and SwiftData can't hot-swap a container's CloudKit configuration mid-process — so the reassignment re-wired nothing and only added a transient second `NSPersistentCloudKitContainer` (a DOD-CRASH-1 risk surface). Sync therefore only engaged on the next cold launch anyway.
3. **[ENTITLEMENT — documented, T-736]** No `aps-environment` (Push Notifications capability), so CloudKit live-change pushes aren't delivered; the other device only pulls on its next launch. The signing-neutral half (`UIBackgroundModes: [remote-notification]`) is already present (T-701/T-702). The `aps-environment` entitlement requires Apple-Developer-portal provisioning the App ID doesn't yet have — adding it to the entitlements file would break the signed / CD build — so it is documented for the owner, not added.
4. **[EXPECTATION — documented, T-736]** Opt-in is off by default and needs the same iCloud account on both devices.

### T-736 — DUT-6 CloudKit sync: hardened launch-only opt-in + crash-safe container + sync status surface — ✅ shipped 2026-05-31

- **Approach for cause #2: hardened launch-only (NOT a live mid-session re-wire), because a live re-wire is not cleanly feasible and is unsafe.** `RecipeStore` is a `@ModelActor` whose `ModelContext`/`ModelContainer` is bound at init with no supported swap API; every feature view-model holds that same `store` reference; and the app injects **no** `ModelContainer` into the SwiftUI environment (no `.modelContainer()` / `@Query` / `@Environment(\.modelContext)` in production code — verified), so even rebuilding `AppDependencies.modelContainer` re-wires nothing. A partial live re-wire would also reopen a `.private` container at an arbitrary moment — exactly the DOD-CRASH-1 hazard. So the persisted opt-in flag (`dod.cloudkit.syncOptInV1`) is made the **single source of truth, read at launch**, the toggle's only job is to persist it (and tell the user a relaunch applies it), and the container is built deterministically from the flag at the next launch.
- **What shipped — code (all in the CloudKit/sync paths to avoid colliding with the concurrent DODFeatureFeed Voice-section work).**
  - **`RecipeStore+Containers.swift`:** new `cloudKitSyncOptIn(in:)` single-source flag reader; new `productionContainer(defaults:inMemory:) -> ContainerBuildResult` that builds the container to match the flag **with a DOD-CRASH-1 safety net** — on the opt-in path, if the CloudKit `.private` open throws (undeployed Production schema / unavailable account / PushKit failure), it **falls back to a plain local container** (`usedCloudKitFallback == true`) instead of letting the error reach `AppDependencies.init`'s `fatalError`; the opt-out path still rethrows a genuine migration failure (MIGRATION.md rule 4). The fallback branch is extracted as `buildCloudKitWithFallback(cloudKitBuild:localBuild:)` so it is unit-testable by injection (a real `.private` open can't be made to throw hermetically without entitlements). `makeProductionConfiguration()` + `recreateContainerAfterOptInChange()` retained for back-compat; the latter's doc now states it is **not** used for mid-session swapping.
  - **`CloudKitSyncStatus.swift` (new, DODPersistence):** a pure `Sendable` coarse status (`off` / `relaunchPending` / `syncing` / `idle` / `error`) with a pinned `displayString` and an `init(event: CloudKitSyncEventSummary)` mapping — the minimal cause-B surface, formatting-pure so it is testable without a live CloudKit account.
  - **`AppDependencies.swift`:** builds the container via the crash-safe `productionContainer(defaults:)`, records `usedCloudKitFallback`, logs an explicit "sync ON but mirror never engaged — most likely the Production schema was never deployed" error from `bootstrap()` when the fallback fired; reads the opt-in via the single-source reader; `modelContainer` is now a `let` (built once, never swapped). **`settingsDependencies()` no longer rebuilds the container** — it persists the flag + fires the `syncEnabled`/`syncDisabled` analytics (unchanged single dispatch seam) and exposes the diagnostics' latest status to the Settings row.
  - **`CloudKitSyncDiagnostics.swift`:** now also tracks `latestStatus: CloudKitSyncStatus` from each observed mirror event (cause B), alongside the existing logging.
  - **`SettingsDependencies` + `SettingsViewModel` (DODFeatureFeed):** `setCloudSyncOptIn` documented as flag-write-only; new `currentCloudSyncStatus()` seam (default `.off`); `SettingsViewModel.cloudSyncStatus` + `updateCloudSyncStatus(_:)` + `refreshCloudSyncStatus()`; `cloudSyncStatusText` now renders `relaunchPending` (wins) else the mapped `cloudSyncStatus.displayString` (Idle / Syncing… / Sync error), preserving the prior "Idle" / "Relaunch DOD to apply" copy the existing L1 tests pin. `SettingsView` pulls the status on appear via `.task`.
- **What shipped — tests (L1, all green).** DODPersistence 76 (was 63): new `CloudKitContainerSelectionTests` (flag-as-source-of-truth; opt-out config never touches CloudKit; opt-out build has no fallback; injected CloudKit-build failure ⇒ local fallback; success ⇒ no fallback) + `CloudKitSyncStatusTests` (displayString + event mapping). DODFeatureFeed 43 (was 39): new `SettingsViewModelCloudSyncStatusTests` (status→sublabel mapping; relaunch-pending override; refresh-from-dependency; refresh skipped while pending). Existing DOD-CRASH-1 `CloudKitSchemaCompatibilityTests` + `SchemaV4Tests` + the prior SettingsViewModel cloud-sync tests stay green.
- **What remains OWNER-SIDE-BLOCKED (cannot be fixed in code).**
  - **Schema deploy (cause #1 — the most likely reason TestFlight sync fails):** run a **Debug** build on a device/sim signed into iCloud, turn iCloud Sync ON in Settings, relaunch, save a recipe (this auto-creates the `CD_*` record types in the **Development** CloudKit environment). Then CloudKit Console (`icloud.developer.apple.com` → container `iCloud.com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp`) → **Deploy Schema Changes…** → promote Development → **Production**. Re-test on the TestFlight build. Until this is done, the app runs on the crash-safe local fallback (saved recipes stay on-device; no crash) and the device log shows the "Production schema never deployed" hint.
  - **Live push (cause #3 — optional, only if instant cross-device propagation is wanted):** in the Apple Developer portal enable **Push Notifications** on App ID `com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp`, regenerate the provisioning profile, then add `aps-environment` to `App/DODApp.entitlements`. NOT done here because it requires portal provisioning the App ID lacks and would break the signed / CD build. Without it, sync still works — the other device pulls on its next launch / foreground instead of instantly.
- **Files:** `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/{RecipeStore+Containers.swift, CloudKitSyncStatus.swift (new)}`, `Packages/DODPersistence/Tests/DODPersistenceTests/{CloudKitContainerSelectionTests.swift (new), CloudKitSyncStatusTests.swift (new)}`, `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/{SettingsDependencies.swift, SettingsViewModel.swift, SettingsViewModel+CloudSync.swift, SettingsView.swift}`, `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Tests/DODFeatureFeedTests/{SettingsViewModelTests.swift, SettingsViewModelCloudSyncStatusTests.swift (new)}`, `App/{AppDependencies.swift, CloudKitSyncDiagnostics.swift}`, `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry). **No entitlements / `project.yml` / Info.plist changes** (signing-neutral by design).
- **AC:** Fixes cause #2 (the flag is the single launch-time source of truth; the container is built to match; no mid-session no-op rebuild). Preserves DOD-CRASH-1 (the opt-in `.private` open can never crash the launch — it degrades to local) with the prior tests green + new fallback coverage. Adds cause-B diagnosability (Idle / Syncing… / Sync error in the existing status row). Documents causes #1 + #3 + #4 for the owner. Pins REG-25 / AC-41.1 (opt-out never touches CloudKit).
- **Verification:** `swift test` green on DODPersistence (76) + DODFeatureFeed (43); `bin/format.sh` + `bin/lint.sh --strict` clean (0 violations); `xcodegen generate` + `xcodebuild -scheme DODApp -destination 'iOS Simulator,iPhone 17' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED**; regenerated `Info.plist` still carries `remote-notification`; entitlements unchanged.
- **Est:** ~4h (container hardening + status surface + L1 coverage + owner-step docs).
- **Deps:** main at T-735 / CL-132. Built on Phase 15 (T-700..T-708). Source-localized to the CloudKit/sync code paths + DODPersistence container factory; a trivial rebase against the concurrent DODFeatureFeed Voice-section branch is expected (different region of `SettingsView`/`SettingsViewModel`).
- **||:** P19-cloudkit-sync-fix (single task). **`e2e` label NOT required** — the change is L1-covered, behind the off-by-default opt-in, and signing-neutral; the on-device sync round-trip is owner-validated after the schema deploy.

## Phase 21 — Push entitlement for instant CloudKit cross-device sync (DUT-6 cause #3, 2026-06-01)

DUT-6 cause #3: with no `aps-environment` (Push Notifications capability), CloudKit live-change pushes (`NSPersistentCloudKitContainer`'s `CKDatabaseSubscription`) are not delivered, so the other device only updates on its next launch/foreground instead of instantly. The signing-neutral half (`UIBackgroundModes: [remote-notification]`) shipped in T-701/T-702. T-736 (Phase 19) documented adding the entitlement as owner-side-blocked because it requires Developer-portal provisioning the App ID does not yet have. This phase lands the code change behind a **DRAFT** PR so it is ready the moment the owner enables Push on the App ID. Branch `feat/push-entitlement`.

### T-740 — DUT-6 Push entitlement: add aps-environment + wire the capability (DRAFT until App ID provisioned) — code-complete 2026-06-01

- **What shipped — `App/DODApp.entitlements`.** Added the Push Notifications capability key `aps-environment` with value `development`. The value is `development` on purpose: it is what Xcode's Push capability writes for the everyday Debug/device-dev state, and Xcode **automatically rewrites it to `production` when archiving for App Store / TestFlight** (a distribution provisioning profile's `aps-environment` allowlist always contains `production`). So the single committed value is correct for BOTH Debug/simulator and the Release-archive CD (which uses automatic signing) — hardcoding `production` would give local/dev-signed builds the wrong APNs environment. Researched + confirmed against Apple's `aps-environment` docs + the known Xcode archive-time override behavior.
- **What shipped — `project.yml`.** Documented the Push capability at the App target's `CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS: App/DODApp.entitlements` line. This repo drives all capabilities (iCloud/CloudKit, App Groups, now Push) purely through that entitlements file — xcodegen has no first-class capability block, and automatic signing + `-allowProvisioningUpdates` provision whatever the entitlements file declares. The comment mirrors the existing `UIBackgroundModes` documentation so the wiring is discoverable in `project.yml`.
- **Why DRAFT / DO NOT MERGE yet.** Automatic signing cannot provision `aps-environment` for an App ID that lacks the Push Notifications capability. Merging before the owner enables Push on App ID `com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp` (Developer portal → Identifiers → the App ID → Push Notifications → save) and the provisioning profile is regenerated would break the TestFlight CD at the signing step. The PR is opened as a draft with a prominent top-of-body warning + the owner steps.
- **What remains OWNER-SIDE (the unblock, one time).** Developer portal → Identifiers → App ID `com.dutchovendaddy.DODApp` → enable **Push Notifications** → save → regenerate the provisioning profile (automatic signing refreshes it on the next archive). Then mark the draft ready and merge. After merge, the next release carries `aps-environment` (upgraded to `production` at archive time) and CloudKit pushes are delivered, so the other device syncs instantly.
- **Files:** `App/DODApp.entitlements` (add `aps-environment`), `project.yml` (document the capability at `CODE_SIGN_ENTITLEMENTS`), `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry). **No Swift / Info.plist changes** — the `remote-notification` background mode is already present; this is purely the signing/entitlement half.
- **AC:** Completes DUT-6 cause #3 in code (the entitlement is present + provisioned by automatic signing). Keeps the existing `UIBackgroundModes: [remote-notification]`. Gated behind a draft PR so the signed/CD build is never broken before the App ID is provisioned.
- **Verification:** `plutil -lint App/DODApp.entitlements` → OK; `aps-environment` = `development` confirmed via PlistBuddy. `xcodegen generate` clean. `xcodebuild -scheme DODApp -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17' -derivedDataPath /tmp/dd-push CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED** (simulator does not enforce entitlements, so this passes even though distribution signing would require the App ID capability). `bin/lint.sh` (0 violations) + `bin/format.sh` clean; no Swift touched.
- **Est:** ~1h (research correct aps value + entitlement + project.yml doc + simulator build verify).
- **Deps:** main at T-736 / CL-133. Builds on Phase 19 T-736 (documented this as cause #3) and Phase 15 T-701/T-702 (the `remote-notification` half). Touches only `App/DODApp.entitlements` + `project.yml` — no source-side collision with any phase or in-flight branch.
- **||:** P21-push-entitlement (single task). **`e2e` label NOT required** — no app code; an entitlement + signing change verified by the simulator build. **DRAFT — DO NOT MERGE until the App ID has Push Notifications enabled in the Developer portal and the profile is regenerated.**

## Phase 23 — User profile Phase b (photo picker + crop + render, DUT-36, 2026-06-02)

Linear issue **DUT-36 "User profile + gated write surfaces"** (Phase b of 4). Phase a (T-739, merged `c09c898`) shipped the data model + Settings → Profile section + edit view + Sign Out + Delete Profile + initial-letter avatar. Phase b wires the Profile Picture row end-to-end: `PhotosPicker` (privacy-preserving — no `NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription`) → custom SwiftUI square crop → `ProfilePhotoStore` (Documents/JPG at 512×512 @ 0.85) → `ProfilePhotoView` loads + renders with `InitialLetterAvatarView` fallback. Phases c (Ratings gate) and d (comment composer auto-fill) ship in separate PRs.

### T-740 — DUT-36 Phase b: photo picker + crop + render (US-44 amendment, CL-137)

- **What:** Phase b of DUT-36's four-PR delivery. Lands the new ``ProfilePhotoStore`` actor (Documents-directory JPG persistence at 512×512 @ 0.85 JPEG quality, UUID-keyed filename `profile-photo-<UUID().uuidString>.jpg`), the ``ProfilePhotoPicker`` SwiftUI wrapper around `PhotosPicker` (`matching: .images`, `photoLibrary: .shared()` — privacy-preserving, no `NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription` Info.plist entry needed), the custom ``ProfilePhotoCropView`` SwiftUI square crop modal (GeometryReader-sized container + `MagnificationGesture` 1.0..4.0 + `DragGesture` pan + dimmed overlay outside the square crop + Done/Cancel toolbar; Done renders the cropped image via `UIGraphicsImageRenderer` and hands a `UIImage` back through a callback), the pure `cropRect(for:)` transform-math helper (so the L1 test suite pins the math without a view host), the updated ``ProfilePhotoView`` (loads from `ProfilePhotoStore.load(filename:)` in `.task`; caches the `UIImage` in `@State`; falls back to ``InitialLetterAvatarView`` when `photoFilename` is nil OR the file is missing — graceful degradation), the ``ProfileEditView`` wire-up (Profile Picture row tap → picker when no photo, confirmation dialog with Replace / Remove / Cancel when a photo exists), and the ``ProfileStoring/clear()`` amendment to also delete the on-disk photo file when the current profile carries a `photoFilename` (Sign Out + Delete Profile leave both Keychain + Documents in the same clean state — no orphan JPGs). The Settings Profile section header renders via ``ProfilePhotoView`` (was `InitialLetterAvatarView` directly) so the uploaded photo surfaces in Settings when one exists.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (US-44 amendment — AC-44.3 amended via strike-through + replacement framing to wire `PhotosPicker` + custom crop + `ProfilePhotoStore` + `ProfilePhotoView`; AC-44.8 added — Replace + Remove dialog when a photo exists; AC-44.9 added — Sign Out + Delete Profile also clear the photo file). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-137 entry — the locked architecture decisions, the privacy-preserving picker rationale, the 512×512 JPG @ 0.85 quality output, the UUID-keyed Documents-directory filename, the Replace + Remove dialog pattern, the Sign Out + Delete Profile cleanup path, the considered-and-rejected `UIImagePickerController(allowsEditing:)` / Mantis / BSImagePicker / PNG / fixed-filename / Keychain-blob / synchronous-disk-IO / gesture-snapshot alternatives). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry). `specs/dod-ios-app/backlog.md` is NOT touched.
  - **New source files (commits 2-3):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoStore.swift` (the `ProfilePhotoStoring` protocol + `ProfilePhotoStore` actor with `save(_:) async throws -> String` / `load(filename:) async -> UIImage?` / `clear(filename:) async throws`; `InMemoryProfilePhotoStore` actor for tests; UIKit-only — `#if canImport(UIKit)` gates the implementation). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoCropView.swift` (the custom square crop modal + the pure `cropRect(for:)` transform-math helper exposed `static` for the L1 test surface; UIKit-only). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoPicker.swift` (the SwiftUI wrapper around `PhotosPicker`; binds `selectedItem: PhotosPickerItem?` and surfaces a labeled button; UIKit-only).
  - **Source edits (commit 3):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoView.swift` (replace the Phase a always-fall-through stub with a `.task`-driven `ProfilePhotoStore.load(filename:)` + `@State private var loadedImage: UIImage?` cache + the `Image(uiImage:)` render in a `Circle()` clip; falls back to `InitialLetterAvatarView` when filename is nil OR the file is missing). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileEditView.swift` (replace the Phase a stub photo row with the picker + crop wire-up: `@State private var selectedItem: PhotosPickerItem?` / `@State private var croppingImage: UIImage?` / `@State private var photoActionDialog: Bool` / `@State private var inFlightPhotoFilename: String?`; tap when no photo → `PhotosPicker` directly; tap when photo exists → confirmation dialog; `.onChange(of: selectedItem)` loads transferable data + creates a `UIImage` + assigns to `croppingImage`; `.sheet(item: croppingImage)` presents `ProfilePhotoCropView`; Done callback saves via `ProfilePhotoStore.save(_:)` + updates `inFlightPhotoFilename` + clears old file if Replace flow). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileStore.swift` (gain a `ProfilePhotoStoring?` collaborator on `KeychainProfileStore` + `InMemoryProfileStore`; amend `clear()` to read the existing profile pre-clear, call `photoStore?.clear(filename:)` on its `photoFilename` if present, then clear the Keychain row).
  - **Wiring (commit 3):** `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/SettingsView.swift` (the existing ProfileSection populated-state header already routes through `ProfilePhotoView` via the section's own composition — no SettingsView edit needed; the `ProfileSection` populated row already uses `ProfilePhotoView(profile: profile, diameter: 60)` so it picks up the photo automatically). `App/AppDependencies.swift` (instantiate one `ProfilePhotoStore` per process + pass it into the `KeychainProfileStore` via the new `photoStore:` init parameter).

Pins the Phase a contracts unchanged (Profile section placement, required Display Name + Email, Sign Out + Delete Profile semantics, initial-letter default avatar, US-15 UUID bridge, no retroactive attribution).

## Phase 24 — User profile Phase c (Ratings & Reviews gate + return-to-recipe modal, DUT-36, 2026-06-02)

Linear issue **DUT-36 "User profile + gated write surfaces"** (Phase c of 4). Phase a (T-739, merged `c09c898`) + Phase b (T-740, merged `905eeed`) shipped the data model + Settings → Profile section + edit view + Sign Out + Delete Profile + photo picker + crop + render. Phase c gates the Ratings & Reviews WRITE composer (star picker + review text box + submit) on profile existence: blur + popup overlay when no profile exists; the popup CTA presents `ProfileEditView` as a modal sheet OVER the recipe (recipe stays mounted, scroll preserved); on sheet dismiss the `@Observable` re-render reactively ungates. Phase d (T-742, comment composer auto-fill) ships separately.

## Phase 25 — User profile Phase d (composer auto-fill + own-comment profile photo, DUT-36, 2026-06-02)

Linear issue **DUT-36 "User profile + gated write surfaces"** (Phase d of 4 — FINAL — closes DUT-36 on merge). Phases a/b/c (T-739 / T-740 / T-741 — merged `c09c898` / `905eeed` / `5ac5f5e`) shipped the profile data model + Settings section + edit view + Sign Out + Delete Profile + photo picker + crop + render + Ratings & Reviews profile gate. Phase d removes the redundant Display Name + Email TextField inputs from the rating/review/comment composer (replaces them with a static `PostingAsHeader` showing the user's profile identity above the empty comment editor) and renders the user's profile photo on their own comment rows via a case-insensitive `authorEmail == profile.email` match. The WP REST POST payload is unchanged in shape; only the SOURCE of `author_name` + `author_email` changes from user-input to profile-read.
