Removes the editable Display Name + Email TextField rows (`authorFields` builder + `AuthorFieldKind` / `AuthorFieldSpec` helpers in `RecipeDetailRatingsSection+AuthorFields.swift`, deleted in full) from the rating/review/comment composer per user-spec ("just strings of text above the comment field that is empty and ready for input"). Adds `RecipeComment.authorEmail: String` (default `""` — preserves wire-format privacy since the WP GET payload doesn't include emails; `WPDTO.Comment.toDomain()` maps to empty); the submit path stamps `RecipeComment.authorEmail = profile.email` on the just-posted comment locally before insertion via `insertPostedCommentIfNew(_:)` so own-comment photos render immediately in-session. `CommentRow` rendering inside `commentsList` now consults the row builder + the case-insensitive `comment.authorEmail.lowercased() == profile.email.lowercased()` check to swap in `ProfilePhotoView(profile: profile, diameter: 40, photoStore: viewModel.profilePhotoStoreForGate)` for the current user's own comments — other users' comments fall through to the existing Gravatar `AsyncImage` path. Old guest comments naturally fall through (their empty `authorEmail` doesn't match a real profile's email — AC-44.7 preserved by construction). The Phase c gate is preserved verbatim — when `profile == nil` the composer is still blurred + popup-overlaid; AC-44.12 only changes what the composer LOOKS LIKE when unlocked.

### T-743 — Profile + Ratings design tweaks (US-44 amendment, CL-140)

- **What:** Four focused polish tweaks bundled into one PR on top of the just-shipped DUT-36 feature set (Phases a–d / T-739–T-742 all merged). **(1) Ratings empty-state label gate.** `RecipeDetailRatingsSection`'s `ratingsHeader` now hides the "Be the first to rate this recipe." invitation unless BOTH `viewModel.ratingSummary?.count ?? 0 == 0` AND `viewModel.comments.isEmpty` — a recipe with 0 ratings but ≥1 comment isn't "empty" any more (CL-140 (1)). **(2) Circle crop overlay + unified `.clipShape(Circle())`.** `ProfilePhotoCropView`'s dimmed-outside overlay's "hole" is swapped from `Rectangle` to `Circle` so the user sees how the avatar will actually look (CL-140 (2)). The pre-audit confirmed every avatar render site in the app (`InitialLetterAvatarView` at 60pt / 32pt / 40pt; `ProfilePhotoView`; `CommentRow`'s default AsyncImage path + the Phase d `avatarOverride` path) already uses `.clipShape(Circle())` — so this commit is overlay-shape-only. The on-disk image stays a square JPG at 512×512 @ 0.85 quality (locked per CL-137, render-side clipping per CL-140 (2)). **(3) Custom back chevron + unsaved-changes confirmationDialog.** `ProfileEditView`'s `Cancel` toolbar button is replaced by a system back chevron (`Image(systemName: "chevron.left")`, no text); `.navigationBarBackButtonHidden(true)` suppresses the system back. Three `@State` snapshots (`initialDisplayName`, `initialEmail`, `initialPhotoFilename`) seed on `.onAppear`; `isDirty` is computed against them. Back-chevron taps with `isDirty == true` open a `.confirmationDialog("You have unsaved changes", titleVisibility: .visible)` with "Continue Editing" + "Leave Without Saving" (role: `.destructive`). `.interactiveDismissDisabled(isDirty)` prevents drag-to-dismiss past unsaved changes in the modal-sheet context (Phase c gate-CTA path). Sign Out + Delete Profile bypass the dialog by construction — both buttons call `handleClear()` (clears profile + dismisses) without consulting `isDirty` or `showLeaveConfirmation`; the Delete Profile button's existing destructive alert "Delete your profile?" is preserved and is distinct from the new unsaved-changes dialog (CL-140 (3) + (4)). **(4) "Done" → "Save" rename.** `.topBarTrailing` `Button("Done")` becomes `Button("Save")`; action / disabled state / behavior unchanged. Accessibility identifier `"profile-edit-done"` is renamed to `"profile-edit-save"` to keep the contract honest (CL-140 (5)). The Phase c gate (`RatingsProfileGate` popup) is NOT touched — Tweak 1's empty-state label is a separate, lower-down element. Comment-row rendering for OTHER users (Phase d's `commentRow(for:)` + `avatarOverride`) is NOT touched — other users' avatars were already Circle-clipped at the `CommentRow` level. `App/Info.plist` is NOT touched — no `NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription` is needed.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (US-44 amendment — AC-44.14 added [empty-state label gate requires zero ratings AND zero comments] + AC-44.15 added [uniform `.clipShape(Circle())` across avatar render sites + circle crop overlay] + AC-44.16 added [custom back chevron + unsaved-changes confirmationDialog + `interactiveDismissDisabled(isDirty)` + Sign Out / Delete Profile bypass + `Save` rename]). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-140 entry — the locked decisions for each tweak, the render-side-vs-PNG-with-alpha rationale for the storage format, the Sign Out / Delete Profile bypass-by-construction explanation, the considered-and-rejected alternatives). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry). `specs/dod-ios-app/backlog.md` is NOT touched.
  - **Ratings empty-state gate (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Sources/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/RecipeDetailRatingsSection.swift` (the `ratingsHeader` branch gains a 2-condition check — `(summary?.count ?? 0) > 0 || !viewModel.comments.isEmpty` shows the aggregate / hidden / etc., `else` shows the invitation; the existing `summary.count` swiftlint disable comment stays). `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/Tests/DODFeatureRecipeDetailTests/RecipeDetailRatingsRenderingTests.swift` OR a new `RecipeDetailRatingsEmptyStateGateTests.swift` (L1 test covers 4 combinations: 0/0 shows the label / >0/0 shows aggregate / 0/>0 hides label / >0/>0 shows aggregate).
  - **Circle crop overlay (commit 3):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoCropView.swift` (the `cropOverlay(cropSize:frameSize:)` helper's two inner `Rectangle().frame(...)` shapes — the mask hole + the visible stroke — become `Circle().frame(...)`; the outer dimmed-overlay `Rectangle().fill(Color.black.opacity(0.55))` stays as a `Rectangle` since it spans the full frame). No avatar render site needs changes (pre-audit confirmed all use Circle).
  - **Custom back chevron + unsaved-changes confirmationDialog (commit 4):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileEditView.swift` (three new `@State` initial-value snapshots; `isDirty` computed property; `showLeaveConfirmation` `@State` Bool; toolbar's `.cancellationAction` placement REMOVED and replaced with a `.topBarLeading` `ToolbarItem` carrying the back chevron Button; `.navigationBarBackButtonHidden(true)` modifier; `.interactiveDismissDisabled(isDirty)` modifier; `.confirmationDialog(...)` modifier; `.onAppear` extended to seed the three snapshot vars). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/` — optional L1 if we can pin `isDirty` without a UI host (the computed property is exposed via `@testable` `var isDirty: Bool { get }` — the four-combination check covers `clean / name-dirty / email-dirty / photo-dirty`).

### T-744 — DUT-37 hide composer email + DUT-38 solid gate popup background (US-44 amendment, CL-141)

- **What:** Two XS polish tweaks bundled into one PR on top of T-743 / CL-140's design-tweak pass. **(1) DUT-37 — hide email from ``PostingAsHeader``.** The composer's `PostingAsHeader` (extracted by T-742 in `RecipeDetailRatingsSection+PhaseD.swift`) currently renders avatar + display name + email; the email row is removed so the composer surface displays avatar + display name only. The `VStack` collapses to a single `Text(profile.displayName)`; the `accessibilityLabel` collapses to "Posting as `<displayName>`". Published comments never expose `author_email` (verified at 3 layers — WP REST `/wp/v2/comments` GET redacts, `WPCommentDTO` zeroes on parse, `CommentRow` has no email render path), so the composer's own-email display was inconsistent with that privacy posture. Email is preserved on Settings → Profile section header + `ProfileEditView` (where Spencer actively manages it) and continues to flow through unchanged as `author_email` in the WP REST submission — only the composer header UI display changes. **(2) DUT-38 — gate popup solid surface background.** ``RatingsProfileGate``'s popup card today renders as a grey-brown haze in dark mode because the `.background(RoundedRectangle.fill(.surface).shadow(...))` chain renders the shadow + fill as a single composite layer that doesn't read as fully opaque to SwiftUI's depth-aware Material blending against the underlying `.ultraThinMaterial` overlay. Fix: hoist the `.shadow(...)` OUT of the `.background(...)` fill chain so the fill commits as its own opaque layer first, then the shadow applies to the composed card. Card colors stay on the existing token: light mode renders white (`#FFFFFF`), dark mode renders deep warm-brown (`#1B140E`), matching the recipe-detail page background. The ZStack composition in `gatedRateAndReviewCard` (composer.blur → Material.Rectangle → RatingsProfileGate) is preserved verbatim per AC-44.10 + the task brief's locked decision. **What this does NOT touch.** WP REST submission payload (locked — `author_email` still flows through as `profile.email`); the gate ZStack structure (preserved); Profile edit view, photo crop, comment-row rendering for OTHER users (out of scope per task brief). No third-party deps (Constitution §3).

### T-745 — DUT-39 direct picker + Edit Photo + original-image storage (US-44 amendment, CL-142)

- **What:** Two related changes to the Profile Picture row in ``ProfileEditView`` bundled into one PR, closes Linear DUT-39. **(1) Direct picker on placeholder (the easy half — bug fix).** When `profile.photoFilename == nil` (placeholder showing), tapping the Profile Picture row should open ``PhotosPicker`` directly. Per CL-142 the conditional is already in place in `ProfileEditView+Photo.swift` (lines 41-45: `if inFlightPhotoFilename != nil { showPhotoActionDialog = true } else { isPickerPresented = true }`), so this step is verify-and-document rather than restructure. The bug-fix commit message records the state-on-inspection. **(2) Edit Photo feature (the substantive half).** Add an **Edit Photo** option to the action sheet (only fires when a photo IS set). Loads the user's existing source image and re-opens ``ProfilePhotoCropView`` so they can re-crop without re-picking from the photo library. Requires storing the **picked original** alongside the cropped 512×512 derivative: ``UserProfile`` gains an optional `photoOriginalFilename` field; ``ProfilePhotoStore`` gains `saveOriginal` / `loadOriginal` / `clearOriginal` methods; Replace + Edit + Remove + Sign Out + Delete Profile flows all extend to handle both files. The original is downscaled to a longest-dimension cap of 2048 pixels at JPEG 0.85 (~300-500KB), preserves aspect ratio. Legacy users with only the cropped derivative fall back to re-cropping the cropped image (documented quality limitation but functional). **The trade-off (locked in CL-142).** Per-profile Documents footprint grows from ~50-150KB (cropped only) to ~350-650KB total (cropped + 2048-max original) — negligible on iOS, enables meaningful re-crop without library round-trip. **No NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription** (`PhotosPicker` doesn't need it, locked per CL-137 + reiterated). **No new third-party dependency** (Constitution §3). The ``ProfilePhotoCropView`` core interface is preserved (still accepts a `UIImage` + `onComplete` + `onCancel`; both Replace and Edit flows feed it different sources but the view itself is unchanged).

### T-746 — DUT-40 stale photoFilename file-existence validation (US-44 amendment, CL-143)

- **What:** Self-heal stale `inFlightPhotoFilename` / `inFlightPhotoOriginalFilename` references at ``ProfileEditView`` mount. T-745 / CL-142's AC-44.8 conditional picker entry (`if inFlightPhotoFilename != nil { showPhotoActionDialog = true } else { isPickerPresented = true }` in `handleProfilePictureRowTap`) is logically correct but doesn't account for the filename string carrying a STALE reference to a deleted file. The simulator Keychain persists across `simctl uninstall`/install while Documents is wiped, so any dev rebuild after setting a profile photo creates this mismatch — `ProfilePhotoView` gracefully falls back to `InitialLetterAvatarView` on the missing file so visually the user sees the placeholder, but the conditional treats the stale string as "has photo" and surfaces the action sheet instead of the picker (the user-facing complaint in Linear DUT-40). Theoretical production edge cases (Files.app delete, storage-pressure eviction, partial CloudKit restore) make this not just a dev-hygiene issue. Fix: add `exists(filename:)` + `existsOriginal(filename:)` sync-shape (actor-async) methods to ``ProfilePhotoStoring``; in ``ProfileEditView`` add a `.task` after the existing `.onAppear` seeding that awaits the existence check on both in-flight filenames and nils stale references. The `handleProfilePictureRowTap` conditional is NOT changed — it becomes correct by construction once stale references are cleared at mount. The Save flow is NOT changed either — `handleSave()` persists the now-correctly-nil in-flight state to the Keychain row so the inconsistency self-heals permanently (no migration / proactive scan needed). Closes Linear DUT-40.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (US-44 — AC-44.8 amended to note the file-existence validation at view mount + the stale-reference self-heal semantics). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-143 entry — the bug shape, the Keychain-vs-Documents persistence asymmetry, the chosen `.task` lifecycle approach, the considered-and-rejected alternatives). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Photo store sync-exists helper (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoStore.swift` (`ProfilePhotoStoring` protocol gains `exists(filename:) async -> Bool` + `existsOriginal(filename:) async -> Bool`; production ``ProfilePhotoStore`` actor implements both via `fileManager.fileExists(atPath:)` — same primitive `load(filename:)` already pre-flights with, but without the subsequent `Data(contentsOf:)` read; `InMemoryProfilePhotoStore` actor implements via `stored[filename] != nil` dictionary lookup). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/ProfilePhotoStoreTests.swift` (L1 tests: `exists` true for a real file, false for a missing filename, false for empty string; `existsOriginal` mirror; both stores).
  - **Stale-reference self-heal at view mount (commit 3):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileEditView.swift` (add `.task { ... }` modifier after `.onAppear` that awaits the existence check on `inFlightPhotoFilename` + `inFlightPhotoOriginalFilename` and nils stale references). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/ProfileEditViewDirtyStateTests.swift` or new file (L1 integration: stale filename + missing file → view-mount task nils the in-flight; real filename + extant file → in-flight stays populated; assert via injected mock store).
  - **Out of bounds:** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileEditView+Photo.swift` (unchanged — `handleProfilePictureRowTap`'s conditional is preserved verbatim; per task brief). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfileEditView+Save.swift` (unchanged — `handleSave` already persists the in-flight state including nil; the self-heal flows naturally on next save). `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/{ProfileStore,UserProfile,ProfilePhotoView,InitialLetterAvatarView}.swift` (unchanged — `ProfilePhotoView` already gracefully degrades on missing files per CL-137; the new `exists` method is a sibling, not a replacement). `App/AppDependencies.swift` (unchanged — composition root continues to construct the production stores; `exists` is a protocol-level addition).
- **AC:** US-44 AC-44.8 amended (the conditional is file-existence-validated at view mount; stale references nil out before the conditional is evaluated). Pins the Phase a + b + c + d contracts unchanged.
- **Est:** ~30 min total (~10 min spec + ~15 min impl + ~5 min verify).
- **Deps:** main is at `7063728` (T-745 / CL-142 merged). Branch (`fix/T-746-stale-photofilename`) is off `origin/main` and independent of every in-flight branch. Source-side touches `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/{ProfilePhotoStore,ProfileEditView}.swift` (2 source edits) + `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/{ProfilePhotoStoreTests}.swift` (1 test extension) + a new or extended test file for the view-mount integration.
- **||:** P29-stale-photofilename (DUT-40 bug). `e2e` label IS applied — the picker-entry routing was visibly wrong (action sheet on placeholder after dev rebuild) and is now correct (picker direct from placeholder); the dev-rebuild reproducer is end-to-end user-observable.

### T-748 — DUT-54 profile photo crop units bug (US-44 amendment, CL-145)

- **What:** Fix the units bug in ``ProfilePhotoCropView/cropRect(...)`` that made the saved profile picture larger than the visible crop circle (Spencer's "cropping doesn't render after the profile pic is saved" complaint). The pre-T-748 helper signature `cropRect(for:offset:imageSize:cropSize:)` conflated screen points + source pixels: `offset` arrives from `DragGesture.translation` in SCREEN POINTS but the helper treated it as source pixels (so pan gestures moved the crop `1/scaleFactor` × less than the user expected — ~7-8× too small for typical phone-photo configs), and `cropSize` was normalized to "fraction of source shorter side" so passing `cropSize: 1.0` always saved the FULL inscribed square of the source rather than the contents of the visible crop circle (~17% larger than the actual circle on a typical portrait-source/portrait-screen config). Combined effect at zero pan / zero zoom: saved crop is essentially the entire source image (just trimmed to a square) — looks "uncropped" relative to what the user framed. New signature: `cropRect(for:offset:imageSize:cropDiameterInScreenPoints:displayedImageScaleFactor:)`. Helper converts both `offset` + `cropDiameterInScreenPoints` to source pixels via `displayedImageScaleFactor * scale`; clamps `visibleSide` to source's shorter side so a circle wider than the source doesn't produce a degenerate rect. View captures `GeometryReader.proxy.size` into `@State` (`.onAppear` + `.onChange(of:)` inside the GeometryReader closure) so `renderCroppedImage()` (called from the toolbar Done button outside the closure) can read it. Companion helper ``displayedImageScaleFactor(imageSize:proxySize:)`` exposes SwiftUI's `.scaledToFit()` ratio as a pure static so the test suite can pin the conversion. Closes Linear DUT-54.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (US-44 — AC-44.3 amended to note the screen-pt → source-px conversion + the new helper signature). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-145 entry — the symptom, the two-axis units bug, the fix shape, the locked decision to do the conversion inside the helper (not at the call site), considered-and-rejected alternatives (a) `cropSize: 0.85` partial fix that ignores the offset bug, (b) compute `cropRect` inside the GeometryReader closure and capture into @State on every gesture change — too chatty + races the Done tap, (c) refactor to a UIViewRepresentable wrapper around `CIImage` — overkill for an XS fix). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoCropView.swift` (new helper signature; new `displayedImageScaleFactor` static; view captures `capturedProxySize` @State; `renderCroppedImage()` reads captured proxy + computes scaleFactor; doc-comments amended).
  - **Tests (commit 2 same):** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/ProfilePhotoCropMathTests.swift` (full rewrite for the new signature; new test cases cover the screen-pt → source-px conversion at scale 1.0 + scale 2.0, the "circle wider than source" clamp case, the defensive guards including a new zero-scaleFactor guard).
  - **Out of bounds:** `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoStore.swift` (unchanged — the cropped image arrives at the right pixels after the helper fix; `save(_:)` continues to re-render to 512×512 + write to disk). `ProfileEditView+Photo.swift` (unchanged — `handleCroppedImage` still receives the renderer's output + writes to disk + assigns `inFlightPhotoFilename`). The crop view's gestures + visual overlay + `cropOverlay(...)` are unchanged (visible layout identical pre/post T-748).
- **AC:** US-44 AC-44.3 amended (the saved profile photo matches the contents of the visible crop circle; pan + zoom gestures move the saved crop in proportion to the screen-pt gesture amount; supersedes the `cropSize: 1.0` contract from T-740 / CL-137 which silently saved the full inscribed square). Pins every other AC-44 contract unchanged.
- **Est:** ~45 min total (~15 min spec + ~15 min impl + ~15 min verify).
- **Deps:** main is at `667fe72` (DUT-53 ingredient self-heal merged). Branch (`fix/T-748-crop-units-bug`) is off `origin/main` and independent of every in-flight branch. Source-side touches `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Sources/DODFeatureProfile/ProfilePhotoCropView.swift` (1 source edit) + `Packages/DODFeatureProfile/Tests/DODFeatureProfileTests/ProfilePhotoCropMathTests.swift` (1 test rewrite).
- **||:** P31-crop-units-bug (DUT-54). `e2e` label IS applied — the saved crop visibly differs from what the user framed and the fix is end-to-end user-observable on the Profile Picture row's avatar.

### T-756 — DUT-62 App Appearance observability + live sheet theme (US-36 amendment, CL-153)

- **What:** Fix two App Appearance bugs: (1) the picker's selected-value label didn't update on change, and (2) the Settings sheet didn't re-theme live (only on close + reopen). Root cause: `SettingsViewModel.appearance` (+ `temperaturePreference` + `voiceGender`) were computed-over-store, which `@Observable` can't track (so mutations never re-rendered the picker); and the Settings `.sheet` doesn't inherit `RootView`'s `preferredColorScheme`. Fix: convert the three picker preferences to `@Observable` stored properties (didSet-persist, init-seed) + apply `.preferredColorScheme(viewModel.appearance.colorScheme)` to `SettingsView` so the sheet re-themes live. Closes Linear DUT-62.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-36.2 amended — observable preference + sheet's own preferredColorScheme). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-153 — the two bugs, the observation + sheet root causes, the fix, considered-and-rejected alternatives). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source (commit 2):** `SettingsViewModel.swift` (convert `appearance` + `voiceGender` to stored+didSet; add `temperaturePreference` stored+didSet moved from the extension; seed all three in init; trim docs to stay ≤400). `SettingsViewModel+Temperature.swift` (drop the computed property, keep the key). `SettingsPreferences.swift` (`AppearancePreference.colorScheme: ColorScheme?` helper + `import SwiftUI`). `SettingsView.swift` (`.preferredColorScheme(viewModel.appearance.colorScheme)` on the body). `App/RootView.swift` (`preferredColorScheme(for:)` delegates to `appearance.colorScheme`).
  - **Tests (commit 2 same):** `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Tests/DODFeatureFeedTests/SettingsViewModelObservationTests.swift` (NEW — `withObservationTracking` proves each picker preference mutation emits an observation change; would fail on the old computed code + a persistence-preservation test).
  - **Out of bounds:** `shareFormat` (stays computed — no reactive UI reads it post-T-750); the toggles (`useMetricUnits` / `notificationsEnabled` / `telemetryEnabled` — interactive state masks the issue, deferred); the persisted keys + wire format; Recipe Detail's `@AppStorage` temperature read; the voice store read by `SystemSpeechSynthesizer`.
- **AC:** US-36 AC-36.2 amended (observable appearance + sheet preferredColorScheme). Pins every other Settings AC unchanged.
- **Est:** ~65 min total.

### T-757 — DUT-63 iCloud Sync confirmation dialog (US-41 amendment, CL-154)

- **What:** Fix two iCloud Sync confirmation bugs: (1) the dialog's buttons were orange (need white), and (2) the toggle never flipped ON. Both stem from it being a system `.alert` — alert buttons inherit the app's orange `.tint` (no per-button override), and the alert's `isPresented`-binding cancel-on-dismiss synchronously reverted the async `confirmCloudSyncFlip()` so the flip was swallowed. Fix: replace the system `.alert` with a custom branded `CloudSyncConfirmationDialog` (dimmed backdrop + centered `DODColor.surface` card, filled `castIronBrown` primary button with `cream` white text, plain Cancel) presented via an `.overlay` driven purely by `cloudSyncConfirmationRequest` — no `isPresented` binding, so no cancel-on-dismiss race. The view-model flow is unchanged. Closes Linear DUT-63.
- **Files:**
  - **Spec/clarifications/tasks (commit 1):** `specs/dod-ios-app/spec.md` (AC-41.3 amended — custom branded confirmation dialog). `specs/dod-ios-app/clarifications.md` (new CL-154 — the two bugs, the system-alert root causes, the custom-dialog fix, considered-and-rejected alternatives). `specs/dod-ios-app/tasks.md` (this entry).
  - **Source (commit 2):** `Packages/DODFeatureFeed/Sources/DODFeatureFeed/SettingsView+CloudSync.swift` (replace `CloudSyncConfirmationAlertModifier` (system `.alert`) with `CloudSyncConfirmationDialog` + `CloudSyncConfirmationDialogModifier` overlay; the `cloudSyncConfirmationAlert(viewModel:)` entry name is kept).
  - **Out of bounds:** `SettingsViewModel+CloudSync.swift` (the VM confirm/cancel/request flow — unchanged; all its L1 tests pass untouched); `CloudSyncRows` (the toggle + status row — unchanged); the CL-89 confirm-every-flip contract (honored).

### T-761 — DUT-67 Decouple Save and Download: download saves + downloads, save = save only (US-5/US-35 amendment, CL-158)

- **What:** Split the two recipe-detail toolbar actions cleanly. (1) **Download also saves** — the live `downloadForOffline` calls a new `RecipeStore.markSaved(id:)` after `markDownloaded`, the VM flips `isSaved = true` + republishes the saved widget. (2) **Download no longer treats "saved" as "already downloaded"** — the `.alreadyDownloaded` short-circuit keys only on the real `downloadedAt` pin, so a merely-saved recipe downloads fresh; a re-download still surfaces "Already downloaded" yet still ensures the recipe is saved ("downloaded recipes still save"). (3) **Save is a lightweight favorite** — snackbar "Saved for offline." → "Saved."; Save sets only `isSaved`, never downloads. Closes Linear DUT-67.

Tests: `RecipeStoreTests` (+`markSavedIsIdempotentAndPinsTheRow`), `RecipeDetailDownloadTests` (retargeted + 3 new), `FakeRecipeDetailDependencies` (mirror prod).

### T-772 — DUT-79 Saved widget images: fix filename→URL resolver pointing at the wrong directory (US-17 amendment, CL-169)

- **What:** The Saved widget never rendered hero photos. `SavedRecipesWidgetEntryView.heroImageURL(forFilename:)` appended the filename to the App Group container **root** (`<container>/<sha>.img`), missing the `widget-images/` subdir the bridge writes to (`WidgetImageBridge.imageSubdirectory`) — so it looked one directory too high and fell back to the gradient for every saved row, even though CL-163 set the filename and CL-167 bridged the bytes. The Latest widget worked because it used `WidgetImageBridge.fileURL`. **Fix:** delegate `heroImageURL(forFilename:)` to `WidgetImageBridge.fileURL(forFilename:)` — the single canonical resolver both widgets share. Closes Linear DUT-79. The real completion of the DUT-76 saved-images work.
- **Files:** `specs/dod-ios-app/{clarifications.md (CL-169), spec.md (AC-17.3 amended), tasks.md}`. `Widget/SavedRecipesWidgetEntryView.swift` (1-line behavior change + doc comment).

Branch `fix/T-772-saved-widget-image-path`. **||:** P47-widget-overhaul (DUT-79). No `e2e` label — Widget extension target isn't unit-testable; the fix routes through `WidgetImageBridge.fileURL`, whose `widget-images/` inclusion is locked by `WidgetImageBridgeTests.fileURLReturnsContainerPlusSubdirectoryPlusFilename`; on-device confirmed (filename + bridged file both present, only the resolution path was wrong).

### T-776 — DUT-82 Saved tab "Saved" header vanishes after a context-menu action — iOS 26 NavigationStack workaround (US-5 amendment, CL-173)

- **What:** The Saved tab's large `.navigationTitle("Saved")` disappears after any card context-menu action (Unsave, or Remove Download from T-775) and only returns on relaunch — a confirmed iOS 26 NavigationStack bug (`.navigationTitle` + `.toolbar` + list + a state transition; [Apple forums/thread/789557](https://developer.apple.com/forums/thread/789557)). PRE-EXISTING: Unsave (CL-103) triggers it on `main`, so T-775's Remove Download merely added a second action that hits the same OS bug. Two in-app fixes failed (sync optimistic mutation; stable `ToolbarItem` + pinned `displayMode`). Fix: render "Saved" as a manual large header in the view content (`Text("Saved").font(.largeTitle).fontWeight(.bold)` in a `VStack` above `content`) + drop `.navigationTitle`; keep the `.toolbar` cart; `.isHeader` trait preserves VoiceOver. Closes Linear DUT-82.

### T-778 — DUT-84 Warn before removing a download while offline — confirm dialog (Keep Download / Remove Download), offline-only (US-35 amendment, CL-175)

- **What:** When the user un-downloads a recipe while offline (no cell/Wi-Fi), confirm first — removing the offline copy would strand the recipe until service returns. Both remove surfaces gate on `dependencies.isOnline()`: the recipe-detail download toggle (`RecipeDetailViewModel.toggleDownload()`) and the Saved-tab "Remove Download" menu (`SavedViewModel.requestRemoveDownload(id:)`). Offline → a native alert ("You're offline" / "If you remove this download, you won't be able to open this recipe until you're back online. You can always download it again once you're connected.") with **Remove Download** (destructive) + **Keep Download** (cancel); online → remove immediately, unchanged. Shared `View.offlineRemoveDownloadAlert(isPresented:onRemove:)` (DODDesignSystem) so both surfaces match. `SavedDependencies` gains `isOnline()` → `NetworkMonitor`. Unsave intentionally not gated (Save ≠ offline pin per AC-35.7; a download survives unsave per AC-35.5). Closes Linear DUT-84.
- **Files:** `DODDesignSystem/OfflineRemoveDownloadAlert.swift` (new), `RecipeDetailView+OfflineWarning.swift` (new — `@Bindable` modifier), `RecipeDetailView.swift`, `RecipeDetailViewModel.swift` (+`showOfflineRemoveDownloadWarning`; `isOffline` moved to `+Download` for the file cap), `RecipeDetailViewModel+Download.swift`, `SavedDependencies.swift`, `SavedView.swift`, `SavedViewModel.swift`, `AppDependencies.swift`; tests `RecipeDetailDownloadTests` (+3), `SavedViewModelTests` (fake), `SavedViewModelDownloadWarningTests` (new, +4).
- **AC:** US-35 AC-35.8 added (offline remove-download confirmation); AC-35.1 (detail toggle) + AC-34.6 (Saved menu) amended (CL-175 canonical). **Est:** ~75 min. **Deps:** main at `ae115a9` (T-777 merged). Branch `feat/T-778-offline-remove-download-warning`. **||:** P48-saved-polish (DUT-84). No `e2e` label — local state + a system alert; L1 (7 new tests: detail VM ×3 + saved VM ×4) pinned; offline path unit-tested (forcing the sim's `NWPathMonitor` offline isn't practical), build green + lint/format clean, all files ≤ 400 lines.

### T-779 — DUT-85 Search: record a Recent only on Return or keyboard dismissal, not on every typing pause (US-12 amendment, CL-176)

- **What:** Recents were recorded in the search-completion path (runs on every 150 ms debounce), so any mid-typing pause dropped a partial query under "Recent". Decouple it: keep the live as-you-type search + telemetry, but record a recent only on **Return** (`.onSubmit`) or **keyboard dismissal** (search field focus loss). Split `recordRecentAndTelemetry` → `sendSearchTelemetry` (telemetry only) + add `SearchViewModel.commitRecentSearch()` (new `+Recents` extension; skips curated-"Try" taps per REG-19 and < 2-char queries). `DODSearchField` gains an optional `onFocusChange` callback (internal `@FocusState`); `SearchView` wires `onFocusChange` + `.onSubmit`. Closes Linear DUT-85.
- **Files:** `DODDesignSystem/Components/DODSearchField.swift` (+`onFocusChange`/`@FocusState`), `SearchView.swift` (wire focus + onSubmit; `layoutRaw` internal), `SearchView+Toolbar.swift` (new — relocated `layoutToggleToolbarButton` for the file-length cap), `SearchViewModel.swift` (split telemetry; `queryFromCuratedTap`/`recents`/`recentSearches` access widened for the extension), `SearchViewModel+Recents.swift` (new — `commitRecentSearch()`), `SearchViewModel+T643.swift` (caller rename); tests `SearchViewModelTests` (3 updated), `SearchViewModelRecentsTests` (new, +2).
- **AC:** US-12 AC-12.4 amended (recents recorded on commit, not the live search) (CL-176 canonical). **Est:** ~60 min. **Deps:** main at `24465e2` (T-778 merged). Branch `feat/T-779-search-recents-submit-dismiss`. **||:** P-search-polish (DUT-85). No `e2e` label — local state + focus wiring; L1 (DODFeatureSearch suite + 2 new recents tests) pinned; build green + lint/format clean, all files ≤ 400 lines.

### T-793 — DUT-16 Login Phase a: Sign in with Apple — on-device auth session store (US-46 new, CL-189)

- **What:** First verifiable slice of DUT-16 (login). The on-device **`AppleAuthSession`** value type + **`AppleAuthSessionStoring`** protocol + **`KeychainAppleAuthSessionStore`** (production) + **`InMemoryAppleAuthSessionStore`** (tests) in DODSupport — mirrors the US-15 `GuestIdentityStoring` family. Device-local Keychain (`kSecAttrSynchronizable: false`, DUT-30 parity). Stores the stable Apple `userIdentifier` + optional first-auth-only `displayName` / `email` (private-relay aware). Dumb exact-persist store; first-auth merge is the (later) coordinator's job. **Backend-independent + guest stays default** — no behavior change to any existing surface; this is purely additive foundation.
- **Files:** `DODSupport/AppleAuthSession.swift` (new), `DODSupportTests/AppleAuthSessionStoreTests.swift` (new). Spec: `spec.md` (US-46), `clarifications.md` (CL-189), `tasks.md` (T-793).
- **AC:** US-46 / AC-46.1 (CL-189 canonical); AC-46.2..46.5 scope the follow-up slices (SwiftUI `SignInWithAppleButton` + `ASAuthorizationController` coordinator + `applesignin` entitlement; Settings Account section + in-app Delete Account per 5.1.1(v); comment/rating identity routing; Phase b/c/d). **Est:** ~1.5 h (S). **Deps:** main at T-789 (`6837c47`). Branch `feat/T-793-apple-auth-session-store`. **||:** P-support (owns a new DODSupport file; no overlap with the concurrent T-790/791/792 PRs). No `e2e` label — pure-Swift foundation, no UI / nav / persistence-schema / composition-root change. swift-format + SwiftLint `--strict` clean; **all 394 DODSupport L1 tests pass locally (Xcode 26.5 `swift test`)**. **Decision pending (Phase d):** backend provider (WordPress / Firebase / Supabase / custom) — flagged for the owner; does not block Phase a. **Follow-up:** the SwiftUI sign-in slice (AC-46.2/46.3) needs the `com.apple.developer.applesignin` capability enabled in the Apple Developer portal.

### T-795 — CI: L3 UI Smoke device matrix (older iPhones + iPad)

- **What:** Run the L3 UI Smoke suite across a **device matrix** so regressions that only show on an older/smaller iPhone are caught. `ensure-ios-simulator` gains a `device` input (default `iPhone 17`, create-if-missing on the newest iOS runtime — composes with the T-NNN runtime-download fix); the L3 job becomes a `fail-fast: false` matrix over **iPhone 14 / 15 / 16 / 17** (full `DODAppUITests` suite — the iPhone bottom-tab layout is identical across them) **+ iPad Pro 11-inch** (the device-agnostic `test_settingsAccountSectionIsPresent` only). L4 snapshots + L1-DODApp + L5 stay iPhone-17 (the action's default) — L4 baselines are iPhone-17 geometry.
- **Files:** `.github/actions/ensure-ios-simulator/action.yml` (`device` input), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (L3 `strategy.matrix` + `with: device` + `${{ matrix.only_testing }}`).
- **AC:** L3 runs green on iPhone 14/15/16/17; iPad runs the Account smoke. **Est:** ~1.5 h. **Deps:** stacked on T-794 (#224, the device-agnostic Account smoke + latest `SmokeTests`) + the CI runtime-download fix (#222) — this branch merges both. Branch `chore/ci-device-matrix-smoke`. **Verification:** both workflow YAMLs parse; locally (Xcode 26.5) the scroll-dependent Settings smokes (`test_settingsShopRowIsPresentAndTappable`, `test_settingsPageHasNoDebugTestButton`) pass on **iPhone 14**, and the Account smoke passes on iPhone 14/15/16/17 + iPad — so the matrix legs are validated before CI. **Follow-up — done in T-796:** the `SmokeTests` class is now device-agnostic so iPad runs the **full** class (not just the Account smoke).

### T-796 — iPad full SmokeTests: device-agnostic UI smoke suite

- **What:** Make `UITests/SmokeTests` run on the iPad sidebar layout (US-38 / DUT-89), so the T-795 matrix's iPad leg runs the **full** `SmokeTests` class instead of only the Account smoke. Adds three helpers — `isPad` (idiom), `waitForAppReady` (waits on the Settings gear, present on both the iPhone tab nav + iPad sidebar/detail, instead of the iPhone-only tab bar), and `goToTab(phoneLabel:padTitle:)` (taps the bottom-tab button on iPhone, the sidebar `List` row on iPad — the labels differ: `AppTab.tabLabel` "Recipes" vs `AppTab.title` "Recipes & Articles"). Launch / settings / onboarding / Saved-tab tests switch to these; the two genuinely iPhone-bottom-tab-contract tests (`test_allFourTabsAreReachable`, `test_tabBarOrderMatchesSpec`) `XCTSkipIf(isPad)` (the iPad sidebar is a different surface with its own contract). The iPad matrix leg's `only_testing` becomes `DODAppUITests/SmokeTests`.
- **Files:** `UITests/SmokeTests.swift` (helpers + 7 test edits), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (iPad matrix leg → full `SmokeTests`).
- **AC:** iPad runs the full `SmokeTests` class green; iPhone unaffected. **Est:** ~1.5 h. **Deps:** stacked on T-795 (#225). Branch `test/ipad-smoke-device-agnostic`. **Verification (Xcode 26.5):** on **iPad Pro** — `savedTab` (sidebar nav), `settingsPageHasNoDebugTestButton`, `appLaunches` pass; the two tab tests **skip** (clear reasons); 0 failures. On **iPhone 17** (regression) — the same tests all **run + pass** (the tab tests run, not skip; `goToTab` uses the tab bar). **Follow-up:** `RecipeDetailRatingsSmokeTests` (the other UITest class) isn't retrofitted, so the iPad leg runs `SmokeTests` specifically, not all of `DODAppUITests`; retrofit it if iPad coverage of the ratings smoke is wanted.

### T-797 — Sign in with Apple account-deletion token revoke (US-46 / AC-46.6, CL-191) — closes the DUT-98 App Store blocker

- **What:** Makes Sign in with Apple App-Store-submittable: account deletion **revokes the Apple token** (5.1.1(v)). **Backend:** `backend/siwa-revoke/` — a stateless Cloudflare Worker (TS) holding the SiwA key; `POST /exchange` (code→refresh token) + `POST /revoke`, ES256 `client_secret` via WebCrypto, gated by `X-DOD-App-Key`. **iOS:** `AppleAuthSession.refreshToken` (+ Keychain), `SiwaRevokeClient` (DODSupport, injectable transport), `AccountViewModel` exchanges the code on sign-in + best-effort-revokes on delete. Graceful-degrade until `SiwaRevokeConfig.production` is filled (TestFlight needs no Worker).
- **Files:** `backend/siwa-revoke/{src/index.ts,wrangler.toml,package.json,tsconfig.json,README.md,test/clientSecret.test.mjs}` (new), `DODSupport/{AppleAuthSession.swift (refreshToken),SiwaRevokeClient.swift (new)}`, `DODSupportTests/SiwaRevokeClientTests.swift` (new), `DODFeatureFeed/{AccountViewModel.swift,SettingsView+Account.swift}`, `DODFeatureFeedTests/AccountViewModelTests.swift`. Spec: `spec.md` (US-46 AC-46.6), `clarifications.md` (CL-191).
- **AC:** US-46 / AC-46.6 (CL-191 canonical). **Est:** ~4 h. **Deps:** stacked on T-794/796 (the SiwA UI). Branch `feat/T-797-siwa-revoke`. No `e2e` label. **Verification:** ES256 `client_secret` round-trips (Node `node:test`); iOS exchange/revoke + AccountViewModel paths L1-tested (407 DODSupport + 93 DODFeatureFeed pass); swift-format + SwiftLint `--strict` clean; xcodebuild app build green. **Owner steps (gate App Store submission of SiwA):** create a Sign in with Apple **Key** in the portal, `wrangler deploy`, set `APPLE_PRIVATE_KEY` + `APP_SHARED_SECRET` secrets, fill `SiwaRevokeConfig.production`. Closes DUT-98. The interactive sign-in→exchange→delete→revoke round-trip is device-verified once the Worker is live.

## Summary

- **Total tasks:** 73 (Phase 1–5) + 6 (Phase 6 consultant pass) + 5 (Phase 7 comments + ratings) + 6 (Phase 8 polish: T-310, T-320, T-321, T-322, T-323, T-330) + 5 (Phase 8 follow-ups surfaced by T-330: T-331, T-332, T-333, T-334, T-335) + 1 (Phase 9 categories modernization: T-340) + 16 (Phase 10: T-350, T-360, T-361, T-370, T-380, T-390, T-391, T-392, T-393, T-580, T-610, T-590, T-620, T-630, T-631, T-640) + 10 (Phase 12 Shopping List: T-680, T-681, T-682, T-683, T-684, T-685, T-686, T-687, T-688, T-689) + 9 (Phase 15 CloudKit sync: T-700, T-701, T-702, T-703, T-704, T-705, T-706, T-707, T-708) + 1 (Phase 19 CloudKit sync fix: T-736) = 132 (Phase 16–18 follow-up tasks T-730..T-735 are tracked in-line within their phases)
- **Total estimate:** ~143 hours + ~17 hours (Phase 6) + ~19 hours (Phase 7) + ~13 hours (Phase 8) + ~9 hours (Phase 8 follow-ups) + ~2 hours (Phase 9) + ~13 hours (Phase 10) + ~19 hours (Phase 12) + ~29 hours (Phase 15) = ~264 hours
- **Critical path:** Cluster A → Domain (T-010, T-011) → Networking (T-058) → Recipe Detail (T-110..T-121). Roughly 6 weeks at one focused contributor; 3–4 weeks with two contributors using the parallelism tags.
- **Parallel clusters once Cluster A lands:** B-domain, B-support, B-design, B-analytics can all run simultaneously.
- **Parallel clusters once Cluster C + D land:** E-feed, E-cats, E-search, E-detail, E-saved can all run simultaneously (Saved depends on Detail finishing the offline path).
- **Phase 6 parallelism:** F6-cards, F6-icon, F6-detail, F6-onb can all run in parallel. F6-cook (T-304, T-305) is the only sequential thread inside Phase 6.
- **Phase 8 parallelism:** P8-tab (T-310) and P8-darkmode (T-330) are fully independent. The widget cluster sequences T-320 → T-321 → T-322 → T-323 internally but is independent of P8-tab and P8-darkmode externally. So three worktrees can run simultaneously: one on T-310, one on T-320 (then handing forward inside the cluster), one on T-330.
- **Phase 9 parallelism:** P9-categories (T-340) is independent of every Phase 8 task and is the only Phase 9 work item.
- **Phase 10 parallelism:** P10-search (T-350 + T-500), P10-glyph (T-380), P10-latest-widget (T-360 → T-361), P10-lockscreen (T-370), P10-widget-appearance (T-390 → T-394 / T-395), P10-saved-desc (T-400), P10-detail-cleanup (T-410), P10-livetests (T-420), and P10-categories-brown (T-430) are independent of every Phase 8 + Phase 9 task and of each other. T-500 (Search-tab polish bundle) amends T-350's AC-20.3 carve-out and is bounded entirely to `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/Sources/DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` + `SearchViewModel.swift` — independent of every other Phase 10 task source-wise. Parallel with T-510 (new-recipe surfacing, networking-only — different code path) and T-520 (color overhaul, `DODColor` token-level — different surface); if T-520 re-records any `SearchView` snapshot file T-500 also touches, T-500's semantic changes win because T-500 owns `SearchView.swift` for round 6. T-360 → T-361 sequences internally (T-361 consumes the `WidgetImageBridge` T-360 introduces). T-380 assumes T-310 already shipped. T-370 (lock-screen widget) ran on a parallel branch and conflicted with T-360 only on `Widget/DODAppWidgetBundle.swift` (each adds a registration line), resolved by the later PR rebasing on the first. T-390 is an audit-style task that touches `WidgetCard.Hero` (via a surgical `widgetAccentedRenderingMode(.fullColor)` opt-out) and adds new Tinted/Vibrant snapshot baselines — independent of T-360/T-361's image-bridge work (the audit doesn't depend on real-image render, only on the widget composition itself), and explicitly scoped to home-screen widgets so T-370's lock-screen widget code is not touched. T-400 is a single-string rewrite of `SavedRecipesWidget`'s gallery description — independent of every other Phase 10 entry (no shared files; touches only `Widget/SavedRecipesWidget.swift`'s description argument). T-410 (recipe detail cleanup) amends T-302's Phase 6 polish decision and assumes T-380 already shipped the bookmark glyph on the nav-bar Save — it touches `RecipeDetailView` + `RecipeDetailFloatingActions` + `CommentComposer` and is independent of every other Phase 10 task. T-420 (L2 nightly test for new-recipe surfacing) lives entirely inside `Packages/DODIntegrationTests/Tests/` — touches no source code, runs only in the nightly job per AC-T3, and shares no files with any other Phase 10 task. T-430 amends T-340's `CategoryListView` surface color and re-records the six `CategoryListViewSnapshotTests` baselines — bounded entirely to `Packages/DODFeatureCategories/**`, so it does not touch any widget surface, any other tab, or any DesignSystem token. T-394 (Featured widget contrast fix) and T-395 (Saved widget contrast audit verdict, clean) run on parallel branches against T-390's audit miss — both branches add their own copy of CL-46 + AC-23.7 and collide deliberately at merge time on `specs/dod-ios-app/{clarifications.md,spec.md,tasks.md,backlog.md}`; whoever lands second amends the existing CL-46 entry to fold both audits' framings. T-395 ships zero source edits (clean audit verdict) so the source-side merge is collision-free with T-394's `WidgetCard.swift` strengthening. New tasks added to Phase 10 should explicitly declare their parallelism tag and any cross-cluster dep.
- **Phase 11 parallelism:** P11-test-pyramid (T-600 through T-605) sequences internally — T-600 produces the audit doc that motivates T-601's spec amendment; T-601 locks the scheme name + four CI trigger surfaces; T-602 stands up the target + scheme; T-603 lands the five seed journeys against the target; T-604 wires the CI workflow that invokes the scheme; T-605 documents the label-gating policy for PR authors. The cluster is independent of every Phase 8 / 9 / 10 task. The branch (`feat/T-400-test-pyramid-l5-e2e`) carries all six commits and lands as a single merge commit (CONTRIBUTING.md "merge commit for multi-task clusters that need history preserved"). Follow-ups (T-610: host-side fake-dependencies switch; T-611: comments/ratings POST-path coverage against a stub; T-612: migrate de-facto-E2E methods out of `UITests/SmokeTests.swift`) are deliberately deferred — see `test-pyramid-audit.md` "Followups" section.
- **Phase 15 parallelism:** P15-cloudkit-sync sequences internally with limited parallelism after T-700 + T-701 + T-702 land. The DAG is: T-700 (spec, no deps) → T-701 (entitlements + container, depends on T-700) → T-702 (sync adapter, depends on T-701 because the adapter's `ModelConfiguration(_:groupContainer:cloudKitDatabase: .private(...))` call requires the entitlement) → {T-703 (Settings toggle), T-706 (conflict resolution) — independent of each other, both depend on T-702}; T-704 (opt-in sheet) depends on T-702 + T-703 (the `dod.cloudKitSyncEnabled` flag is shared); T-705 (offline queue + status surface) depends on T-702 + T-703 (the `CloudKitSyncStatus` enum the Settings sublabel reads); T-707 (analytics + §9 amendment) depends on T-702 + T-703 + T-704 (the dispatch sites); T-708 (L1 + L3 + L5 tests, gated by `e2e` label per CL-58) depends on T-702 + T-703 + T-704 + T-707 (the full feature surface). Two contributors can parallelize after T-702 lands by splitting T-703 (Settings UI) and T-706 (merge policy); after T-703 lands, T-704 (opt-in sheet) and T-705 (status surface) fan out. Phase 15 is independent of every Phase 8 / 9 / 10 / 11 / 12 task source-wise — owns `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/{CloudKitSyncAdapter,CloudKitSyncStatus,LastWriteWinsMergePolicy,MockCKContainer}.swift` (new), `Packages/DODAnalytics/Sources/DODAnalytics/SyncErrorCategory.swift` (new), `Packages/DODFeatureOnboarding/Sources/DODFeatureOnboarding/CloudKitOptInSheet.swift` (new), and additive extensions to `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/{CachedRecipe,RecipeStore,RecipeStore+Containers}.swift` (the `modifiedAt` field + per-write timestamp settings), `Packages/DODFeatureSettings/Sources/DODFeatureSettings/{SettingsView,SettingsViewModel,SettingsDependencies}.swift` (the iCloud Sync row), `App/{DODApp.entitlements,Info.plist,RootView,AppDependencies}.swift` (the two new entitlement keys + the `remote-notification` background mode + the opt-in sheet integration + the `CKAccountChanged` observer). T-707's constitution §9 amendment + T-700's §1 / §3 / §4 amendments are the only files outside `Packages/**` / `App/**` / `UITests/**` that this phase touches. No source-side collisions with any prior phase. The deliberately-deferred items per CL-88 (`PersistentShoppingListItem` syncing waits until T-682 lands) and CL-98 (Google Play / Android non-applicability) are NOT graduated as part of Phase 15 — they remain backlog candidates per their per-CL activation triggers.
- **Phase 12 parallelism:** P12-shopping sequences internally with two parallelizable seams. The DAG is: T-680 (spec, no deps) → {T-681 (domain + classifier), T-683 (DesignSystem primitives) — independent of each other, both depend only on T-680}; T-682 (persistence) depends on T-681 for the `Aisle` type; T-684 (view + view-model in new `DODFeatureShoppingList` package) depends on T-682 + T-683; T-685 (entry surfaces in Saved + RecipeDetail) depends on T-684; T-686 (share path) depends on T-684; T-687 (analytics + constitution §9 amendment) depends on T-684 + T-686; T-688 (L3 smoke) depends on T-684 + T-685; T-689 (L5 E2E, gated by `e2e` label per CL-58) depends on T-684 + T-685 + T-686 + T-687. Two contributors can parallelize after T-680 lands by splitting T-681 and T-683; once T-682 + T-683 are in, T-684 is the bottleneck (single owner) but then T-685 / T-686 fan out again. Phase 12 is independent of every Phase 8 / 9 / 10 / 11 task source-wise — owns `Packages/DODFeatureShoppingList/**` (new), `Packages/DODDomain/Sources/DODDomain/Aisle.swift` (new), `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/IngredientAisleClassifier.swift` (new), `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/{ShoppingListItem,SchemaV4}.swift` (new + rewrite), `Packages/DODDesignSystem/Sources/DODDesignSystem/Components/{ShoppingListRow,AisleSectionHeader}.swift` (new), and a handful of single-line extensions on existing `Packages/DODFeatureSaved/**` + `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/**` + `Packages/DODAnalytics/**` files for the entry-surface + dispatch wiring. The constitution §9 amendment in T-687 is the only file outside `Packages/**` and `UITests/**` that this phase touches; no source-side collisions with T-660 (already merged), T-670 (Spencer, in-flight on `Packages/DODFeatureSearch/**`), or T-690 (round-3 dad Voice Mode, pending graduation on `Packages/DODFeatureRecipeDetail/**` Cook Mode surface — T-685's RecipeDetail toolbar extension touches a different region of the same package, see CL-73 for the per-call-site placement decision). The deliberately-deferred items per CL-68 (location-based notifications), CL-69 (multi-list lifecycle), and CL-71 (pantry inventory) are NOT graduated as part of Phase 12 — they remain backlog candidates per their per-CL activation triggers.

- **Phase 17 parallelism:** P17-voice sequences internally — T-720 (engine, `DODFeatureRecipeDetail` → moved to `DODSupport` in T-721) → T-721 (Settings picker + quality readout + Preview, depends on the SettingsView-owning PRs #72 + #86 landing) → T-722 (download nudge + `VoiceSelector` quality helper, depends on T-721's voice section + `VoicePreviewing` catalog seam). T-721 + T-722 (DUT-5) own `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/VoiceSelection.swift` + the `DODFeatureFeed` Settings voice section (`VoicePreview.swift`, `SettingsView+Voice.swift`, `SettingsViewModel+Voice.swift`, + the existing `SettingsView`/`SettingsViewModel`/`FeedView` edits) + the single `App/TabStack.swift` previewer-wiring line — no source-side collisions with other phases. The voice fix is intentionally a picker + readout + guidance nudge, NOT an engine rewrite or cloud TTS: Apple ships only the compact ("robotic") voice tier preinstalled and exposes no public API to download the natural tiers from inside the app, so the app SHOWS the tier and guides the user to iOS Settings → Accessibility → Spoken Content → Voices (CL-123).
- **Phase 19 parallelism:** P19-cloudkit-sync-fix is a single task (T-736) on `fix/dut-6-cloudkit-sync`. Source-localized to the CloudKit/sync code paths — `Packages/DODPersistence/Sources/DODPersistence/{RecipeStore+Containers,CloudKitSyncStatus}.swift` + the `App/{AppDependencies,CloudKitSyncDiagnostics}.swift` composition root + the `DODFeatureFeed` Settings cloud-sync seam — so it is conflict-free with prior phases. A trivial rebase against the concurrent DODFeatureFeed Voice-section branch is expected (it touches a different region of `SettingsView`/`SettingsViewModel`). The owner-side schema deploy (cause #1) and the optional `aps-environment` Push provisioning (cause #3) are documented in T-736, not graduated as code tasks — they happen on the owner's Apple ID outside any PR.

### T-813 — "Your First Cookout" content spine (US-53 / AC-53.1, CL-207) — the DUT-183 keystone backbone

- **What:** The pure content model of the guided first-cookout. `GuidedCookout` value type (curated gateway dish + `Stage` gather/fire/cook/celebrate + `Step`s with Ned's coaching copy + celebration/next-step) and `GuidedCookout.firstCookout` (the Dutch oven lasagna). - **T-807 — Per-recipe charcoal converter, pure calculator core (US-50 / AC-50.1 / CL-201; DUT-128).** `feat/T-807-charcoal-recipe-converter`. Adds `CharcoalRecipeConverter` to `Packages/DODSupport/Sources/DODSupport/CharcoalRecipeConverter.swift` (pure, Foundation-only, value-type calculator: `recommend(ovenTempF:ovenDiameterInches:task:) -> CharcoalRecommendation`, `CookTask { bake, roast, simmer, fry }`, `CharcoalRecommendation { totalBriquettes; bottom; top; refreshIntervalMinutes }`) + `Packages/DODSupport/Tests/DODSupportTests/CharcoalRecipeConverterTests.swift` (10-test swift-testing L1 suite). This is the **pure core** of DUT-128 (the "manage the fire" step of the *Your First Cookout* keystone) — the inline recipe-card UI is a later slice. Same logic-core seam as `DutchOvenHeatCoach` / `IngredientAisleClassifier` / `StepTimerParser`: ships in `DODSupport` with its own tests ahead of any UI, no source-side collision with any other phase (one new source file + one new test file). **Verification:** `swift-format`, `swiftlint --strict` clean on the source, and the 10-test suite passing under `swift test --package-path Packages/DODSupport`.
- **T-808 — WPRM equipment parse (pure decode core)** [US-51 / AC-51.1 / CL-202 / DUT-156]. Decode the WP Recipe Maker recipe-card `equipment` array into a typed `[DODDomain.Equipment]`. Adds `DODDomain/RecipeEquipment.swift` (the `Equipment` value type, alongside the other recipe value types) + `WPDTO.Equipment` / `WPDTO.RecipeCard` decoders and the `RecipeCard.equipmentList` mapping in `DODNetworking/WPDTOs.swift` (mirrors the `WPDTO.Comment.toDomain()` precedent). Graceful per the WP-DTO house style: absent array → `[]`, blank-name entries skipped, lenient guarded `URL(string:)` (no force-unwrap), `HTMLSanitizer` on the name. Pure decode — no networking call added. Tested by `WPRMEquipmentParseTests` (DODNetworking): present / absent / empty / malformed-partial / HTML-name (6 cases). Independent of every other phase source-wise — owns the new `RecipeEquipment.swift` + an additive section of `WPDTOs.swift` + the new test file. The on-screen "Equipment & Tools" recipe-detail section that renders this list is a deferred later slice (UI only). Branch: `feat/T-808-equipment-parse`.

### T-845 — Recipe gallery card: description fills the title's freed space (dynamic line count), card height held constant (DUT-262, CL-239, BUGFIX)

- **What:** Tester follow-up to DUT-260's uniform cards. DUT-260 pinned BOTH the title + excerpt to `lineLimit(2, reservesSpace:)`, so a short 1-line title still showed only 2 excerpt lines and wasted the freed line. `RecipeCard.textSection` now overlays the real content on a hidden constant-height sizer: the title takes its natural 1–2 lines (`layoutPriority(1)` so it claims its full height first) and the excerpt uses `ViewThatFits` (4→3→2) to fill whatever the title leaves — 1-line title → 3 excerpt lines, 2-line title → 2 (+"…", unchanged). Card height + title↔excerpt padding unchanged; `RecipeCard.ListRow` untouched.
- **Files:** `DODDesignSystem/Components/RecipeCard.swift` (sizer + overlay + `ViewThatFits`); `RecipeCardExcerptSnapshotTests.swift` (new regression guard — short title at grid width); re-recorded only the 1-line-title L4 baselines that shifted (`SnapshotTests/test_recipeCard_full`, `SnapshotTests+AppearanceAudit/test_recipeCard_full_dark`, `RecipeCardHighlightSnapshotTests/test_recipeCard_highlightedTitle`). Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-239).
- **AC:** US-1 / AC-1.3 / DUT-262 (extends CL-233 / DUT-260). CL-239 canonical. **Est:** ~1 h. **Deps:** off main (post #282). Branch `fix/recipe-card-dynamic-excerpt`. **Verification:** SwiftLint + swift-format `--strict` clean; `xcodebuild build` clean; rendered both cases on the iPhone 17 sim — short title → 3 excerpt lines, 2-line title → 2 + "…", BOTH at the same 734px height; full DODDesignSystem L4 suite green (only the `moderationBadge` local outlier).

Added defaulted `CachedImage.byteCount` (in-place migration; pure cache → no data risk), set on every write; eviction + clearImageCache now sum it via `propertiesToFetch` (no blob fault). One-time-per-launch backfill for pre-existing rows.

### T-848 — Tab headers: uniform position + true black/white color + always-visible Saved title (DUT-263, CL-242, BUGFIX)

- **What:** Tester follow-up to DUT-261. (1) Pin EVERY tab's `DODScreenHeader` at the top so titles line up + stay put (Recipes & Saved titles moved out of their `ScrollView` → no longer scroll away). Button-less tabs (Search/Settings/Saved) reserve the same bar height via `View.dodReservesNavBarHeight()` — an empty inline nav title (no pill). A first cut used a hidden 1x1 toolbar item, but iOS 26 draws even a clear item as an empty Liquid-Glass capsule (tester: "iconless buttons / visual noise") → swapped to the empty-title approach. All titles land at the same Y (~295px on iPhone 17); real Cooking Tools + cart buttons stay. (2) `DODScreenHeader` moved from the warm `DODColor.label` (grey/cream) to a new `DODColor.labelStrong` (`#000000`/`#FFFFFF`). (3) The "Saved" title renders above every state (was `.loaded`-only), state body centered beneath.

Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-242).

### T-849 — DUT-264: Heat Coach adjusts the coal count for wind, not just time (CL-243)

- **What:** The coal-delta model only adjusted coals for ambient temperature; wind only changed the replenish cadence + showed a tip. Added `windCoalDelta` (+3-4 when windy, additive with the ambient delta) + a `windCoalNote` line in the conditions section, so wind bumps the COAL COUNT (matching the campfire "+3-4 for wind" promise / DUT-247).
- **Files:** `DutchOvenHeatCoach.swift`, `HeatCoachModel.swift`, `HeatCoachView+Sections.swift`, `DutchOvenHeatCoachTests.swift`, `HeatCoachModelTests.swift`. Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-243).
- **AC:** DUT-48 (Heat Coach) / DUT-264. CL-243 canonical. **Est:** ~1 h. **Deps:** off main. Branch `fix/DUT-264-heat-coach-wind-coals`. **Open for Ned:** confirm +3-4 magnitude + wind/cold stacking (ships additive). **Verification:** swift-format (recursive) + SwiftLint `--strict` clean; 462 DODSupport + 98 DODFeatureFeed tests pass; iOS app build green.
### T-850 — DUT-265: Categories pages off X-WP-TotalPages (parity with DUT-237) (CL-244)

- **What:** DUT-237's pagination fix never reached Categories — `CategoryRecipesViewModel` still used `if fetched.count < 20 { reachedEnd = true }`, so a short mid-list page latched pagination. Mirrored the Feed fix: `CategoriesDependencies.fetchPosts` returns `(items, totalPages)` via `client.postsPage`, and `load` sets `reachedEnd = currentPage >= totalPages`.
- **Files:** `CategoriesDependencies.swift`, `CategoryRecipesViewModel.swift`, `CategoriesTests.swift` (fake + 1 regression test). Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-244).
- **AC:** US-2 / AC-2.3 / DUT-265. CL-244 canonical. **Est:** ~45 min. **Deps:** off main. Branch `fix/DUT-265-categories-pagination`. **Verification:** swift-format (recursive) + SwiftLint `--strict` clean; 3 DODFeatureCategories tests pass; iOS app build green.

### T-851 — Feed "Cooking Tools" callout sits above the pinned title (DUT-274, CL-245, BUGFIX)

- **What:** Regression from DUT-263. Pinning the Feed title moved it out of the `ScrollView`, leaving the onboarding "Cooking Tools" callout as the first scroll item — below the title — so its upward trailing tail (meant to point at the Cooking Tools nav-bar button, DUT-200) pointed at the title instead. Moved the callout ABOVE the pinned title in the `VStack` so it sits under the button with the tail aligned; when dismissed the title returns to the top (DUT-263 alignment preserved). Offline top-inset moved to the `VStack`.

### T-852 — Every tab title pinned at top in a header row with its inline button; nav bars hidden (DUT-275, CL-246, BUGFIX)

- **What:** Per Spencer — get every tab's title up to the same high Y. Button tabs (Recipes' Cooking Tools, Saved's cart) sat ~48pt lower than button-less tabs because a nav-bar button forces a bar whose height insets the content. Fix: hide the nav bar on every tab (`.toolbar(.hidden, for: .navigationBar)`, iOS-guarded) and render the title + optional action button in ONE pinned `DODScreenHeader` row (title leading, button trailing). `DODScreenHeader` gained a generic trailing slot; title-only init unchanged. Cooking Tools + cart moved from `.toolbar` into the header row. Removed `dodReservesNavBarHeight()` (CL-242's empty-title reservation — obsolete). Callout floats as an overlay on the content below the header row. All four titles now land at ~247px (Feed/Saved/Search 247, Settings 246). Pushed detail screens keep their own nav bar (back button verified).
- **Files:** `DODDesignSystem/Components/DODScreenHeader.swift` (generic trailing slot; removed reservation helper); `DODFeatureFeed/FeedView.swift` (header row + callout overlay + hide bar); `DODFeatureSaved/SavedView.swift` (cart in header row + hide bar); `DODFeatureSearch/SearchView.swift` + `DODFeatureFeed/SettingsView.swift` (hide bar). Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-246).
- **AC:** US-1 / US-3 / US-32 / header consistency (supersedes CL-242's reservation). CL-246 canonical. **Est:** ~2 h. **Deps:** off main (post #289). Branch `fix/DUT-275-feed-title-top-callout-overlay`. **Verification:** built + ran the app on the iPhone 17 sim — every title at ~247px, Cooking Tools + cart inline beside titles, callout floats + points at the button, recipe-detail back button works; SwiftLint + swift-format `--strict` clean; feature packages compile on macOS.

### T-853 — DUT-266: SiwA token exchange can't resurrect a torn-down session (CL-247)

- **What:** The fire-and-forget auth-code→refresh-token exchange unconditionally re-saved the AppleAuthSession on completion at both surfaces (AccountViewModel.applySignIn + AppleProfileSignIn.apply). A sign-out/delete in flight let the late write resurrect the deleted session + persist a never-revoked token (re-opening the DUT-217 5.1.1(v) gap). Both sites now re-check the session is still the one being signed in before the post-exchange save.
- **Files:** `AccountViewModel.swift`, `AppleProfileSignIn.swift` (exchange extracted to `scheduleRefreshTokenExchange`), `AccountViewModelTests.swift` (2 new race tests). Spec: `clarifications.md` (CL-247).
- **AC:** US-46 / AC-46.6 / DUT-266. CL-247 canonical. **Est:** ~1.5 h. **Deps:** off main. Branch `fix/DUT-266-siwa-exchange-race`. **Verification:** swift-format (recursive) + SwiftLint `--strict` clean; 100 DODFeatureFeed + 50 DODFeatureProfile tests pass; iOS app build green.
